find_program(GLSLC glslc REQUIRED)

file(GLOB_RECURSE GLSL_FILES
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/*.vert"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/*.frag"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/*.comp"
)

foreach(GLSL ${GLSL_FILES})
    get_filename_component(FILE_NAME ${GLSL} NAME)
    set(SPIRV "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/game/shaders/${FILE_NAME}.spv")
    add_custom_command(
        OUTPUT ${SPIRV}
        COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E make_directory "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/game/shaders"
        COMMAND ${GLSLC} -I "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/common" ${GLSL} -o ${SPIRV}
        DEPENDS ${GLSL}
        COMMENT "Shader compiling ${FILE_NAME}"
    )
    list(APPEND SPIRV_FILES ${SPIRV})
endforeach()

add_custom_target(Shaders ALL DEPENDS ${SPIRV_FILES})

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/AI/AIController.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include "Core/Log.h"
#include "Components/CharacterComponent.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

class ICommand;

class AIController : public IComponent
{
public:
    AIController(PhysicsSystem& physics) : m_physics(physics) {}
    
    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override 
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_character = owner.getComponent<CharacterComponent>();
    }

    void moveTowards(glm::vec3 direction, float speed)
    {
        if (m_character)
            m_character->setMoveInput(direction * speed);
    }

    void faceDirection(glm::vec3 direction)
    {
        if (glm::length(direction) < 0.001f) return;
        float yawRad = atan2(direction.x, -direction.z);
        m_owner->transform.rotation.y = glm::degrees(yawRad);
    }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (m_character)
            m_character->syncTransform(deltaTime);

        static float logTimer = 0.0f;
        logTimer += deltaTime;
        if (logTimer >= 1.0f)
        {
            LOG("enemy pos: " << m_owner->transform.position.x << "," 
                << m_owner->transform.position.y << "," << m_owner->transform.position.z);
            logTimer = 0.0f;
        }

        updateVision();
        updateCommands(deltaTime);
    }

    void setVisionTarget(GameObject* target) { m_visionTarget = target; }
    bool canSeeTarget() const { return m_canSeeTarget; }
    glm::vec3 getLastKnowTargetPosition() const { return m_lastKnowTargetPos; }
    glm::vec3 getOwnerPosition() const { return m_owner->transform.position; }

    void setVisionRange(float range) { m_visionRange = range;}
    void setVisionFOV(float degrees) { m_visionFOVCos = cos(glm::radians(degrees * 0.5f)); }
    void setEyeOffset(glm::vec3 offset) { m_eyeOffset = offset; }

    void pushCommand(std::unique_ptr<ICommand> command);
    void clearCommands();

private:
    void updateVision();
    void updateCommands(float deltaTime);

    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;

    GameObject* m_visionTarget = nullptr;
    bool m_canSeeTarget = false;
    glm::vec3 m_lastKnowTargetPos{0.0f};

    float m_visionRange = 15.0f;
    float m_visionFOVCos = cos(glm::radians(60.0f));
    glm::vec3 m_eyeOffset{0.0f, 1.6f, 0.0f};

    CharacterComponent* m_character = nullptr;

    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<ICommand>> m_commandQueue;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/AI/ICommand.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once

class AIController;

class ICommand
{
public:
    virtual ~ICommand() = default;

    virtual bool execute(AIController& ai, float deltaTime) = 0;
};

// FILE: Engine/include/Audio/AudioSystem.h

#pragma once
#include "miniaudio.h"
#include <string>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <memory>

class AudioSystem
{
public:
    void initialize();
    void cleanup();

    bool loadSound(const std::string& name, const std::string& path);

    void playSound(const std::string& name, float volume = 1.0f);
    void playSound3D(const std::string& name, glm::vec3 position, float volume = 1.0);

    void setListenerPosition(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 forwarwd, glm::vec3 up);
    void setOcclusion(const std::string& name, float occlusionFactor);

    void updateSoundPosition(const std::string& name, glm::vec3 position);
    void stopSound(const std::string& name);

    void setListenerWorldPosition(glm::vec3 pos) { m_listenerPos = pos; }
    glm::vec3 getListenerWorldPosition() const { return m_listenerPos; }

private:
    glm::vec3 m_listenerPos{0.0f};
    ma_context m_context;
    ma_engine m_engine;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unique_ptr<ma_sound>> m_sounds;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/AudioComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Audio/AudioSystem.h"
#include <string>

class AudioComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    AudioComponent(AudioSystem& audio, PhysicsSystem& physics, std::string soundName)
        : m_audio(audio), m_physics(physics), m_soundName(std::move(soundName))
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override { m_owner = &owner; }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (!m_isPlaying) return;

        m_audio.updateSoundPosition(m_soundName, m_owner->transform.position);

        float occlusion = calculateOcclusion();
        m_audio.setOcclusion(m_soundName, occlusion);
    }

    void play(float volume = 1.0f)
    {
        m_audio.playSound3D(m_soundName, m_owner->transform.position, volume);
        m_isPlaying = true;
    }

    void stop()
    {
        m_audio.stopSound(m_soundName);
        m_isPlaying = false;
    }

private:
    float calculateOcclusion() const
    {
        glm::vec3 soundPos = m_owner->transform.position;
        glm::vec3 listenerPos = m_audio.getListenerWorldPosition();
        glm::vec3 toListener = listenerPos - soundPos;
        float distance = glm::length(toListener);

        if (distance < 0.01f) return 0.0f;

        glm::vec3 direction = toListener / distance;

        RaycastHit hit = m_physics.raycast(soundPos, direction, distance);

        return hit.hit ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    }

    AudioSystem& m_audio;
    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    std::string m_soundName;
    bool m_isPlaying = false;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/CameraComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Core/Camera.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>

class CameraComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    enum class Mode { FirstPerson, ThirdPerson };
    
    CameraComponent(Camera& targetCamera, Mode mode = Mode::FirstPerson)
        : m_camera(targetCamera), m_mode(mode)
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override { m_owner = &owner; }

    void setPitch(float degreesPitch) { m_pitch = degreesPitch; }

    void applyToCamera()
    {
        if (!m_active) return;

        glm::vec3 basePos = m_owner->transform.position;
        float yawRad = glm::radians(m_owner->transform.rotation.y);
        float pitchRad = glm::radians(m_pitch);
        
        glm::vec3 forward;
        forward.x = cos(pitchRad) * sin(yawRad);
        forward.y = sin(pitchRad);
        forward.z = -cos(pitchRad) * cos(yawRad);
        forward = glm::normalize(forward);

        glm::vec3 camPos = (m_mode == Mode::FirstPerson)
            ? basePos + m_eyeOffset
            : basePos + m_eyeOffset - forward * m_armLength;

        m_camera.setPosition(camPos);
        m_camera.lookAt(camPos + forward);
    }

    Camera& getCamera() { return m_camera; }
    glm::vec3 getEyeOffset() const { return m_eyeOffset; }

    void setActive(bool active) { m_active = active; }
    bool isActive() const { return m_active; }

    void setEyeOffset(glm::vec3 offset) { m_eyeOffset = offset; }
    void setArmLength(float length) { m_armLength = length; }
    void setMode(Mode mode) { m_mode = mode; }

private:
    Camera& m_camera;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;

    Mode m_mode;
    bool m_active = true;

    float m_pitch = 0.0f;

    glm::vec3 m_eyeOffset{0.0f, 1.6f, 0.0f};
    float m_armLength = 4.0f;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/CharacterComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include "Core/TimeSystem.h"
#include <Jolt/Physics/Character/CharacterVirtual.h>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <algorithm>

class CharacterComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    CharacterComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics, TimeSystem& time,
        float radius = 0.3f, float height = 1.8f, float mass = 80.0f)
        : m_physics(physics), m_time(time), m_radius(radius), m_height(height), m_mass(mass)
    {}

    ~CharacterComponent()
    {
        s_allCharacters.erase(std::remove(s_allCharacters.begin(), s_allCharacters.end(), this), s_allCharacters.end());
    }

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_character = m_physics.createCharacterVirtual(owner.transform.position, m_radius, m_height, m_mass);
        m_previousPosition = m_currentPosition = owner.transform.position;

        s_allCharacters.push_back(this);
    }

    void syncTransform(float deltaTime)
    {
        float alpha = glm::clamp(m_time.getFixedAlpha(), 0.0f, 1.0f);
        m_owner->transform.position = glm::mix(m_previousPosition, m_currentPosition, alpha);
    }

    void fixedUpdate(float fixedDeltaTime) override
    {
        m_previousPosition = m_currentPosition;

        JPH::Vec3 velocity(m_moveInput.x, m_verticalVelocity, m_moveInput.z);
        m_character->SetLinearVelocity(velocity);
        m_physics.updateCharacter(m_character.GetPtr(), fixedDeltaTime);

        if (isOnGround())
            m_verticalVelocity = 0.0f;
        else
            m_verticalVelocity -= 9.81f * fixedDeltaTime;

        m_currentPosition = rawPosition();

        resolveCharacterOverlap();
    
        std::vector<JPH::BodyID> overlapping = m_physics.queryOverlappingBodies(rawPosition(), m_radius);

        std::vector<JPH::BodyID> currentTriggers;
        for (auto id : overlapping)
            if (m_physics.isSensor(id))
                currentTriggers.push_back(id);

        for (auto id : currentTriggers)
        {
            bool wasInside = std::find(m_previousTriggers.begin(), m_previousTriggers.end(), id) != m_previousTriggers.end();
            if (!wasInside)
                m_physics.getContactListener().notifyEnter(id, m_bodyIdPlaceholder);
        }
        for (auto id : m_previousTriggers)
        {
            bool stillInside = std::find(currentTriggers.begin(), currentTriggers.end(), id) != currentTriggers.end();
            if (!stillInside)
                m_physics.getContactListener().notifyExit(id, m_bodyIdPlaceholder);
        }

        m_previousTriggers = currentTriggers;
    }

    glm::vec3 getVelocity() const { return glm::vec3(m_moveInput.x, m_verticalVelocity, m_moveInput.z); }

    void setMoveInput(glm::vec3 worldSpaceHorizontal) { m_moveInput = worldSpaceHorizontal; }

    void jump(float jumpSpeed = 5.0f)
    {
        if (isOnGround())
            m_verticalVelocity = jumpSpeed;
    }

    bool isOnGround() const
    {
        return m_character->GetGroundState() == JPH::CharacterVirtual::EGroundState::OnGround;
    }

    glm::vec3 getPosition() const { return m_owner->transform.position; }

private:
    glm::vec3 rawPosition() const
    {
        JPH::RVec3 pos = m_character->GetPosition();
        return glm::vec3(pos.GetX(), pos.GetY(), pos.GetZ());
    }

    void resolveCharacterOverlap()
    {
        for (CharacterComponent* other : s_allCharacters)
        {
            if (other == this) continue;

            glm::vec3 toOther = other->m_currentPosition - m_currentPosition;
            toOther.y = 0.0f;

            float distance = glm::length(toOther);
            float combinedRadius = m_radius + other->m_radius;

            if (distance < combinedRadius && distance > 0.001f)
            {
                glm::vec3 pushDir = toOther / distance;
                float overlap = combinedRadius - distance;

                m_currentPosition -= pushDir * overlap * 0.5f;
                other->m_currentPosition += pushDir * overlap * 0.5f;
            }
        }
    }

    static inline std::vector<CharacterComponent*> s_allCharacters;

    std::vector<JPH::BodyID> m_previousTriggers;
    JPH::BodyID m_bodyIdPlaceholder;

    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    TimeSystem& m_time;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    JPH::Ref<JPH::CharacterVirtual> m_character;

    float m_radius, m_height, m_mass;
    glm::vec3 m_moveInput{0.0f};
    float m_verticalVelocity = 0.0f;

    glm::vec3 m_previousPosition{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_currentPosition{0.0f};
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/InputComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Core/Input.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

class InputComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    explicit InputComponent(Input& input) : m_input(input) {}

    glm::vec2 getMoveAxis() const
    {
        glm::vec2 move(0.0f);
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::W)) move.y += 1.0f;
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::S)) move.y -= 1.0f;
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::D)) move.x += 1.0f;
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::A)) move.x -= 1.0f;

        move.x += m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::LeftX);
        move.y += -m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::LeftY);

        if (glm::length(move) > 1.0f)
            move = glm::normalize(move);

        return move;
    }

    glm::vec2 getLookDelta() const
    {
        float gamepadYaw = m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::RightX) * m_gamepadLookSpeed * 0.05;
        float gamepadPitch = -m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::RightY) * m_gamepadLookSpeed * 0.05;

        return glm::vec2(m_input.getMouseDeltaX() + gamepadYaw, m_input.getMouseDeltaY() + gamepadPitch);
    }

    bool isJumpJustPressed() const { return m_input.isActionJustPressed("Up"); }
    bool isFirePressed() const { return m_input.isActionPressed("Fire"); }
    bool isFireJustPressed() const { return m_input.isActionJustPressed("Fire"); }
    bool isInteractJustPressed() const { return m_input.isActionJustPressed("Interact"); }

    void setGamepadLookSpeed(float speed) { m_gamepadLookSpeed = speed; }

private:
    Input& m_input;
    float m_gamepadLookSpeed = 150.0f;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/RigidBodyComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#define GLM_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL

#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include <Jolt/Physics/Body/BodyID.h>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>
#include <glm/gtx/euler_angles.hpp>
#include "Scene/Mesh.h"

class RigidBodyComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    enum class Shape { Box, Sphere, Mesh, ConvexHull };
    enum class Motion { Static, Dynamic, Kinematic };

    RigidBodyComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics, Shape shape, Motion motion,
        std::shared_ptr<Mesh> collisionMesh = nullptr,
        glm::vec3 halfExtentOrRadius = glm::vec3(0.5f), float mass = 1.0f)
        : m_physics(physics), m_shape(shape), m_motion(motion)
        , m_collisionMesh(collisionMesh), m_size(halfExtentOrRadius), m_mass(mass)
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        glm::vec3 startPos = owner.transform.position;
    
        switch (m_shape)
        {
            case Shape::Box:
                switch (m_motion)
                {
                    case Motion::Static: m_bodyId = m_physics.createStaticBox(startPos, m_size); break;
                    case Motion::Kinematic: m_bodyId = m_physics.createKinematicBox(startPos, m_size); break;
                    case Motion::Dynamic: m_bodyId = m_physics.createDynamicBox(startPos, m_size, m_mass); break;
                }
                break;

            case Shape::Sphere:
                m_bodyId = (m_motion == Motion::Static)
                    ? m_physics.createStaticSphere(startPos, m_size.x)
                    : m_physics.createDynamicSphere(startPos, m_size.x, m_mass);
                break;
            
            case Shape::Mesh:
            {
                std::vector<glm::vec3> positions;
                for (const auto& v : m_collisionMesh->getCpuVertices())
                    positions.push_back(v.pos);
            
                m_bodyId = m_physics.createStaticMesh(positions, m_collisionMesh->getCpuIndices(), startPos);
                break;
            }
        
            case Shape::ConvexHull:
            {
                std::vector<glm::vec3> positions;
                for (const auto& v : m_collisionMesh->getCpuVertices())
                    positions.push_back(v.pos);
                        
                if (m_motion == Motion::Kinematic)
                    m_bodyId = m_physics.createKinematicConvexHull(positions, startPos);
                else
                    m_bodyId = m_physics.createDynamicConvexHull(positions, startPos, m_mass);
                        
                break;
            }
        
            default: break;
        }

        m_physics.registerBody(m_bodyId, &owner);
    }

    void setTransform(glm::vec3 position, glm::quat rotation)
    {
        m_physics.setBodyTransform(m_bodyId, position, rotation);
    }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (m_motion == Motion::Dynamic)
        {
            m_owner->transform.position = m_physics.getBodyPosition(m_bodyId);

            glm::quat q = m_physics.getBodyRotation(m_bodyId);
            m_owner->transform.rotation = glm::degrees(glm::eulerAngles(q));
        }
    }

    JPH::BodyID getBodyId() const { return m_bodyId; }

private:
    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    JPH::BodyID m_bodyId;

    Shape m_shape;
    Motion m_motion;
    std::shared_ptr<Mesh> m_collisionMesh;
    glm::vec3 m_size;
    float m_mass;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/SkeletalMeshComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Scene/SkeletalMesh.h"
#include "Core/Log.h"
#include "Scene/Animation.h"
#include "Vulkan/BoneMatrixBuffer.h"
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

class SkeletalMeshComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    SkeletalMeshComponent(ASH::Device* device, uint32_t frameCount)
    {
        m_boneBuffer.initialize(device, frameCount);
    }

    ~SkeletalMeshComponent()
    {
        m_boneBuffer.cleanup();
    }

    ASH::Buffer* getBoneBuffer(uint32_t frameIndex) const
    {
        return m_boneBuffer.getBuffer(frameIndex);
    }

    void setSkeletalMesh(std::shared_ptr<SkeletalMesh> mesh) { m_skeletalMesh = mesh; }
    std::shared_ptr<SkeletalMesh> getSkeletalMesh() const { return m_skeletalMesh; }

    void createShadowDescriptorSets(ASH::Device* device, ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* layout, uint32_t frameCount)
    {
        m_shadowDescriptroSets.resize(frameCount);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < frameCount; i++)
        {
            m_shadowDescriptroSets[i] = device->createDescriptorSet(layout);

            ASH::DescriptorBufferInfo boneInfo{ m_boneBuffer.getBuffer(i), 0, m_boneBuffer.getBuffer(i)->getSize() };

            ASH::DescriptorWrite write{};
            write.binding = 9;
            write.type = ASH::DescriptorType::UniformBuffer;
            write.bufferInfo = &boneInfo;

            m_shadowDescriptroSets[i]->update(&write, 1);
        }
    }

    ASH::DescriptorSet* getShadowDescriptorSet(uint32_t frameIndex) const
    {
        if (frameIndex >= m_shadowDescriptroSets.size()) return nullptr;
        return m_shadowDescriptroSets[frameIndex].get();
    }

    void play(std::shared_ptr<Animation> animation, bool loop = true)
    {
        m_currentAnimation = animation;
        m_currentTime = 0.0f;
        m_loop = loop;
        m_playing = true;
    }

    bool playAnimationByName(GameObject& owner, const std::string& name, bool loop = true)
    {
        auto it = owner.animations.find(name);
        if (it == owner.animations.end())
        {
            LOG("playAnimationByName: animation '" << name << "' not found");
            return false;
        }
        play(it->second, loop);
        return true;
    }

    void stop() { m_playing = false; }
    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override { m_owner = &owner; }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (!m_skeletalMesh || !m_skeletalMesh->getSkeleton()) return;
        if (m_playing && m_currentAnimation)
        {
            m_currentTime += deltaTime;
            if (m_currentTime > m_currentAnimation->duration)
            {
                if (m_loop)
                    m_currentTime = fmod(m_currentTime, m_currentAnimation->duration);
                else
                {
                    m_currentTime = m_currentAnimation->duration;
                    m_playing = false;
                }
            }
        }
        computePose();
    }

    void uploadBoneMatrices(uint32_t frameIndex)
    {
        BoneMatrixData data{};
        size_t count = std::min(m_boneMatrices.size(), (size_t)MAX_BONES);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; i++)
            data.bones[i] = m_boneMatrices[i];
        m_boneBuffer.update(frameIndex, data);
    }

private:
    void computePose()
    {
        const Skeleton& skeleton = *m_skeletalMesh->getSkeleton();
        size_t jointCount = skeleton.getJointCount();
        if (m_globalTransforms.size() != jointCount)
        {
            m_globalTransforms.resize(jointCount);
            m_boneMatrices.resize(jointCount, glm::mat4(1.0f));
        }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < jointCount; i++)
        {
            const Joint& joint = skeleton.getJoint((int)i);
            glm::mat4 localTransform = m_currentAnimation
                ? m_currentAnimation->sampleJointTransform(joint.name, m_currentTime)
                : joint.localBindTransform;

            if (i == 0)
            {
                static float lastLoggedTime = -1.0f;
                if (m_currentTime - lastLoggedTime > 0.3f)
                {
                    LOG("Bone 0 pose: t=" << m_currentTime
                        << " col0=(" << localTransform[0][0] << "," << localTransform[0][1] << "," << localTransform[0][2] << ")"
                        << " col1=(" << localTransform[1][0] << "," << localTransform[1][1] << "," << localTransform[1][2] << ")"
                        << " pos=(" << localTransform[3][0] << "," << localTransform[3][1] << "," << localTransform[3][2] << ")");
                    lastLoggedTime = m_currentTime;
                }
            }

            if (i == 1)
            {
                static float lastLoggedTime = -1.0f;
                if (m_currentTime - lastLoggedTime > 0.3f)
                {
                    LOG("Bone 1 pose: t=" << m_currentTime
                        << " col0=(" << localTransform[0][0] << "," << localTransform[0][1] << "," << localTransform[0][2] << ")"
                        << " pos=(" << localTransform[3][0] << "," << localTransform[3][1] << "," << localTransform[3][2] << ")");
                    lastLoggedTime = m_currentTime;
                }
            }
            
            if (joint.parentIndex >= 0)
                m_globalTransforms[i] = m_globalTransforms[joint.parentIndex] * localTransform;
            else
                m_globalTransforms[i] = localTransform;
            m_boneMatrices[i] = m_globalTransforms[i] * joint.inverseBindMatrix;
        }
    }

    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    std::shared_ptr<SkeletalMesh> m_skeletalMesh;
    std::shared_ptr<Animation> m_currentAnimation;
    float m_currentTime = 0.0f;
    bool m_loop = true;
    bool m_playing = false;
    std::vector<glm::mat4> m_globalTransforms;
    std::vector<glm::mat4> m_boneMatrices;
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<ASH::DescriptorSet>> m_shadowDescriptroSets;
    BoneMatrixBuffer m_boneBuffer;
};


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/SkeletalVoxelProxyComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Vulkan/Renderer.h"
#include "Vulkan/DynamicVoxelProxy.h"
#include "Components/SkeletalMeshComponent.h"
#include <memory>

class SkeletalVoxelProxyComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    SkeletalVoxelProxyComponent(Renderer& renderer, SkeletalMesh* mesh, Material* material, ASH::Buffer* boneBuffer, uint32_t rebakeInterval = 6)
        : m_renderer(renderer), m_mesh(mesh), m_material(material), m_boneBuffer(boneBuffer), m_rebakeInterval(rebakeInterval)
    {
        m_proxy = renderer.createSkeletalVoxelProxy(mesh, material, boneBuffer);
    }

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override { m_owner = &owner; }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (!m_proxy) return;

        m_frameCounter++;
        if (m_frameCounter >= m_rebakeInterval)
        {
            m_renderer.rebakeSkeletalVoxelProxy(*m_proxy, m_mesh, m_material, m_boneBuffer);
            m_frameCounter = 0;
        }

        m_renderer.stampVoxelProxy(*m_proxy, m_owner->transform.position);
    }

private:
    Renderer& m_renderer;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    SkeletalMesh* m_mesh;
    Material* m_material;
    ASH::Buffer* m_boneBuffer;
    std::unique_ptr<DynamicVoxelProxy> m_proxy;
    uint32_t m_rebakeInterval;
    uint32_t m_frameCounter = 0;
};

class TransformComponent : public IComponent

    void setPosition(glm::vec3 pos) { m_owner->transform.position = pos; }
    void setRotationEuler(glm::vec3 eulerDegrees) { m_owner->transform.rotation = eulerDegrees; }
    void setScale(glm::vec3 scale) { m_owner->transform.scale = scale; }

    glm::vec3 getPosition() const { return m_owner->transform.position; }
    glm::vec3 getRotationEuler() const { return m_owner->transform.rotation; }
    glm::mat4 getWorldMatrix() const { return m_owner->transform.getMatrix(); }

private:
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Components/TriggerComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <functional>

class TriggerComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    TriggerComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics, glm::vec3 halfExtent)
        : m_physics(physics), m_halfExtent(halfExtent)
    {}

    ~TriggerComponent()
    {
        m_physics.getContactListener().unregisterTrigger(m_bodyId);
    }

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_bodyId = m_physics.createTriggerBox(owner.transform.position, m_halfExtent);
        m_physics.registerBody(m_bodyId, &owner);
        m_physics.getContactListener().registerTrigger(m_bodyId, this);
    }

    void fireEnter(JPH::BodyID other) { if (onTriggerEnter) onTriggerEnter(other); }
    void fireExit(JPH::BodyID other) { if (onTriggerExit) onTriggerExit(other); }

    std::function<void(JPH::BodyID)> onTriggerEnter;
    std::function<void(JPH::BodyID)> onTriggerExit;

private:
    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    JPH::BodyID m_bodyId;
    glm::vec3 m_halfExtent;
};

    void setAspect(float aspect) { m_aspect = aspect; }

    void setRoll(float degreesRoll) { m_roll = degreesRoll; }

    void lookAt(glm::vec3 target);

    glm::mat4 getViewMatrix() const;
    glm::vec3 getFront() const { return m_front; }
    glm::vec3 getRight() const { return m_right; }
    glm::vec3 getPosition() const { return m_position; }
    glm::vec3 getUp() const  { return m_up; }
    glm::mat4 getProjectionMatrix() const;

private:
    void updateVectors();

    glm::vec3 m_position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_front{0.0f, 0.0f, -1.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_up{0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_right{1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_worldUp{0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f};

    float m_yaw = -90.0f;
    float m_pitch = 0.0f;

    float m_moveSpeed = 3.0f;
    float m_mouseSensitivity = 0.1f;

    float m_aspect = 16.0f / 9.0f;
    float m_fov = 45.0f;
    float m_nearPlane = 0.1f;
    float m_farPlane = 100.0f;
    float m_roll = 0.0f;
};

{

    void onFramebufferResize();

    void fixedUpdate(float fixedDt);

    void setAntialiasing(AAMethod method) { m_renderer.setAAMethod(method); }

    void setCameraControlMode(bool useController) { }

    std::shared_ptr<ScreenSpaceDecal> addScreenSpaceDecal(const std::string& texturePath,
        glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 rotationEuler, glm::vec3 size,
        const std::string& opacityPath = "", const std::string& normalPath = "", const std::string& mrPath = "");

    std::shared_ptr<Material> createDefaultMaterial();        

private:

    void createMaterialDescriptorSet(std::shared_ptr<Material> material);

    GLFWwindow* m_window = nullptr;

    AudioSystem m_audio;
    TimeSystem m_time;
    Renderer m_renderer;

    Camera m_camera;

    LightData m_lights{};

    PhysicsSystem m_physics;
    NavMesh m_navMesh;

};

#pragma once

struct GltfLight
{
    int type = 0;
    glm::vec3 color{1.0f};
    float intensity = 1.0f;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 direction{0.0f, -1.0f, 0.0f};
};

struct GltfNavVolume
{
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 size{1.0f};
    float cellSize = 0.75f;
};

struct GltfImage
{
    std::vector<unsigned char> pixels;
    int width = 0;
    int height = 0;
};

struct GltfDecal
{
    std::string texturePath;
    std::string opacityPath;
    std::string normalPath;
    std::string mrPath;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 size{1.0f};
    float metallic = 0.0f;
    float roughness = 0.6f;
};

struct GltfMaterial
{
    int albedoImage = -1;
    int normalImage = -1;
    int mrImage = -1;
    int aoImage = -1;
    float metallicFactor = 1.0f;
    float roughnessFactor = 1.0f;
    glm::vec4 baseColorFactor{1.0f};
    int lightmapImage = -1;
    bool hasLightmap = false;
};

struct GltfCollider
{
    std::string shape;
    std::string motion;
    float mass = 1.0f;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 size{1.0f};
};

struct GltfEntity
{
    std::string name;
    std::string entityType;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 scale{1.0f};
};

class GltfModel
{
public:
    void load(const std::string& path);

    const std::vector<Vertex>& getVertices() const { return m_vertices; }
    const std::vector<uint32_t>& getIndices() const { return m_indices; }
    const std::vector<Submesh>& getSubmesh() const { return m_submesh; }
    const std::vector<GltfImage>& getImage() const { return m_images; }
    const std::vector<GltfMaterial>& getMaterials() const { return m_materials; } 
    const std::vector<GltfLight>& getLights() const { return m_lights; }
    const std::vector<GltfDecal>& getDecals() const { return m_decals; }
    const std::vector<GltfCollider>& getColliders() const { return m_colliders; }
    const std::vector<GltfEntity>& getEntities() const { return m_entities; }
    const std::vector<GltfNavVolume>& getNavVolumes() const { return m_navVolumes; }

private:
    void computeTangents();

    std::vector<Vertex> m_vertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> m_indices;
    std::vector<Submesh> m_submesh;
    std::vector<GltfImage> m_images;
    std::vector<GltfMaterial> m_materials;
    std::vector<GltfLight> m_lights;
    std::vector<GltfDecal> m_decals;
    std::vector<GltfCollider> m_colliders;
    std::vector<GltfEntity> m_entities;  
    std::vector<GltfNavVolume> m_navVolumes;
    bool m_hasTangents = false;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Core/Input.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include "Core/IGamepad.h"
#include <string>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

enum class Key
{
    W, A, S, D, E, Q,
    Space, LeftShift, LeftControl,
    Escape, Tab, Enter,
    Num1, Num2, Num3, Num4, Num5,
    F1, F2, F3, F4, F5,
    GraveAccent,
    Unknown
};

enum class MouseButton
{
    Left, Right, Middle,
    Unknown
};

struct InputBinding
{
    enum class Type { Keyboard, Mouse, Gamepad } type = Type::Keyboard;
    Key key = Key::Unknown;
    MouseButton mouseButton = MouseButton::Unknown;
    GamepadButton gamepadButton = GamepadButton::Unknown;

    static InputBinding fromKey(Key k) { return { Type::Keyboard, k, MouseButton::Unknown }; }
    static InputBinding fromMouse(MouseButton b) { return { Type::Mouse, Key::Unknown, b }; }
    static InputBinding fromGamepad(GamepadButton b) { return { Type::Gamepad, Key::Unknown, MouseButton::Unknown, b }; }
};

class Input
{
public:
    void initialize(GLFWwindow* window);
    void update();

    bool isKeyPressed(Key key) const;
    bool isKeyJustPressed(Key key) const;
    bool isKeyJustReleased(Key key) const;

    bool isMouseButtonPressed(MouseButton button) const;
    bool isMouseButtonJustPressed(MouseButton button) const;

    float getMouseDeltaX() const { return m_mouseDeltaX; }
    float getMouseDeltaY() const { return m_mouseDeltaY; }
    float getScrollDelta() const { return m_scrollDelta; }

    void setCursorLocked(bool locked);
    bool isCursorLocked() const { return m_cursorLocked; }

    void bindAction(const std::string& action, InputBinding binding);
    bool isActionPressed(const std::string& action) const;
    bool isActionJustPressed(const std::string& action) const;

    IGamepad& gamepad() { return *m_gamepad; }
    const IGamepad& gamepad() const { return *m_gamepad; }

private:
    int toGLFWKey(Key key) const;
    int toGLFWMouseButton(MouseButton button) const;
    bool rawKeyPressed(Key key) const;
    bool rawMousePressed(MouseButton button) const;

    static void scrollCallback(GLFWwindow* window, double xoffset, double yoffset);

    GLFWwindow* m_window = nullptr;

    double m_lastMouseX = 0.0;
    double m_lastMouseY = 0.0;
    float m_mouseDeltaX = 0.0f;
    float m_mouseDeltaY = 0.0f;
    bool m_firstMouse = true;
    bool m_cursorLocked = true;

    static inline float s_scrollAccumulator = 0.0f;
    float m_scrollDelta = 0.0f;

    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_currKeyState;
    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_prevKeyState;
    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_currMouseState;
    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_prevMouseState;

    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<InputBinding>> m_actionBindings;

    std::unique_ptr<IGamepad> m_gamepad;
};

#pragma once
#include "Vulkan/Vertex.h"
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

struct LevelSubmesh
{
    int32_t materialIndex;
    uint32_t indexOffset;
    uint32_t indexCount;
    glm::vec3 boundsMin;
    glm::vec3 boundsMax;
};

struct LevelMesh
{
    std::vector<Vertex> vertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> indices;
    std::vector<LevelSubmesh> submeshes;
};

struct LevelMaterial
{
    std::string albedoPath;
    std::string normalPath;
    std::string metallicRoughnessPath;
    std::string aoPath;
};

struct LevelObject
{
    std::string name;
    int32_t meshIndex = -1;
    glm::mat4 transform{1.0f};
    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::string> customProperties;
};

struct LevelLight
{
    int32_t type;
    glm::vec3 position;
    glm::vec3 direction;
    glm::vec3 color;
    float intensity;
};

class LevelLoader
{
public:
    void load(const std::string& path);

    const std::vector<LevelMesh>& getMeshes() const { return m_meshes; }
    const std::vector<LevelMaterial>& getMaterials() const { return m_materials; }
    const std::vector<LevelLight>& getLights() const { return m_lights; }
    const std::vector<LevelObject>& getObjects() const { return m_objects; }

private:
    void parseMeshes(const uint8_t* data);
    void parseMaterials(const uint8_t* data);
    void parseObjects(const uint8_t* data);
    void parseLights(const uint8_t* data);

    std::vector<LevelMesh> m_meshes;
    std::vector<LevelMaterial> m_materials;
    std::vector<LevelObject> m_objects;
    std::vector<LevelLight> m_lights;
};

#pragma once
#include "iostream"
#include <sstream>
#include "UI/DebugConsole.h"

#ifdef NDEBUG
    #define LOG(msg) \
        do { \
            std::ostringstream oss; \
            oss << msg; \
            DebugConsole::instance().log(oss.str()); \
        } while(0)
#else
    #define LOG(msg) \
        do { \
            std::ostringstream oss; \
            oss << msg; \
            std::cout << "[ELE] " << oss.str() << std::endl; \
            DebugConsole::instance().log(oss.str()); \
        } while(0)
#endif

struct ModelSubmesh
{
    int32_t materialIndex;
    uint32_t indexOffset;
    uint32_t indexCount;
    glm::vec3 boundsMin;
    glm::vec3 boundsMax;
};

struct ModelMaterial
{
    std::string albedoPath;
    std::string normalPath;
    std::string metallicRoughnessPath;
    std::string aoPath;
};

struct ModelBone
{
    std::string name;
    int32_t parentIndex;
    glm::mat4 inverseBindMatrix;
    glm::mat4 localBindMatrix;
};

class ModelLoader
{
public:
    void load(const std::string& path);

    const std::vector<Vertex>& getVertices() const { return m_vertices; }
    const std::vector<uint32_t>& getIndices() const { return m_indices; }
    const std::vector<ModelSubmesh>& getSubmeshes() const { return m_submeshes; }
    const std::vector<ModelMaterial>& getMaterials() const { return m_materials; }
    const std::vector<ModelBone>& getBones() const { return m_bones; }
    bool hasSkeleton() const { return  !m_bones.empty(); }
    Skeleton buildSkeleton() const;
    const std::vector<SkinnedVertex>& getSkinnedVertices() const { return m_skinnedVertices; }

private:
    std::vector<Vertex> m_vertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> m_indices;
    std::vector<ModelSubmesh> m_submeshes;
    std::vector<ModelMaterial> m_materials;
    std::vector<ModelBone> m_bones;
    std::vector<SkinnedVertex> m_skinnedVertices;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Core/PhysicsLayers.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include <Jolt/Jolt.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/ObjectLayer.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/BroadPhase/BroadPhaseLayer.h>

namespace Layers
{
    static constexpr JPH::ObjectLayer NON_MOVING = 0;
    static constexpr JPH::ObjectLayer MOVING = 1;
    static constexpr JPH::ObjectLayer NUM_LAYERS = 2;
}

namespace BroadPhaseLayers
{
    static constexpr JPH::BroadPhaseLayer NON_MOVING(0);
    static constexpr JPH::BroadPhaseLayer MOVING(1);
    static constexpr JPH::uint NUM_LAYERS = 2;
}

class ObjectLayerPairFilterImpl : public JPH::ObjectLayerPairFilter
{
public:
    bool ShouldCollide(JPH::ObjectLayer inObject1, JPH::ObjectLayer inObject2) const override
    {
        switch (inObject1)
        {
            case Layers::NON_MOVING:
                return inObject2 == Layers::MOVING;
            case Layers::MOVING:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
};

class BPLayerInterfaceImpl : public JPH::BroadPhaseLayerInterface
{
public:
    BPLayerInterfaceImpl()
    {
        m_objectToBroadPhase[Layers::NON_MOVING] = BroadPhaseLayers::NON_MOVING;
        m_objectToBroadPhase[Layers::MOVING] = BroadPhaseLayers::MOVING;
    }

    JPH::uint GetNumBroadPhaseLayers() const override { return BroadPhaseLayers::NUM_LAYERS; }

    JPH::BroadPhaseLayer GetBroadPhaseLayer(JPH::ObjectLayer inLayer) const override
    {
        return m_objectToBroadPhase[inLayer];
    }

    #if defined(JPH_EXTERNAL_PROFILE) || defined(JPH_PROFILE_ENABLED)
        virtual const char* GetBroadPhaseLayerName(JPH::BroadPhaseLayer inLayer) const override
        {
            switch ((JPH::BroadPhaseLayer::Type)inLayer)
            {
                case (JPH::BroadPhaseLayer::Type)BroadPhaseLayers::NON_MOVING: return "NON_MOVING";
                case (JPH::BroadPhaseLayer::Type)BroadPhaseLayers::MOVING:     return "MOVING";
                default: return "INVALID";
            }
        }
    #endif

private:
    JPH::BroadPhaseLayer m_objectToBroadPhase[Layers::NUM_LAYERS];
};

class ObjectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilterImpl : public JPH::ObjectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilter
{
public:
    bool ShouldCollide(JPH::ObjectLayer inLayer1, JPH::BroadPhaseLayer inLayer2) const override
    {
        switch (inLayer1)
        {
            case Layers::NON_MOVING:
                return inLayer2 == BroadPhaseLayers::MOVING;
            case Layers::MOVING:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
};

        const std::string& metallic, const std::string& roughness);

    void registerMesh(std::shared_ptr<Mesh> m) { m_meshes.push_back(m); }
    void registerMaterial(std::shared_ptr<Material> m) { m_materials.push_back(m); }

    void cleanup();

    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Material>> m_materials;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Core/TimeSystem.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once

class TimeSystem
{
public:
    void update(float rawDeltaTime);

    float deltaTime() const { return m_paused ? 0.0f : m_rawDeltaTime * m_timeScale; }
    float unscaledDeltaTime() const { return m_rawDeltaTime; }

    float fixedDeltaTime() const { return m_fixedDeltaTime; }
    void setFixedDeltaTime(float dt) { m_fixedDeltaTime = dt; }

    void setTimeScale(float scale) { m_timeScale = scale; }
    float getTimeScale() const { return m_timeScale; }

    float getFixedAlpha() const { return m_accumulator / m_fixedDeltaTime; }

    void pause() { m_paused = true; }
    void resume() { m_paused = false; }
    bool isPaused() const { return m_paused; }

    double totalTime() const { return m_totalTime; }

    int consumeFixedSteps();

private:
    float m_rawDeltaTime = 0.0f;
    float m_timeScale = 1.0f;
    float m_fixedDeltaTime = 1.0f / 60.0f;
    float m_accumulator = 0.0f;
    double m_totalTime = 0.0;
    bool m_paused = false;

    static constexpr int MAX_FIXED_STEPS_PER_FRAME = 5;
};

#pragma once
#include <Jolt/Jolt.h>
#include <Jolt/Core/JobSystemThreadPool.h>
#include <Jolt/Core/TempAllocator.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/PhysicsSystem.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Character/CharacterVirtual.h>
#include "Core/PhysicsLayers.h"
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp> 
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <memory>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/ContactListener.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/Shape/SphereShape.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/CollideShape.h>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <functional>

struct RaycastHit
{
    bool hit = false;
    JPH::BodyID bodyId;
    glm::vec3 point{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 normal{0.8f, 1.0f, 0.0f};
    float distance = 0.0f;
};

class TriggerComponent;
class GameObject;

class TriggerContactListener : public JPH::ContactListener
{
public:
    void registerTrigger(JPH::BodyID id, TriggerComponent* trigger) { m_triggers[id] = trigger; }
    void unregisterTrigger(JPH::BodyID id) { m_triggers.erase(id); }

    void OnContactAdded(const JPH::Body& body1, const JPH::Body& body2,
        const JPH::ContactManifold&, JPH::ContactSettings&) override
    {
        dispatch(body1.GetID(), body2.GetID(), true);
    }

    void notifyEnter(JPH::BodyID trigger, JPH::BodyID other) { dispatch(trigger, other, true); }
    void notifyExit(JPH::BodyID trigger, JPH::BodyID other) { dispatch(trigger, other, false); }

    void OnContactRemoved(const JPH::SubShapeIDPair& subShapePair) override
    {
        dispatch(subShapePair.GetBody1ID(), subShapePair.GetBody2ID(), false);
    }

private:
    void dispatch(JPH::BodyID a, JPH::BodyID b, bool entered);

    std::unordered_map<JPH::BodyID, TriggerComponent*> m_triggers;
};

class PhysicsSystem
{
public:
    void initialize();
    void update(float fixedDeltaTime);
    void cleanup();

    JPH::BodyInterface& bodyInterface() { return m_physicsSystem->GetBodyInterface(); }

    JPH::BodyID createStaticBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent);
    JPH::BodyID createDynamicBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent, float mass);

    JPH::BodyID createStaticSphere(glm::vec3 position, float radius);
    JPH::BodyID createDynamicSphere(glm::vec3 position, float radius, float mass);

    JPH::BodyID createStaticMesh(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& positions, const std::vector<uint32_t>& indices, glm::vec3 worldPosition);
    JPH::BodyID createDynamicConvexHull(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& positions, glm::vec3 worldPosition, float mass);

    JPH::BodyID createKinematicBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent);

    JPH::BodyID createKinematicConvexHull(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& positions, glm::vec3 worldPosition);

    JPH::BodyID createTriggerBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent);

    JPH::Ref<JPH::CharacterVirtual> createCharacterVirtual(glm::vec3 position, float radius, float height, float mass = 80.0f);
    void updateCharacter(JPH::CharacterVirtual* character, float deltaTime);

    glm::vec3 getBodyPosition(JPH::BodyID id) const;
    glm::quat getBodyRotation(JPH::BodyID id) const;
    TriggerContactListener& getContactListener() { return m_contactListener; }

    void registerBody(JPH::BodyID id, GameObject* owner) { m_bodyOwners[id.GetIndexAndSequenceNumber()] = owner; }
    GameObject* getOwner(JPH::BodyID id) const
    {
        auto it = m_bodyOwners.find(id.GetIndexAndSequenceNumber());
        return (it != m_bodyOwners.end()) ? it->second : nullptr;
    }

    void setBodyTransform(JPH::BodyID id, glm::vec3 position, glm::quat rotation);

    RaycastHit raycast(glm::vec3 origin, glm::vec3 direction, float maxDistance);

    std::vector<JPH::BodyID> queryOverlappingBodies(glm::vec3 position, float radius);
    bool isSensor(JPH::BodyID id) const;

private:
    std::unique_ptr<JPH::TempAllocatorImpl> m_tempAllocator;
    std::unique_ptr<JPH::JobSystemThreadPool> m_jobSystem;
    std::unique_ptr<JPH::PhysicsSystem> m_physicsSystem;

    BPLayerInterfaceImpl m_broadPhaseLayerInterface;
    ObjectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilterImpl m_objectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilter;
    ObjectLayerPairFilterImpl m_objectLayerPairFilter;

    TriggerContactListener m_contactListener;

    std::unordered_map<JPH::uint32, GameObject*> m_bodyOwners;

    static constexpr JPH::uint MAX_BODIES = 1024;
    static constexpr JPH::uint NUM_BODY_MUTEXES = 0;
    static constexpr JPH::uint MAX_BODY_PAIRS = 1024;
    static constexpr JPH::uint MAX_CONTACT_CONSTRAINTS = 1024;
};

#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>

struct PositionKey { float time; glm::vec3 value; };
struct RotationKey { float time; glm::quat value; };
struct ScaleKey { float time; glm::vec3 value;};

struct JointAnimationChannel
{
    std::string jointName;
    std::vector<PositionKey> poisitonKeys;
    std::vector<RotationKey> rotationKeys;
    std::vector<ScaleKey> scaleKeys;
};

class Animation
{
public:
    std::string name;
    float duration = 0.0f;
    std::vector<JointAnimationChannel> channels;

    glm::mat4 sampleJointTransform(const std::string& jointName, float time) const;
    
private:
    static glm::vec3 interpolatePosition(const std::vector<PositionKey>& keys, float time);
    static glm::quat interpolateRotation(const std::vector<RotationKey>& keys, float time);
    static glm::vec3 interpolateScale(const std::vector<ScaleKey>& keys, float time);
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Scene/GameObject.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/Transform.h"
#include "Scene/Mesh.h"
#include "Scene/Material.h"
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include <memory>
#include <vector>
#include <typeindex>
#include "Scene/Animation.h"
#include <unordered_map>

class GameObject
{
public:
    std::string name;
    std::string entityType;
    Transform transform;
    std::shared_ptr<Mesh> mesh;
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Material>> materials;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::shared_ptr<Animation>> animations;
    bool isDecal = false;
    bool isDynamic = false;

    void setMaterial(std::shared_ptr<Material> mat) { materials = { mat }; }
    std::shared_ptr<Material> getMaterial(int index) const
    {
        if (materials.empty()) return nullptr;
        if (index < 0 || index >= (int)materials.size()) return materials[0];
        return materials[index];
    }

    template<typename T, typename... Args>
    T& addComponent(Args&&... args)
    {
        auto comp = std::make_unique<T>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
        T& ref = *comp;
        comp->onAttach(*this);
        m_components[std::type_index(typeid(T))] = std::move(comp);
        return ref;
    }

    template<typename T>
    T* getComponent()
    {
        auto it = m_components.find(std::type_index(typeid(T)));
        if (it == m_components.end()) return nullptr;
        return static_cast<T*>(it->second.get());
    }

    template<typename T>
    bool hasComponent() const
    {
        return m_components.find(std::type_index(typeid(T))) != m_components.end();
    }

    template<typename T>
    void removeComponent()
    {
        m_components.erase(std::type_index(typeid(T)));
    }

    void updateComponents(float deltaTime)
    {
        for (auto& [type, comp] : m_components)
            comp->update(deltaTime);
    }

    void fixedUpdateComponents(float fixedDeltaTime)
    {
        for (auto& [type, comp] : m_components)
            comp->fixedUpdate(fixedDeltaTime);
    }

private:
    std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::unique_ptr<IComponent>> m_components;

};

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include "Vulkan/Texture.h"
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

class Material
{
public:
    void load(ASH::Device* device,
        const std::string& albedoPath,
        const std::string& normalPath,
        const std::string& metallicPath,
        const std::string& roughnessPath);

    void loadFromPixels(ASH::Device* device,
        const unsigned char* albedoPx, int aw, int ah,
        const unsigned char* normalPx, int nw, int nh,
        const unsigned char* mrPx, int mw, int mh,
        const unsigned char* aoPx, int ow, int oh,
        const unsigned char* lightmapPx, int lw, int lh, bool hasLightmap);

    ASH::Texture* getAlbedoTexture() const { return m_albedo.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getAlbedoSampler() const { return m_albedo.getSampler(); }
    ASH::Texture* getNormalTexture() const { return m_normal.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getNormalSampler() const { return m_normal.getSampler(); }
    ASH::Texture* getMetallicRoughnessTexture() const { return m_metallicRoughness.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getMetallicRoughnessSampler() const { return m_metallicRoughness.getSampler(); }
    ASH::Texture* getAoTexture() const { return m_ao.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getAoSampler() const { return m_ao.getSampler(); }
    ASH::Texture* getLightmapTexture() const { return m_lightmap.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getLightmapSampler() const { return m_lightmap.getSampler(); }
    bool hasLightmap() const { return m_hasLightmap; }

    void createDescriptorSet(ASH::Device* device, ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* layout,
        const std::vector<ASH::Buffer*>& uboBuffers,
        const std::vector<ASH::Buffer*>& lightBuffers,
        ASH::Texture* shadowTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowSampler,
        ASH::Texture* shadowAtlasTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowAtlasSampler,
        ASH::Texture* irradianceTexture, ASH::Sampler* irradianceSampler,
        ASH::Texture* voxelTexture, ASH::Sampler* voxelSampler, ASH::Buffer* voxelGridBuffer,
        ASH::Buffer* voxelGridInfoBuffer,
        uint32_t frameCount);


    void createSkinnedDescriptorSet(ASH::Device* device, ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* layout,
        const std::vector<ASH::Buffer*>& uboBuffers,
        const std::vector<ASH::Buffer*>& lightBuffers,
        ASH::Texture* shadowTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowSampler,
        ASH::Texture* shadowAtlasTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowAtlasSampler,
        const std::vector<ASH::Buffer*>& boneBuffers,
        ASH::Texture* irradianceTexture, ASH::Sampler* irradianceSampler,
        uint32_t frameCount);

    ASH::DescriptorSet* getDescriptorSet(uint32_t frame) const { return m_descriptorSets[frame].get(); }
    void cleanup();

private:
    Texture m_albedo;
    Texture m_normal;
    Texture m_metallicRoughness;
    Texture m_ao;
    Texture m_lightmap;
    bool m_hasLightmap = false;
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<ASH::DescriptorSet>> m_descriptorSets;
};

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include "Vulkan/VertexBuffer.h"
#include "Vulkan/IndexBuffer.h"
#include "Scene/Submesh.h"
#include <string>
#include <vector>

class Mesh
{
public:
    void load(ASH::Device* device, const std::string& path, bool keepCpuData = false);
    void loadFromData(ASH::Device* device,
        const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
        const std::vector<uint32_t>& indices,
        const std::vector<Submesh>& submeshes,
        bool keepCpuData = false);

    void cleanup();
    void bind(ASH::CommandBuffer* cmd) const;
    void drawSubmesh(ASH::CommandBuffer* cmd, const Submesh& sm) const;
    const std::vector<Submesh>& getSubmeshes() const { return m_submeshes; }
    bool hasCpuData() const { return !m_cpuVertices.empty(); }
    const std::vector<Vertex>& getCpuVertices() const { return m_cpuVertices; }
    const std::vector<uint32_t>& getCpuIndices() const { return m_cpuIndices; }
    glm::vec3 getLocalBoundCentre() const { return m_boundsCenter; }
    float getLocalBoundsRadius() const { return m_boundRadius; }
    glm::vec3 getLocalBoundsMin() const { return m_boundsMin; }
    glm::vec3 getLocalBoundsMax() const { return m_boundsMax; }

private:
    glm::vec3 m_boundsCenter{0.0f};
    float m_boundRadius = 0.0f;
    glm::vec3 m_boundsMin{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_boundsMax{0.0f};
    void computeBounds(const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices);
    VertexBuffer m_vertexBuffer;
    IndexBuffer  m_indexBuffer;
    std::vector<Submesh> m_submeshes;
    std::vector<Vertex> m_cpuVertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> m_cpuIndices;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Scene/Scene.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

class Scene
{
public:
    GameObject& addObject()
    {
        m_pendingObjects.push_back(std::make_shared<GameObject>());
        return *m_pendingObjects.back();
    }

    void removeObject(const std::shared_ptr<GameObject>& obj)
    {
        m_objects.erase(std::remove(m_objects.begin(), m_objects.end(), obj), m_objects.end());
        m_version++;   // ДОБАВИТЬ — состав объектов изменился
    }

    void flush() { flushPenging(); }

    void clear()
    {
        m_objects.clear();
        m_pendingObjects.clear();
        m_version++;   // ДОБАВИТЬ
    }

    const std::vector<std::shared_ptr<GameObject>>& getObjects() const { return m_objects; }

    // ДОБАВИТЬ — версия увеличивается при каждом структурном изменении списка объектов
    uint32_t getVersion() const { return m_version; }

    GameObject* findByName(const std::string& name) const
    {
        for (const auto& obj : m_objects)
            if (obj->name == name)
                return obj.get();
        return nullptr;
    }

    void updateComponents(float deltaTime)
    {
        flushPenging();
        for (auto& obj : m_objects)
            obj->updateComponents(deltaTime);
    }

    void fixedUpdateComponents(float fixedDeltaTime)
    {
        flushPenging();
        for (auto& obj : m_objects)
            obj->fixedUpdateComponents(fixedDeltaTime);
        flushPenging();
    }

    std::vector<GameObject*> findAllByType(const std::string& type) const
    {
        std::vector<GameObject*> result;
        for (const auto& obj : m_objects)
            if (obj->entityType == type)
                result.push_back(obj.get());
        return result;
    }

private:
    void flushPenging()
    {
        if (m_pendingObjects.empty()) return;
        for (auto& obj : m_pendingObjects)
            m_objects.push_back(std::move(obj));
        m_pendingObjects.clear();
        m_version++;
    }

    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<GameObject>> m_objects;
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<GameObject>> m_pendingObjects;
    uint32_t m_version = 0;
};

#pragma once

    Texture m_opacityTexture;

    Texture m_mrTexture;

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Scene/Skeleton.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>

struct Joint
{
    std::string name;
    int parentIndex = -1;
    glm::mat4 inverseBindMatrix{1.0f};
    glm::mat4 localBindTransform{1.0f};
};

class Skeleton
{
public:
    int addJoint(const Joint& joint)
    {
        int index = (int)m_joints.size();
        m_joints.push_back(joint);
        m_nameToIndex[joint.name] = index;
        return index;
    }

    void setJointParent(int jointIndex, int parentIndex)
    {
        if (jointIndex >= 0 && jointIndex < (int)m_joints.size())
            m_joints[jointIndex].parentIndex = parentIndex;
    }

    int getJointIndex(const std::string& name) const
    {
        auto it = m_nameToIndex.find(name);
        return (it != m_nameToIndex.end()) ? it->second : -1;
    }

    const Joint& getJoint(int index) const { return m_joints[index]; }
    size_t getJointCount() const { return m_joints.size(); }
    const std::vector<Joint>& getJoints() const { return m_joints; }

private:
    std::vector<Joint> m_joints;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> m_nameToIndex;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Scene/Transform.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>

struct Transform
{
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotation{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 scale{1.0f};

    glm::mat4 getMatrix() const 
    {
        glm::mat4 m = glm::mat4(1.0f);

        m = glm::translate(m, position);

        m = glm::rotate(m, glm::radians(rotation.y), glm::vec3(0, 1, 0));
        m = glm::rotate(m, glm::radians(rotation.x), glm::vec3(1, 0, 0));
        m = glm::rotate(m, glm::radians(rotation.z), glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));

        m = glm::scale(m, scale);

        return m;
    }

    glm::mat4 getNormalMatrix() const
    {
        return glm::transpose(glm::inverse(getMatrix()));
    }
};


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/UI/DebugConsole.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <deque>
#include <mutex>

class DebugConsole 
{
public:
    static DebugConsole& instance()
    {
        static DebugConsole inst;
        return inst;
    }

    void log(const std::string& message)
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(m_mutex);
        m_entries.push_back(message);
        if (m_entries.size() > m_maxEntries)
            m_entries.pop_front();
    }

    const std::deque<std::string>& getEntries() const { return m_entries; }

    void clear() { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(m_mutex); m_entries.clear(); }

private:
    std::deque<std::string> m_entries;
    size_t m_maxEntries = 500;
    std::mutex m_mutex;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/UI/DebugConsoleWidget.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "UI/Widget.h"
#include "UI/DebugConsole.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>

class DebugConsoleWidget : public Widget
{
public:
    void draw() override
    {
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(m_position.x, m_position.y), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
        ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(m_size.x, m_size.y), ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);

        if (!ImGui::Begin("Debug Console", &m_open))
        {
            ImGui::End();
            return;
        }

        ImGui::InputText("Filter", m_filterBuffer, sizeof(m_filterBuffer));
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::Button("Clear"))
            DebugConsole::instance().clear();

        ImGui::Separator();

        ImGui::BeginChild("ScrollingRegion", ImVec2(0, 0), false, ImGuiWindowFlags_HorizontalScrollbar);

        std::string filter = m_filterBuffer;
        std::transform(filter.begin(), filter.end(), filter.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::tolower(c); });

        for (const auto& entry : DebugConsole::instance().getEntries())
        {
            if (!filter.empty())
            {
                std::string lowerEntry = entry;
                std::transform(lowerEntry.begin(), lowerEntry.end(), lowerEntry.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::tolower(c); });

                if (lowerEntry.find(filter) == std::string::npos)
                    continue;
            }

            ImGui::TextUnformatted(entry.c_str());
        }

        if (ImGui::GetScrollY() >= ImGui::GetScrollMaxY() - 5.0f)
            ImGui::SetScrollHereY(1.0f);

        ImGui::EndChild();
        ImGui::End();
    }

    bool* getOpenFlag() { return &m_open; }
    
private:
    bool m_open = true;
    char m_filterBuffer[128] = "";
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/UI/ImageWidget.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "UI/Widget.h"
#include "Vulkan/Texture.h"
#include <imgui_impl_vulkan.h>
#include <memory>

class ImageWidget : public Widget
{
public:
    ImageWidget(std::shared_ptr<Texture> texture) : m_texture(texture) 
    {
        m_descriptorSet = ImGui_ImplVulkan_AddTexture(
            (VkSampler)m_texture->getSampler(),
            (VkImageView)m_texture->getImageView(),
            VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL);
    }

    ~ImageWidget()
    {
        if (m_descriptorSet)
            ImGui_ImplVulkan_RemoveTexture(m_descriptorSet);
    }

    void draw() override
    {
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(m_position.x, m_position.y));
        ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(m_size.x, m_size.y));
        ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.0f);

        ImGuiWindowFlags flags = ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDecoration | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoInputs
            | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoNav;

        ImGui::Begin(("##image_" + std::to_string((uintptr_t)this)).c_str(), nullptr, flags);
        ImGui::Image((ImTextureID)m_descriptorSet, ImVec2(m_size.x, m_size.y));
        ImGui::End();
    }

private:
    std::shared_ptr<Texture> m_texture;
    VkDescriptorSet m_descriptorSet = nullptr;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/UI/ProgressBarWidget.h

#pragma once
#include "UI/Widget.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <algorithm>

class ProgressBarWidget : public Widget
{
public:
    ProgressBarWidget(float initialValue = 0.0f) : m_value(initialValue) {}
    
    void draw() override
    {
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(m_position.x, m_position.y));
        ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.0f);

        ImGuiWindowFlags flags = ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDecoration | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoInputs
            | ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoNav;
        
        ImGui::Begin(("##progress_" + std::to_string((uintptr_t)this)).c_str(), nullptr, flags);

        ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_PlotHistogram, ImVec4(m_fillColor.r, m_fillColor.g, m_fillColor.b, m_fillColor.a));
        ImGui::ProgressBar(m_value, ImVec2(m_size.x, m_size.y), m_showLabel ? nullptr : "");
        ImGui::PopStyleColor();

        ImGui::End();
    }

    void setValue(float value) { m_value = std::clamp(value, 0.0f, 1.0f); }
    float getValue() const { return m_value; }

    void setFillColor(glm::vec4 color) { m_fillColor = color; }
    void setShowLabel(bool show) { m_showLabel = show; }

private:
    float m_value = 0.0f;
    glm::vec4 m_fillColor{0.2f, 0.7f, 0.2f, 1.0f};
    bool m_showLabel = true;
};

#pragma once
#include "UI/Widget.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <string>

class TextWidget : public Widget
{
public:
    TextWidget(std::string text) : m_text(std::move(text)) {}

    void draw() override
    {
        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(m_position.x, m_position.y));
        ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.0f);

        ImGuiWindowFlags flags = ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDecoration | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoInputs
            | ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoFocusOnAppearing | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoNav | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBackground;
        
        ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowBorderSize, 0.0f);

        ImGui::Begin(("##text_" + std::to_string((uintptr_t)this)).c_str(), nullptr, flags);
        ImGui::SetWindowFontScale(m_fontScale);
        ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(m_color.r, m_color.g, m_color.b, m_color.a), "%s", m_text.c_str());
        ImGui::End();
    
        ImGui::PopStyleVar();
    }

    void setText(const std::string& text) { m_text = text; }
    const std::string& getText() const { return m_text; }

    void setColor(glm::vec4 color) { m_color = color; }

    void setFontScale(float scale) { m_fontScale = scale; }
    float getFontScale() const { return m_fontScale; }

private:
    std::string m_text;
    glm::vec4 m_color{1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f};
    float m_fontScale = 1.0f;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/UI/UISystem.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "UI/Widget.h"
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

class UISystem
{
public:
    template<typename T, typename... Args>
    T& addWidget(Args&&... args)
    {
        auto widget = std::make_unique<T>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
        T& ref = *widget;
        m_widgets.push_back(std::move(widget));
        return ref;
    }

    void removeAllWidgets() { m_widgets.clear(); }

    void draw()
    {
        for (auto& widget : m_widgets)
        {
            if (widget->isVisible())
                widget->draw();
        }
    }

private:
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Widget>> m_widgets;
};

class BloomChain
{

};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Vulkan/BloomTarget.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <memory>

class BloomTarget
{
public:
    void initialize(ASH::Device* device, ASH::Extent2D extent);
    void cleanup();

    ASH::Texture* getColorTexture() const { return m_colorTexture.get(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getSampler() const { return m_sampler.get(); }
    ASH::Format getColorFormat() const { return m_colorFormat; }
    ASH::Extent2D getExtent() const { return m_extent; }

private:
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::Texture> m_colorTexture;
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::Sampler> m_sampler;
    ASH::Format m_colorFormat = ASH::Format::R16G16B16A16_Float;

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Vulkan/Pipeline.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

enum class LayoutType
{
    None,
    Default,
    Fog,
    Decal,
    Skinned,
    PostProcess,
    SingleTexture,
    Skybox,
    VoxelClear,
    Voxelize,
    StampProxy,
    VoxelizeSkinned,
    DownsampleVoxels,
    SkinnedShadow,
};

struct PipelineConfig
{
    std::string vertexShader;
    std::string fragmentShader = "";
    ASH::RenderPass* renderPass = nullptr;
    uint32_t frameCount = 1;
    uint32_t maxMaterials = 100;
    uint32_t pushConstantSize = sizeof(float) * 32;
    ASH::BlendFactor blendSrcFactor = ASH::BlendFactor::One;
    ASH::BlendFactor blendDstFactor = ASH::BlendFactor::Zero;
    ASH::BlendOp blendOp = ASH::BlendOp::Add;
    ASH::CompareOp depthCompareOp = ASH::CompareOp::Less;
    ASH::ShaderStage pushStage = ASH::ShaderStage::Vertex;
    ASH::CullMode cullMode = ASH::CullMode::Back;
    bool depthClampEnable = false;
    uint32_t colorAttachmentCount = 1;
    bool depthWrite = true;
    bool depthTest = true;
    bool blendEnable = false;
    bool useVertexInput = true;
    bool depthBiasEnable = false;
    bool skinnedLayout = false;
    float depthBiasConstant = 0.0f;
    float depthBiasSlope = 0.0f;
    LayoutType layoutType = LayoutType::Default;
};

ASH::DescriptorSetLayoutDesc buildLayoutDesc(LayoutType layoutType);

class Pipeline
{
public:
    void initialize(ASH::Device* device, const PipelineConfig& config);
    void cleanup();

    ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* getDescriptorSetLayout() const { return m_descriptorSetLayout.get(); }
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::DescriptorSet> createDescriptorSet() { return m_device->createDescriptorSet(m_descriptorSetLayout.get()); }
    ASH::Pipeline* getPipeline() const { return m_pipeline.get(); }

private:
    ASH::Device* m_device = nullptr;
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::DescriptorSetLayout> m_descriptorSetLayout;
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::Pipeline> m_pipeline;
};

#pragma once

#include "Vulkan/NoAA.h"

#include "Core/GodRaySettings.h"

#include "Scene/Scene.h"
#include "Vulkan/LdrTarget.h"
#include "Vulkan/FogCameraBuffer.h"
#include "Core/FogSettings.h"
#include "Vulkan/FXAA.h"
#include "Vulkan/DecalBox.h"
#include "Vulkan/DecalCameraBuffer.h"
#include "Scene/ScreenSpaceDecal.h"
#include "Core/Camera.h"
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <vulkan/vulkan.hpp>
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <future>
#include <thread>
#include <memory>

class Renderer
{
public:
    void initialize(GLFWwindow* window);
    void drawScene(const Scene& scene, const Camera& camera);
    void cleanup();

    void onResize() { m_framebufferResized = true; }
    void setLights(const LightData& lights) { m_pendingLights = lights; }
    void setAAMethod(AAMethod method);
    void setExposure(float exp) { m_exposure = exp; }
    void setFogSettings(const FogSettings& settings) { m_fogSettings = settings; }
    void setGodRaySettings(const GodRaySettings& settings) { m_godRaySettings = settings; }
    void setTotalTime(float t) { m_totalTime = t; }
    void addScreenSpaceDecal(std::shared_ptr<ScreenSpaceDecal> decal) { m_screenSpaceDecals.push_back(decal); }
    void setIBLSettings(const IBLSettings& settings) { m_iblSettings = settings; }
    void setVoxelGIEnabled(bool enabled) { m_voxelGIEnabled = enabled; }
    
    void initImGui(GLFWwindow* window);
    void beginImGuiFrame();
    void renderImGui(ASH::CommandBuffer* commandBuffer);
    void cleanupImGui();

    void loadEnvironmentMap(const std::string& hdrPath);

    ASH::Device* getDevice() const { return m_context.getDevice(); }
    const FogSettings& getFogSettings() const { return m_fogSettings; }
    ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* getDescriptorSetLayout() const { return m_pipeline.getDescriptorSetLayout(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getShadowAtlasSampler() const { return m_shadowAtlas.getSampler(); }
    ASH::Texture* getShadowAtlasView() const { return m_shadowAtlas.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Texture* getShadowView() const { return m_shadowMap.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getShadowSampler() const { return m_shadowMap.getSampler(); }
    ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* getDecalDescriptorSetLayout() const { return m_decalPipeline.getDescriptorSetLayout(); }
    ASH::Texture* getDepthView() const { return m_depthBuffer.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getDepthSampler() const { return m_depthBuffer.getSampler(); }
    ASH::Texture* getIrradianceView() const { return m_irradianceCubemap.getTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getIrradianceSampler() const { return m_irradianceCubemap.getSampler(); }
    const LightBuffer& getLightBuffer() const { return m_lightBuffer; }
    const DecalCameraBuffer& getDecalCameraBuffer() const { return m_decalCameraBuffer; }
    const GodRaySettings& getGodRaySettings() const { return m_godRaySettings; }
    std::vector<ASH::Buffer*> getUboBuffers() const;
    std::vector<ASH::Buffer*> getLightBuffers() const;
    uint32_t getFramesInFlight() const { return MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; }
    ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* getSkinnedDescriptorSetLayout() const { return m_skinnedPipeline.getDescriptorSetLayout(); }
    ASH::DescriptorSetLayout* getSkinnedShadowDescriptorSetLayout() const { return m_skinnedShadowPipeline.getDescriptorSetLayout(); }
    float getAspect() const;
    ASH::Texture* getVoxelAtlasTexture() const { return m_voxelGI.getAtlasTexture(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getVoxelAtlasSampler() const { return m_voxelGI.getAtlasSampler(); }
    ASH::Buffer* getVoxelPageTableBuffer() const { return m_voxelGI.getPageTableBuffer(); }
    ASH::Buffer* getVoxelGridInfoBuffer() const { return m_voxelGridInfoBuffer.get(); }

    void enableVoxelGI(float chunkSize, uint32_t chunkResolution, int32_t visibilityRadiusChunks);


    std::vector<const GameObject*> parallelCull(const Scene& scene, const std::array<glm::vec4, 6>& planes) const;
    bool isVisible(const GameObject& obj, const std::array<glm::vec4, 6>& planes) const;

private:
    void recordCommandBuffer(ASH::CommandBuffer* cmd, uint32_t imageIndex,
        const Scene& scene, const Camera& camera);

// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <memory>

class ShadowAtlas
{
public:
    static constexpr uint32_t TILE_SIZE = 512;
    static constexpr uint32_t TILES_X = 6;
    static constexpr uint32_t TILES_Y = 3;

    void initialize(ASH::Device* device);
    void cleanup();

    ASH::Texture* getTexture() const { return m_texture.get(); }
    ASH::Sampler* getSampler() const { return m_sampler.get(); }
    ASH::Format getFormat() const { return m_format; }
    uint32_t getWidth() const { return TILE_SIZE * TILES_X; }
    uint32_t getHeight() const { return TILE_SIZE * TILES_Y; }

    ASH::Rect2D getTileRect(uint32_t lightSlot, uint32_t face) const
    {
        ASH::Rect2D rect{};
        rect.x = (int32_t)(face * TILE_SIZE);
        rect.y = (int32_t)(lightSlot * TILE_SIZE);
        rect.width = TILE_SIZE;
        rect.height = TILE_SIZE;
        return rect;
    }

private:
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::Texture> m_texture;
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::Sampler> m_sampler;
    ASH::Format m_format = ASH::Format::D32_Float;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Vulkan/SkinnedVertex.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <array>

struct SkinnedVertex
{
    glm::vec3 pos;
    glm::vec3 normal;
    glm::vec2 texCoord;
    glm::vec3 tangent;
    glm::vec4 boneIndices{0.0f};
    glm::vec4 boneWeights{0.0f};

    static ASH::VertexBindingDesc getBindingDescriptorASH()
    {
        ASH::VertexBindingDesc binding{};
        binding.binding = 0;
        binding.stride = sizeof(SkinnedVertex);
        binding.perInstance = false;
        return binding;
    }

    static std::array<ASH::VertexAttributeDesc, 6> getAttributeDescriptionsASH()
    {
        std::array<ASH::VertexAttributeDesc, 6> attributes{};

        attributes[0].binding = 0; attributes[0].location = 0;
        attributes[0].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[0].offset = offsetof(SkinnedVertex, pos);

        attributes[1].binding = 0; attributes[1].location = 1;
        attributes[1].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[1].offset = offsetof(SkinnedVertex, normal);

        attributes[2].binding = 0; attributes[2].location = 2;
        attributes[2].format = ASH::Format::R32G32_Float;
        attributes[2].offset = offsetof(SkinnedVertex, texCoord);

        attributes[3].binding = 0; attributes[3].location = 3;
        attributes[3].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[3].offset = offsetof(SkinnedVertex, tangent);

        attributes[4].binding = 0; attributes[4].location = 4;
        attributes[4].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32A32_Float;
        attributes[4].offset = offsetof(SkinnedVertex, boneIndices);

        attributes[5].binding = 0; attributes[5].location = 5;
        attributes[5].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32A32_Float;
        attributes[5].offset = offsetof(SkinnedVertex, boneWeights);

        return attributes;
    }

};

class SwapChain
{

    void recreate(VulkanContext& context, GLFWwindow* window);
    void cleanup();

    bool acquireNextImage(uint32_t& outImageIndex, ASH::Semaphore* signalSemaphore)
    {
        return m_swapChain->acquireNextImage(outImageIndex, signalSemaphore);
    }

    void present(uint32_t imageIndex, ASH::Semaphore* waitSemaphore)
    {
        m_swapChain->present(imageIndex, waitSemaphore);
    }

    ASH::Texture* getImage(uint32_t index) const { return m_swapChain->getImage(index); }
    uint32_t getImageCount() const { return m_swapChain->getImageCount(); }
    ASH::Extent2D getExtent() const { return m_swapChain->getExtent(); }
    ASH::Format getImageFormat() const { return m_format; }

private:
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::SwapChain> m_swapChain;
    ASH::Format m_format = ASH::Format::B8G8R8A8_UNorm;
};
