// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Vulkan/Vertex.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <array>

struct Vertex
{
    glm::vec3 pos;
    glm::vec3 color;
    glm::vec2 texCoord;
    glm::vec3 normal;
    glm::vec3 tangent;
    glm::vec2 lightmapUV;

    bool operator==(const Vertex& other) const
    {
        return pos == other.pos && color == other.color && 
            texCoord == other.texCoord && normal == other.normal;
    }

    static ASH::VertexBindingDesc getBindingDescriptorASH()
    {
        ASH::VertexBindingDesc binding{};
        binding.binding = 0;
        binding.stride = sizeof(Vertex);
        binding.perInstance = false;
        return binding;
    }

    static std::array<ASH::VertexAttributeDesc, 6> getAttributeDescriptionsASH()
    {
        std::array<ASH::VertexAttributeDesc, 6> attributes{};

        attributes[0].binding = 0; attributes[0].location = 0;
        attributes[0].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[0].offset = offsetof(Vertex, pos);

        attributes[1].binding = 0; attributes[1].location = 1;
        attributes[1].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[1].offset = offsetof(Vertex, color);

        attributes[2].binding = 0; attributes[2].location = 2;
        attributes[2].format = ASH::Format::R32G32_Float;
        attributes[2].offset = offsetof(Vertex, texCoord);

        attributes[3].binding = 0; attributes[3].location = 3;
        attributes[3].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[3].offset = offsetof(Vertex, normal);

        attributes[4].binding = 0; attributes[4].location = 4;
        attributes[4].format = ASH::Format::R32G32B32_Float;
        attributes[4].offset = offsetof(Vertex, tangent);

        attributes[5].binding = 0; attributes[5].location = 5;
        attributes[5].format = ASH::Format::R32G32_Float;
        attributes[5].offset = offsetof(Vertex, lightmapUV);

        return attributes;
    }
};

namespace std
{
    template<> struct hash<Vertex>
    {
        size_t operator()(const Vertex& v) const
        {
            size_t h1 = hash<float>()(v.pos.x) ^ (hash<float>()(v.pos.y) << 1) ^ (hash<float>()(v.pos.z) << 2);
            size_t h2 = hash<float>()(v.texCoord.x) ^ (hash<float>()(v.texCoord.y) << 1);
            size_t h3 = hash<float>()(v.normal.x) ^ (hash<float>()(v.normal.z) << 1);
            return h1 ^ (h2 << 3) ^ (h3 << 6);
        }
    };
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Vulkan/VoxelChunk.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "ASH/ASH.h"
#include "Vulkan/DynamicVoxelProxy.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <functional>

struct ChunkCoord
{
    int32_t x, y, z;

    bool operator==(const ChunkCoord& other) const
    {
        return x == other.x && y == other.y && z == other.z;
    }
};

struct ChunkCoordHash
{
    size_t operator()(const ChunkCoord& c) const
    {
        size_t h = std::hash<int32_t>()(c.x);
        h ^= std::hash<int32_t>()(c.y) + 0x9e3779b9 + (h << 6) + (h >> 2);
        h ^= std::hash<int32_t>()(c.z) + 0x9e3779b9 + (h << 6) + (h >> 2);
        return h;
    }
};

struct VoxelChunk
{
    ChunkCoord coord{};
    int32_t atlasSlotIndex = -1;
    bool needsRebake = true;
};


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/include/Vulkan/VoxelChunkManager.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Vulkan/VoxelChunk.h"
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

class VoxelChunkManager
{
public:
    void initialize(float chunkSize, int32_t visibilityRadiusChunks, int32_t atlasSlotsPerAxis);

    struct UpdateResult
    {
        std::vector<ChunkCoord> chunksToBake;
        std::vector<ChunkCoord> chunksToEvict;
    };
    UpdateResult update(glm::vec3 cameraPosition);

    ChunkCoord worldToChunkCoord(glm::vec3 worldPos) const;
    glm::vec3 chunkCoordToWorldMin(ChunkCoord coord) const;

    float getChunkSize() const { return m_chunkSize; }
    int32_t getAtlasSlotsPerAxis() const { return m_atlasSlotsPerAxis; }
    int32_t getMaxAtlasSlots() const { return m_atlasSlotsPerAxis * m_atlasSlotsPerAxis * m_atlasSlotsPerAxis; }

    const std::unordered_map<ChunkCoord, VoxelChunk, ChunkCoordHash>& getActiveChunks() const { return m_activeChunks; }
    VoxelChunk* getChunk(ChunkCoord coord);

private:
    int32_t findFreeAtlasSlot();

    float m_chunkSize = 8.0f;
    int32_t m_visibilityRadiusChunks = 2;
    int32_t m_atlasSlotsPerAxis = 5;

    std::unordered_map<ChunkCoord, VoxelChunk, ChunkCoordHash> m_activeChunks;
    std::vector<bool> m_atlasSlotOccupied;
};

#pragma once

{
public:
    void initialize(GLFWwindow* window);
    void shutdown();

    ASH::Device* getDevice() const { return m_device.get(); }
private:
    std::unique_ptr<ASH::Device> m_device;
    VkSurfaceKHR m_surface = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/ibl/equirect_to_cubemap.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragLocalPos;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D equirectMap;

const vec2 invAtan = vec2(0.1591, 0.3183);

vec2 sampleSphericalMap(vec3 dir)
{
    vec2 uv = vec2(atan(dir.z, dir.x), asin(dir.y));
    uv *= invAtan;
    uv += 0.5;
    uv.y = 1.0 - uv.y;
    return uv;
}

void main()
{
    vec2 uv = sampleSphericalMap(normalize(fragLocalPos));
    vec3 color = texture(equirectMap, uv).rgb;

    float maxBrightness = 8.0;
    float luminance = dot(color, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
    if (luminance > maxBrightness)
        color *= maxBrightness / luminance;

    outColor = vec4(color, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/ibl/equirect_to_cubemap.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 viewProj;
} push;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragLocalPos;

void main()
{
    fragLocalPos = inPosition;
    gl_Position = push.viewProj * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
}


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/ibl/irradiance_convolution.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragLocalPos;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

layout(binding = 0) uniform samplerCube environmentMap;

const float PI = 3.14159265359;

void main()
{
    vec3 N = normalize(fragLocalPos);

    vec3 up = abs(N.y) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0) : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vec3 right = normalize(cross(up, N));
    up = normalize(cross(N, right));

    vec3 irradiance = vec3(0.0);
    float sampleDelta = 0.025;
    float sampleCount = 0.0;

    for (float phi = 0.0; phi < 2.0 * PI; phi += sampleDelta)
    {
        for (float theta = 0.0; theta < 0.5 * PI; theta += sampleDelta)
        {
            vec3 tangentSample = vec3(sin(theta) * cos(phi), sin(theta) * sin(phi), cos(theta));
            vec3 sampleVec = tangentSample.x * right + tangentSample.y * up + tangentSample.z * N;

            irradiance += texture(environmentMap, sampleVec).rgb * cos(theta) * sin(theta);
            sampleCount += 1.0;
        }
    }

    irradiance = PI * irradiance / sampleCount;
    outColor = vec4(irradiance, 1.0);
}


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/ibl/skybox.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragLocalPos;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

layout(binding = 1) uniform samplerCube environmentMap;

void main()
{
    vec3 color = texture(environmentMap, fragLocalPos).rgb;
    color = color / (color + vec3(1.0));
    outColor = vec4(color, 1.0);
}


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/ibl/skybox.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera
{
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
} cam;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragLocalPos;

void main()
{
    fragLocalPos = inPosition;

    mat4 viewNoTranslation = mat4(mat3(cam.view));
    vec4 pos = cam.proj * viewNoTranslation * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);

    gl_Position = pos.xyww;
}


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/pbr/decal_box.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "brdf.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D decalTexture;      // albedo.rgb + базовая opacity в .a
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D depthTexture;

layout(binding = 2) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 invViewProj;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

struct Light { vec4 position; vec4 color; };
layout(binding = 3) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(binding = 4) uniform sampler2D opacityTexture;
layout(binding = 5) uniform sampler2D normalTexture;
layout(binding = 6) uniform sampler2D mrTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    mat4 model;
    mat4 invModel;
    vec4 params;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec4 fragClipPos;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 screenUV = (fragClipPos.xy / fragClipPos.w) * 0.5 + 0.5;
    float sceneDepth = texture(depthTexture, screenUV).r;

    vec4 ndc = vec4(screenUV * 2.0 - 1.0, sceneDepth, 1.0);
    vec4 worldPosH = cam.invViewProj * ndc;
    vec3 worldPos = worldPosH.xyz / worldPosH.w;

    vec3 localPos = (push.invModel * vec4(worldPos, 1.0)).xyz;
    if (any(greaterThan(abs(localPos), vec3(0.5))))
        discard;

    vec2 decalUV = localPos.xz + 0.5;

    vec4 albedoTex = texture(decalTexture, decalUV);
    float opacity = albedoTex.a * texture(opacityTexture, decalUV).r;
    if (opacity < 0.01)
        discard;

    vec3 albedo = pow(albedoTex.rgb, vec3(2.2));

    mat3 normalMat = mat3(transpose(inverse(push.model)));
    vec3 T  = normalize(normalMat * vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    vec3 B  = normalize(normalMat * vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
    vec3 N0 = normalize(normalMat * vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0));
    mat3 TBN = mat3(T, B, N0);

    vec3 nSample = texture(normalTexture, decalUV).rgb * 2.0 - 1.0;
    vec3 N = normalize(TBN * nSample);

    vec3 mr = texture(mrTexture, decalUV).rgb;
    float roughness = clamp(mr.g * push.params.y, 0.05, 1.0);
    float metallic  = clamp(mr.b * push.params.x, 0.0, 1.0);

    vec3 V = normalize(cam.camPos.xyz - worldPos);
    vec3 F0 = mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metallic);
    vec3 Lo = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < lightData.count.x; i++)
    {
        Light light = lightData.lights[i];
        vec3 L, radiance;

        if (light.position.w < 0.5) {
            L = normalize(-light.position.xyz);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a;
        } else {
            vec3 toLight = light.position.xyz - worldPos;
            float dist2 = max(dot(toLight, toLight), 0.01);
            L = normalize(toLight);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a / dist2;
        }

        vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
        float D = DistributionGGX(N, H, roughness);
        float G = GeometrySmith(N, V, L, roughness);
        vec3  F = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(H, V), 0.0), F0);

        vec3 specular = (D * G * F) / (4.0 * max(dot(N, V), 0.0) * max(dot(N, L), 0.0) + 0.0001);
        vec3 kD = (1.0 - F) * (1.0 - metallic);
        float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);

        Lo += (kD * albedo / PI + specular) * radiance * NdotL;
    }

    vec3 ambient = vec3(0.02) * albedo;
    outColor = vec4(ambient + Lo, opacity);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/pbr/decal_box.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 2) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 invViewProj;
} cam;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    mat4 model;
    mat4 invModel;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;

layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragClipPos;

void main()
{
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    gl_Position = cam.proj * cam.view * worldPos;
    fragClipPos = gl_Position;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/pbr/pbr.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

#include "brdf.glsl"
#include "tonemap.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 prevViewProj;
    mat4 viewProjNoJitter;
    vec4 camPos;
    vec4 iblParams;
} cam;

layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D albedoMap;
layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2D normalMap;
layout(binding = 3) uniform sampler2D metallicRoughnessMap;
layout(binding = 4) uniform sampler2D aoMap;

struct Light {
    vec4 position;
    vec4 color;
};

layout(binding = 5) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(binding = 6) uniform sampler2DShadow shadowMap;
layout(binding = 7) uniform sampler2D shadowAtlas;
layout(binding = 8) uniform sampler2D lightmapMap;
layout(binding = 9) uniform samplerCube irradianceMap;
layout(binding = 10) uniform sampler3D voxelTexture;

layout(binding = 11) readonly buffer PageTable {
    int slots[];
} pageTable;

layout(binding = 12) uniform VoxelGridInfo {
    vec4 chunkParams;
    ivec4 cameraChunkCoord;
    vec4 flags;
} voxelInfo;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec2 fragTexCoord;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 fragLightSpacePos;
layout(location = 3) in mat3 fragTBN;

layout(location = 6) in vec4 fragCurrClipPos;
layout(location = 7) in vec4 fragPrevClipPos;
layout(location = 8) in vec2 fragLightmapUV;

layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 outVelocity;

const vec3 SKY_ZENITH  = vec3(0.20, 0.32, 0.55);
const vec3 SKY_HORIZON = vec3(0.55, 0.60, 0.65);
const vec3 GROUND      = vec3(0.05, 0.05, 0.06);

const float POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE = 25.0;

vec3 sampleAtlasVoxel(vec3 worldPos)
{
    float chunkSize = voxelInfo.chunkParams.x;
    int chunkResolution = int(voxelInfo.chunkParams.y);
    int slotsPerAxis = int(voxelInfo.chunkParams.z);
    int radius = int(voxelInfo.chunkParams.w);
    int paddedResolution = voxelInfo.cameraChunkCoord.w;

    ivec3 chunkCoord = ivec3(floor(worldPos / chunkSize));
    ivec3 relCoord = chunkCoord - voxelInfo.cameraChunkCoord.xyz + ivec3(radius);

    if (any(lessThan(relCoord, ivec3(0))) || any(greaterThanEqual(relCoord, ivec3(slotsPerAxis))))
        return vec3(0.0);

    int pageIndex = relCoord.x + relCoord.y * slotsPerAxis + relCoord.z * slotsPerAxis * slotsPerAxis;
    int atlasSlot = pageTable.slots[pageIndex];

    if (atlasSlot < 0)
        return vec3(0.0);

    int slotZ = atlasSlot / (slotsPerAxis * slotsPerAxis);
    int slotY = (atlasSlot / slotsPerAxis) % slotsPerAxis;
    int slotX = atlasSlot % slotsPerAxis;

    vec3 chunkWorldMin = vec3(chunkCoord) * chunkSize;
    vec3 localPos = (worldPos - chunkWorldMin) / chunkSize;

    float paddingFrac = 1.0 / float(paddedResolution);
    vec3 paddedLocalPos = paddingFrac + localPos * (1.0 - 2.0 * paddingFrac);

    vec3 atlasUV = (vec3(slotX, slotY, slotZ) + paddedLocalPos) / float(slotsPerAxis);

    return texture(voxelTexture, atlasUV).rgb;
}

vec3 calculateVoxelGI(vec3 worldPos, vec3 N)
{
    vec3 up = abs(N.y) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0) : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vec3 right = normalize(cross(up, N));
    up = normalize(cross(N, right));

    const int NUM_CONES = 9;
    vec3 coneDirs[NUM_CONES] = vec3[](
        vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
        vec3(0.0, 0.866, 0.5),
        vec3(0.823, 0.267, 0.5),
        vec3(0.509, -0.7, 0.5),
        vec3(-0.509, -0.7, 0.5),
        vec3(-0.823, 0.267, 0.5),
        vec3(0.588, 0.588, 0.555),
        vec3(-0.588, 0.588, 0.555),
        vec3(0.0, -0.809, 0.588)
    );
    float coneWeights[NUM_CONES] = float[](0.16, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.11, 0.11, 0.12);

    vec3 result = vec3(0.0);
    float chunkSize = voxelInfo.chunkParams.x;
    float voxelSize = chunkSize / voxelInfo.chunkParams.y;

    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_CONES; i++)
    {
        vec3 dir = coneDirs[i].x * right + coneDirs[i].y * up + coneDirs[i].z * N;

        float dist = voxelSize * 0.5;
        float maxDist = chunkSize * 1.2;

        for (int step = 0; step < 30; step++)
        {
            if (dist > maxDist) break;

            vec3 samplePos = worldPos + dir * dist;
            vec3 s = sampleAtlasVoxel(samplePos);

            if (length(s) > 0.001)
            {
                vec3 sNext = sampleAtlasVoxel(worldPos + dir * (dist + voxelSize));
                vec3 blended = (length(sNext) > 0.001) ? (s + sNext) * 0.5 : s;
                result += blended * coneWeights[i];
                break;
            }

            dist += voxelSize * 0.5f;
        }
    }

    return result;
}

vec3 sampleSky(vec3 dir)
{
    float t = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, dir.y * 0.5 + 0.5);
    vec3 upperSky = mix(SKY_HORIZON, SKY_ZENITH, t);
    return mix(GROUND, upperSky, smoothstep(-0.05, 0.05, dir.y));
}

float interleavedGradientNoise(vec2 p)
{
    const vec3 magic = vec3(0.06711056, 0.00583715, 52.9829189);
    return fract(magic.z * fract(dot(p, magic.xy)));
}

float calculateShadow(vec4 lightSpacePos, vec3 N, vec3 L, vec2 screenXY)
{
    vec3 proj = lightSpacePos.xyz / lightSpacePos.w;
    proj.xy = proj.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;

    if (proj.x < 0.0 || proj.x > 1.0 ||
        proj.y < 0.0 || proj.y > 1.0 ||
        proj.z < 0.0 || proj.z > 1.0)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    float bias = max(0.0025 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.0006);
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(shadowMap, 0));

    float angle = interleavedGradientNoise(screenXY) * 6.2831853;
    float s = sin(angle), c = cos(angle);
    mat2 rot = mat2(c, -s, s, c);

    float shadow = 0.0;
    const int R = 1;
    for (int x = -R; x <= R; x++)
    {
        for (int y = -R; y <= R; y++)
        {
            vec2 offset = rot * (vec2(x, y) * texelSize);
            shadow += texture(shadowMap, vec3(proj.xy + offset, proj.z - bias));
        }
    }
    float samples = float((2 * R + 1) * (2 * R + 1));
    return shadow / samples;
}

vec3 sampleCubeFaceAndUV(vec3 dir)
{
    vec3 absDir = abs(dir);
    float faceIndex;
    vec2 uv;

    if (absDir.x >= absDir.y && absDir.x >= absDir.z)
    {
        faceIndex = dir.x > 0.0 ? 0.0 : 1.0;
        uv = (dir.x > 0.0) ? vec2(-dir.z, -dir.y) / absDir.x : vec2(dir.z, -dir.y) / absDir.x;
    }
    else if (absDir.y >= absDir.x && absDir.y >= absDir.z)
    {
        faceIndex = dir.y > 0.0 ? 2.0 : 3.0;
        uv = (dir.y > 0.0) ? vec2(dir.x, dir.z) / absDir.y : vec2(dir.x, -dir.z) / absDir.y;
    }
    else
    {
        faceIndex = dir.z > 0.0 ? 4.0 : 5.0;
        uv = (dir.z > 0.0) ? vec2(dir.x, -dir.y) / absDir.z : vec2(-dir.x, -dir.y) / absDir.z;
    }

    uv = uv * 0.5 + 0.5;
    return vec3(uv, faceIndex);
}

float sampleShadowAtlas(vec3 dir, int lightSlot)
{
    vec3 faceUV = sampleCubeFaceAndUV(dir);

    const float TILE = 1.0 / 6.0;
    const float ROW = 1.0 / 3.0;

    vec2 atlasUV;
    atlasUV.x = (faceUV.z + faceUV.x) * TILE;
    atlasUV.y = (float(lightSlot) + faceUV.y) * ROW;

    return texture(shadowAtlas, atlasUV).r;
}

float calculatePointShadow(vec3 fragPos, vec3 lightPos, vec3 N, vec2 screenXY, int shadowSlot)
{
    vec3 toFrag = fragPos - lightPos;
    float currentDist = length(toFrag);
    vec3 L = normalize(-toFrag);

    float bias = max(0.15 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.03);

    const int SAMPLES = 20;
    vec3 sampleOffsets[SAMPLES] = vec3[]
    (
        vec3( 1,  1,  1), vec3( 1, -1,  1), vec3(-1, -1,  1), vec3(-1,  1,  1),
        vec3( 1,  1, -1), vec3( 1, -1, -1), vec3(-1, -1, -1), vec3(-1,  1, -1),
        vec3( 1,  1,  0), vec3( 1, -1,  0), vec3(-1, -1,  0), vec3(-1,  1,  0),
        vec3( 1,  0,  1), vec3(-1,  0,  1), vec3( 1,  0, -1), vec3(-1,  0, -1),
        vec3( 0,  1,  1), vec3( 0, -1,  1), vec3( 0, -1, -1), vec3( 0,  1, -1)
    );

    float angle = interleavedGradientNoise(screenXY) * 6.2831853;
    float s = sin(angle), c = cos(angle);
    vec3 axis = L;

    mat3 rot = mat3(
        c + axis.x*axis.x*(1.0-c),        axis.x*axis.y*(1.0-c) - axis.z*s, axis.x*axis.z*(1.0-c) + axis.y*s,
        axis.y*axis.x*(1.0-c) + axis.z*s, c + axis.y*axis.y*(1.0-c),        axis.y*axis.z*(1.0-c) - axis.x*s,
        axis.z*axis.x*(1.0-c) - axis.y*s, axis.z*axis.y*(1.0-c) + axis.x*s, c + axis.z*axis.z*(1.0-c)
    );

    float shadow = 0.0;
    float diskRadius = (1.0 + (currentDist / POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE)) / 45.0;

    for (int i = 0; i < SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        vec3 offset = rot * sampleOffsets[i];
        float closestDist = sampleShadowAtlas(toFrag + offset * diskRadius, shadowSlot) * POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE;

        if (currentDist - bias > closestDist)
            shadow += 1.0;
    }

    return shadow / float(SAMPLES);
}

void main()
{
    vec3 albedo = pow(texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).rgb, vec3(2.2));
    vec3 mr = texture(metallicRoughnessMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    float roughness = clamp(mr.g, 0.4, 1.0);
    float metallic = mr.b;
    float ao = texture(aoMap, fragTexCoord).r;

    vec3 N = texture(normalMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    N = N * 2.0 - 1.0;
    N = normalize(fragTBN * N);

    vec3 V = normalize(cam.camPos.xyz - fragWorldPos);
    vec3 R = reflect(-V, N);
    vec3 F0 = mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metallic);

    vec3 Lo = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < lightData.count.x; i++)
    {
        Light light = lightData.lights[i];
        vec3 L, radiance;

        if (light.position.w < 0.5) {
            L = normalize(-light.position.xyz);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a;
        }
        else
        {
            vec3 toLight = light.position.xyz - fragWorldPos;
            float dist2 = max(dot(toLight, toLight), 0.01);
            L = normalize(toLight);
            float atten = 1.0 / dist2;
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a * atten;

            int shadowSlot = -1;
            if (i == lightData.count.y) shadowSlot = 0;
            else if (i == lightData.count.z) shadowSlot = 1;
            else if (i == lightData.count.w) shadowSlot = 2;

            if (shadowSlot >= 0)
            {
                float pShadow = calculatePointShadow(fragWorldPos, light.position.xyz, N, gl_FragCoord.xy, shadowSlot);
                radiance *= (1.0 - pShadow);
            }
        }

        float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
        float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0);

        if (NdotL > 0.0)
        {
            vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
            float D = DistributionGGX(N, H, roughness);
            float G = GeometrySmith(N, V, L, roughness);
            vec3 F = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(H, V), 0.0), F0);

            vec3 numerator = D * G * F;
            float denominator = 4.0 * NdotV * NdotL + 0.0001;
            vec3 specular = numerator / denominator;
            specular = min(specular, vec3(8.0));

            vec3 kD = (1.0 - F) * (1.0 - metallic);

            float visibility = 1.0;
            if (i == 0 && light.position.w < 0.5) {
                vec3 geoN = normalize(fragTBN[2]);
                visibility = calculateShadow(fragLightSpacePos, geoN, L, gl_FragCoord.xy);
            }

            Lo += (kD * albedo / PI + specular) * radiance * NdotL * visibility;
        }
    }

    //vec3 irradiance = texture(irradianceMap, N).rgb;
    //vec3 ambientDiffuse = irradiance * albedo * (1.0 - metallic) * cam.iblParams.x;
    //vec3 ambient = ambientDiffuse * ao;
    
    bool insideVoxelGrid = voxelInfo.flags.x > 0.5;
    vec3 iblAmbient = texture(irradianceMap, N).rgb * albedo * (1.0 - metallic) * cam.iblParams.x;

    vec3 indirectDiffuse;
    if (insideVoxelGrid)
    {
        vec3 voxelBounce = calculateVoxelGI(fragWorldPos, N) * albedo * (1.0 - metallic);
        indirectDiffuse = mix(iblAmbient, voxelBounce, 0.7);
    }
    else
    {
        indirectDiffuse = iblAmbient;
    }

    vec3 ambient = indirectDiffuse * ao;
    
    vec3 color = ambient + Lo;

    vec3 lightmapColor = texture(lightmapMap, fragLightmapUV).rgb;
    color += lightmapColor * 0.05 * ao;
    
    //vec3 rawVoxel = sampleAtlasVoxel(fragWorldPos);
    //outColor = vec4(rawVoxel * 2.0, 1.0);
    //return;

    float alpha = texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).a;
    outColor = vec4(color, alpha);

    vec2 currNDC = fragCurrClipPos.xy / fragCurrClipPos.w;
    vec2 prevNDC = fragPrevClipPos.xy / fragPrevClipPos.w;
    outVelocity = (currNDC - prevNDC) * 0.5;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/pbr/pbr.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 prevViewProj;
    mat4 viewProjNoJitter;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 model;
    mat4 normalMatrix;
    mat4 prevModel;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 inColor;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 inTexCoord;
layout(location = 3) in vec3 inNormal;
layout(location = 4) in vec3 inTangent;
layout(location = 5) in vec2 inLightmapUV;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 fragTexCoord;
layout(location = 2) out vec4 fragLightSpacePos;
layout(location = 3) out mat3 fragTBN;
layout(location = 6) out vec4 fragCurrClipPos;
layout(location = 7) out vec4 fragPrevClipPos;
layout(location = 8) out vec2 fragLightmapUV;

void main()
{
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    gl_Position = cam.proj * cam.view * worldPos;

    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    fragTexCoord = inTexCoord;
    fragLightSpacePos = cam.lightSpaceMatrix * worldPos;

    fragCurrClipPos = cam.viewProjNoJitter * worldPos;

    vec4 prevWorldPos = push.prevModel * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    fragPrevClipPos = cam.prevViewProj * prevWorldPos;

    fragLightmapUV = inLightmapUV;

    mat3 nm = mat3(push.normalMatrix);
    vec3 N = normalize(nm * inNormal);
    vec3 T = normalize(nm * inTangent);
    T = normalize(T - dot(T, N) * N);
    vec3 B = cross(N, T);
    fragTBN = mat3(T, B, N);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/pbr/skinned.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

#include "brdf.glsl"
#include "tonemap.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 prevViewProj;
    mat4 viewProjNoJitter;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D albedoMap;
layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2D normalMap;
layout(binding = 3) uniform sampler2D metallicRoughnessMap;
layout(binding = 4) uniform sampler2D aoMap;

struct Light {
    vec4 position;
    vec4 color;
};

layout(binding = 5) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(binding = 6) uniform sampler2DShadow shadowMap;
layout(binding = 7) uniform sampler2D shadowAtlas;
layout(binding = 8) uniform sampler2D lightmapMap;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec2 fragTexCoord;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 fragLightSpacePos;
layout(location = 3) in mat3 fragTBN;

layout(location = 6) in vec4 fragCurrClipPos;
layout(location = 7) in vec4 fragPrevClipPos;
layout(location = 8) in vec2 fragLightmapUV;

layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 outVelocity;

const vec3 SKY_ZENITH  = vec3(0.20, 0.32, 0.55);
const vec3 SKY_HORIZON = vec3(0.55, 0.60, 0.65);
const vec3 GROUND      = vec3(0.05, 0.05, 0.06);

const float POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE = 25.0;

vec3 sampleSky(vec3 dir)
{
    float t = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, dir.y * 0.5 + 0.5);
    vec3 upperSky = mix(SKY_HORIZON, SKY_ZENITH, t);
    return mix(GROUND, upperSky, smoothstep(-0.05, 0.05, dir.y));
}

float interleavedGradientNoise(vec2 p)
{
    const vec3 magic = vec3(0.06711056, 0.00583715, 52.9829189);
    return fract(magic.z * fract(dot(p, magic.xy)));
}

float calculateShadow(vec4 lightSpacePos, vec3 N, vec3 L, vec2 screenXY)
{
    vec3 proj = lightSpacePos.xyz / lightSpacePos.w;
    proj.xy = proj.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;

    if (proj.x < 0.0 || proj.x > 1.0 ||
        proj.y < 0.0 || proj.y > 1.0 ||
        proj.z < 0.0 || proj.z > 1.0)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    float bias = max(0.0025 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.0006);
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(shadowMap, 0));

    float angle = interleavedGradientNoise(screenXY) * 6.2831853;
    float s = sin(angle), c = cos(angle);
    mat2 rot = mat2(c, -s, s, c);

    float shadow = 0.0;
    const int R = 1;
    for (int x = -R; x <= R; x++)
    {
        for (int y = -R; y <= R; y++)
        {
            vec2 offset = rot * (vec2(x, y) * texelSize);
            shadow += texture(shadowMap, vec3(proj.xy + offset, proj.z - bias));
        }
    }
    float samples = float((2 * R + 1) * (2 * R + 1));
    return shadow / samples;
}

vec3 sampleCubeFaceAndUV(vec3 dir)
{
    vec3 absDir = abs(dir);
    float faceIndex;
    vec2 uv;

    if (absDir.x >= absDir.y && absDir.x >= absDir.z)
    {
        faceIndex = dir.x > 0.0 ? 0.0 : 1.0;
        uv = (dir.x > 0.0) ? vec2(-dir.z, -dir.y) / absDir.x : vec2(dir.z, -dir.y) / absDir.x;
    }
    else if (absDir.y >= absDir.x && absDir.y >= absDir.z)
    {
        faceIndex = dir.y > 0.0 ? 2.0 : 3.0;
        uv = (dir.y > 0.0) ? vec2(dir.x, dir.z) / absDir.y : vec2(dir.x, -dir.z) / absDir.y;
    }
    else
    {
        faceIndex = dir.z > 0.0 ? 4.0 : 5.0;
        uv = (dir.z > 0.0) ? vec2(dir.x, -dir.y) / absDir.z : vec2(-dir.x, -dir.y) / absDir.z;
    }

    uv = uv * 0.5 + 0.5;
    return vec3(uv, faceIndex);
}

float sampleShadowAtlas(vec3 dir, int lightSlot)
{
    vec3 faceUV = sampleCubeFaceAndUV(dir);

    const float TILE = 1.0 / 6.0;
    const float ROW = 1.0 / 3.0;

    vec2 atlasUV;
    atlasUV.x = (faceUV.z + faceUV.x) * TILE;
    atlasUV.y = (float(lightSlot) + faceUV.y) * ROW;

    return texture(shadowAtlas, atlasUV).r;
}

float calculatePointShadow(vec3 fragPos, vec3 lightPos, vec3 N, vec2 screenXY, int shadowSlot)
{
    vec3 toFrag = fragPos - lightPos;
    float currentDist = length(toFrag);
    vec3 L = normalize(-toFrag);

    float bias = max(0.15 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.03);

    const int SAMPLES = 20;
    vec3 sampleOffsets[SAMPLES] = vec3[]
    (
        vec3( 1,  1,  1), vec3( 1, -1,  1), vec3(-1, -1,  1), vec3(-1,  1,  1),
        vec3( 1,  1, -1), vec3( 1, -1, -1), vec3(-1, -1, -1), vec3(-1,  1, -1),
        vec3( 1,  1,  0), vec3( 1, -1,  0), vec3(-1, -1,  0), vec3(-1,  1,  0),
        vec3( 1,  0,  1), vec3(-1,  0,  1), vec3( 1,  0, -1), vec3(-1,  0, -1),
        vec3( 0,  1,  1), vec3( 0, -1,  1), vec3( 0, -1, -1), vec3( 0,  1, -1)
    );

    float angle = interleavedGradientNoise(screenXY) * 6.2831853;
    float s = sin(angle), c = cos(angle);
    vec3 axis = L;

    mat3 rot = mat3(
        c + axis.x*axis.x*(1.0-c),        axis.x*axis.y*(1.0-c) - axis.z*s, axis.x*axis.z*(1.0-c) + axis.y*s,
        axis.y*axis.x*(1.0-c) + axis.z*s, c + axis.y*axis.y*(1.0-c),        axis.y*axis.z*(1.0-c) - axis.x*s,
        axis.z*axis.x*(1.0-c) - axis.y*s, axis.z*axis.y*(1.0-c) + axis.x*s, c + axis.z*axis.z*(1.0-c)
    );

    float shadow = 0.0;
    float diskRadius = (1.0 + (currentDist / POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE)) / 15.0;

    for (int i = 0; i < SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        vec3 offset = rot * sampleOffsets[i];
        float closestDist = sampleShadowAtlas(toFrag + offset * diskRadius, shadowSlot) * POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE;

        if (currentDist - bias > closestDist)
            shadow += 1.0;
    }

    return shadow / float(SAMPLES);
}

void main()
{
    vec3 albedo = pow(texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).rgb, vec3(2.2));
    vec3 mr = texture(metallicRoughnessMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    float roughness = clamp(mr.g, 0.4, 1.0);
    float metallic = mr.b;
    float ao = texture(aoMap, fragTexCoord).r;

    vec3 N = texture(normalMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    N = N * 2.0 - 1.0;
    N = normalize(fragTBN * N);

    vec3 V = normalize(cam.camPos.xyz - fragWorldPos);
    vec3 R = reflect(-V, N);
    vec3 F0 = mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metallic);

    vec3 Lo = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < lightData.count.x; i++)
    {
        Light light = lightData.lights[i];
        vec3 L, radiance;

        if (light.position.w < 0.5) {
            L = normalize(-light.position.xyz);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a;
        }
        else
        {
            vec3 toLight = light.position.xyz - fragWorldPos;
            float dist2 = max(dot(toLight, toLight), 0.01);
            L = normalize(toLight);
            float atten = 1.0 / dist2;
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a * atten;

            int shadowSlot = -1;
            if (i == lightData.count.y) shadowSlot = 0;
            else if (i == lightData.count.z) shadowSlot = 1;
            else if (i == lightData.count.w) shadowSlot = 2;

            if (shadowSlot >= 0)
            {
                float pShadow = calculatePointShadow(fragWorldPos, light.position.xyz, N, gl_FragCoord.xy, shadowSlot);
                radiance *= (1.0 - pShadow);
            }
        }

        float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
        float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0);

        if (NdotL > 0.0)
        {
            vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
            float D = DistributionGGX(N, H, roughness);
            float G = GeometrySmith(N, V, L, roughness);
            vec3 F = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(H, V), 0.0), F0);

            vec3 numerator = D * G * F;
            float denominator = 4.0 * NdotV * NdotL + 0.0001;
            vec3 specular = numerator / denominator;
            specular = min(specular, vec3(8.0));

            vec3 kD = (1.0 - F) * (1.0 - metallic);

            float visibility = 1.0;
            if (i == 0 && light.position.w < 0.5) {
                vec3 geoN = normalize(fragTBN[2]);
                visibility = calculateShadow(fragLightSpacePos, geoN, L, gl_FragCoord.xy);
            }

            Lo += (kD * albedo / PI + specular) * radiance * NdotL * visibility;
        }
    }

    vec3 ambient = vec3(0.03) * albedo * ao;
    vec3 color = ambient + Lo;

    float alpha = texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).a;
    outColor = vec4(color, alpha);

    vec2 currNDC = fragCurrClipPos.xy / fragCurrClipPos.w;
    vec2 prevNDC = fragPrevClipPos.xy / fragPrevClipPos.w;
    outVelocity = (currNDC - prevNDC) * 0.5;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/pbr/skinned.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 prevViewProj;
    mat4 viewProjNoJitter;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

layout(binding = 9) uniform BoneMatrices {
    mat4 bones[100];
} boneData;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 model;
    mat4 normalMatrix;
    mat4 prevModel;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 inNormal;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 inTexCoord;
layout(location = 3) in vec3 inTangent;
layout(location = 4) in vec4 inBoneIndices;
layout(location = 5) in vec4 inBoneWeights;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 fragTexCoord;
layout(location = 2) out vec4 fragLightSpacePos;
layout(location = 3) out mat3 fragTBN;

layout(location = 6) out vec4 fragCurrClipPos;
layout(location = 7) out vec4 fragPrevClipPos;
layout(location = 8) out vec2 fragLightmapUV;

void main()
{
    ivec4 boneIdx = ivec4(inBoneIndices);

    mat4 skinMatrix =
        inBoneWeights.x * boneData.bones[boneIdx.x] +
        inBoneWeights.y * boneData.bones[boneIdx.y] +
        inBoneWeights.z * boneData.bones[boneIdx.z] +
        inBoneWeights.w * boneData.bones[boneIdx.w];

    vec4 skinnedLocalPos = skinMatrix * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);

    vec4 worldPos = push.model * skinnedLocalPos;
    gl_Position = cam.proj * cam.view * worldPos;

    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    fragTexCoord = inTexCoord;
    fragLightSpacePos = cam.lightSpaceMatrix * worldPos;
    fragCurrClipPos = cam.viewProjNoJitter * worldPos;

    vec4 prevWorldPos = push.prevModel * skinnedLocalPos;
    fragPrevClipPos = cam.prevViewProj * prevWorldPos;

    fragLightmapUV = vec2(0.0);

    mat3 skinNormalMat = mat3(skinMatrix);
    mat3 nm = mat3(push.normalMatrix);

    vec3 N = normalize(nm * (skinNormalMat * inNormal));
    vec3 T = normalize(nm * (skinNormalMat * inTangent));
    T = normalize(T - dot(T, N) * N);
    vec3 B = cross(N, T);
    fragTBN = mat3(T, B, N);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/blur.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D image;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    vec2 direction;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(image, 0));
    vec3 result = vec3(0.0);

    float weights[5] = float[](0.227027, 0.1945946, 0.1216216, 0.054054, 0.016216);

    result += texture(image, fragUV).rgb * weights[0];

    for (int i = 1; i < 5; i++)
    {
        vec2 offset = push.direction * texelSize * float(i);
        result += texture(image, fragUV + offset).rgb * weights[i];
        result += texture(image, fragUV - offset).rgb * weights[i];
    }

    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);    
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/brightpass.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D hdrBuffer;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    float threshold;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec3 color = texture(hdrBuffer, fragUV).rgb;
    float brightness = dot(color, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));

    float knee = push.threshold * 0.5 + 1e-4;
    float soft = clamp(brightness - push.threshold + knee, 0.0, 2.0 * knee);
    soft = soft * soft / (4.0 * knee);

    float contribution = max(soft, brightness - push.threshold);
    contribution /= max(brightness, 1e-4);

    outColor = vec4(color * contribution, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/downsample.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D srcTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    vec2 scrTexelSize;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 uv = fragUV;
    vec2 texel = push.scrTexelSize;

    vec3 a = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 b = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 c = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 d = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-0.5, -0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 e = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.5, -0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 f = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-1.0,  0.0)).rgb;
    vec3 g = texture(srcTexture, uv).rgb;
    vec3 h = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 1.0,  0.0)).rgb;
    vec3 i = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-0.5,  0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 j = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.5,  0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 k = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-1.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 l = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 m = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 1.0,  1.0)).rgb;

    vec3 result = (d + e + i + j) * 0.125;
    result += (a + b + f + g) * 0.03125;
    result += (b + c + g + h) * 0.03125;
    result += (f + g + k + l) * 0.03125;
    result += (g + h + l + m) * 0.03125;

    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);    
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/fog.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D depthTexture;

layout(binding = 1) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 invViewProj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2DShadow shadowMap;

struct Light { vec4 position; vec4 color; };
layout(binding = 3) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    vec4 scatteringColorDensity;
    vec4 heightParams;
    vec4 lightParams;
    vec4 miscParams;
} fog;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

float hash(vec3 p)
{
    p = fract(p * 0.3183099 + vec3(0.1, 0.2, 0.3));
    p *= 17.0;
    return fract(p.x * p.y * p.z * (p.x + p.y + p.z));
}

float sampleSunShadow(vec3 worldPos)
{
    vec4 lsPos = cam.lightSpaceMatrix * vec4(worldPos, 1.0);
    vec3 proj = lsPos.xyz / lsPos.w;
    proj.xy = proj.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;

    if (proj.x < 0.0 || proj.x > 1.0 || proj.y < 0.0 || proj.y > 1.0 || proj.z < 0.0 || proj.z > 1.0)
        return 1.0;

    return texture(shadowMap, vec3(proj.xy, proj.z - 0.001));
}

float henyeyGreenstein(float cosAngle, float g)
{
    float g2 = g * g;
    return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * 3.14159265 * pow(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * cosAngle, 1.5));
}

void main()
{
    vec3 scatteringColor = fog.scatteringColorDensity.rgb;
    float density        = fog.scatteringColorDensity.a;
    float heightFalloff   = fog.heightParams.x;
    float heightStart     = fog.heightParams.y;
    float maxDistance     = fog.heightParams.z;
    int   stepCount       = int(fog.heightParams.w);
    float ambientIntensity = fog.lightParams.x;
    float sunIntensity     = fog.lightParams.y;
    float noiseScale       = fog.lightParams.z;
    float noiseSpeed       = fog.lightParams.w;
    float time              = fog.miscParams.x;

    float sceneDepth = texture(depthTexture, fragUV).r;

    if (sceneDepth >= 0.9999)
    {
        outColor = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        return;
    }
    
    vec4 ndc = vec4(fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0, sceneDepth, 1.0);
    vec4 worldPosH = cam.invViewProj * ndc;
    vec3 scenePos = worldPosH.xyz / worldPosH.w;

    vec3 camPos = cam.camPos.xyz;
    vec3 toScene = scenePos - camPos;
    float sceneDist = length(toScene);
    vec3 rayDir = toScene / max(sceneDist, 0.0001);

    float rayLength = min(sceneDist, maxDistance);
    float dt = rayLength / float(stepCount);

    vec3 sunColor = vec3(0.0);
    float sunPower = 0.0;
    if (lightData.count.x > 0 && lightData.lights[0].position.w < 0.5)
    {
        sunColor = lightData.lights[0].color.rgb;
        sunPower = lightData.lights[0].color.a;
    }

    vec3 pos = camPos;
    float transmittance = 1.0;
    vec3 inscatter = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < stepCount; i++)
    {
        pos += rayDir * dt;

        float heightAtten = exp(-max(pos.y - heightStart, 0.0) * heightFalloff);
        float n = hash(pos * noiseScale + vec3(time * noiseSpeed, 0.0, 0.0));
        float localDensity = density * heightAtten * mix(0.6, 1.4, n);

        float stepTransmittance = exp(-localDensity * dt);

        float visibility = sampleSunShadow(pos);
        vec3 sunDir = vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
        if (lightData.count.x > 0 && lightData.lights[0].position.w < 0.5)
            sunDir = normalize(-lightData.lights[0].position.xyz);

        float cosAngle = dot(rayDir, sunDir);
        float phase = henyeyGreenstein(cosAngle, 0.6);
        vec3 sunContribution = sunColor * sunPower * visibility * sunIntensity * phase;
        vec3 ambientContribution = scatteringColor * ambientIntensity;

        vec3 stepInscatter = (sunContribution + ambientContribution) * scatteringColor * localDensity * dt;

        inscatter += stepInscatter * transmittance;
        transmittance *= stepTransmittance;
    }

    float dither = (hash(vec3(gl_FragCoord.xy, 0.0)) - 0.5) / 255.0;
    outColor = vec4(inscatter + dither, 1.0 - transmittance);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/fog.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) out vec2 fragUV;

void main()
{
    fragUV = vec2((gl_VertexIndex << 1) & 2, gl_VertexIndex & 2);
    gl_Position = vec4(fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/fullscreen.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) out vec2 fragUV;

void main()
{
    fragUV = vec2((gl_VertexIndex << 1) & 2, gl_VertexIndex & 2);
    gl_Position = vec4(fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/fxaa.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D srcTexture;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

// FXAA Quality Settings
#define FXAA_SPAN_MAX 8.0
#define FXAA_REDUCE_MUL 1.0/8.0
#define FXAA_REDUCE_MIN 1.0/128.0
#define FXAA_SUBPIX_QUALITY 0.75

float luma(vec3 color)
{
    return dot(color, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
}

void main()
{
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(srcTexture, 0));
    
    vec3 rgbNW = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2(-1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbNE = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2( 1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbSW = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2(-1.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbSE = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2( 1.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbM  = texture(srcTexture, fragUV).rgb;
    
    float lumaNW = luma(rgbNW);
    float lumaNE = luma(rgbNE);
    float lumaSW = luma(rgbSW);
    float lumaSE = luma(rgbSE);
    float lumaM  = luma(rgbM);
    
    float lumaMin = min(lumaM, min(min(lumaNW, lumaNE), min(lumaSW, lumaSE)));
    float lumaMax = max(lumaM, max(max(lumaNW, lumaNE), max(lumaSW, lumaSE)));
    
    vec2 dir;
    dir.x = -((lumaNW + lumaNE) - (lumaSW + lumaSE));
    dir.y =  ((lumaNW + lumaSW) - (lumaNE + lumaSE));
    
    float dirReduce = max((lumaNW + lumaNE + lumaSW + lumaSE) * (0.25 * FXAA_REDUCE_MUL), FXAA_REDUCE_MIN);
    float rcpDirMin = 1.0 / (min(abs(dir.x), abs(dir.y)) + dirReduce);
    dir = min(vec2(FXAA_SPAN_MAX), max(vec2(-FXAA_SPAN_MAX), dir * rcpDirMin)) * texelSize;
    
    vec3 rgbA = 0.5 * (
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir * (1.0/3.0 - 0.5)).rgb +
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir * (2.0/3.0 - 0.5)).rgb
    );
    vec3 rgbB = rgbA * 0.5 + 0.25 * (
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir * -0.5).rgb +
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir *  0.5).rgb
    );
    
    float lumaB = luma(rgbB);
    
    if (lumaB < lumaMin || lumaB > lumaMax) {
        outColor = vec4(rgbA, 1.0);
    } else {
        outColor = vec4(rgbB, 1.0);
    }
    
    float lumaAverage = (1.0/12.0) * (2.0 * (lumaNW + lumaNE + lumaSW + lumaSE) + lumaM);
    float subpix1 = lumaAverage - lumaM;
    float subpix2 = subpix1 * subpix1;
    float subpix3 = subpix2 * subpix2;
    float subpix = clamp((-subpix3) * 8.0 + FXAA_SUBPIX_QUALITY, 0.0, 1.0);
    
    vec3 finalColor = mix(outColor.rgb, rgbM, subpix);
    outColor = vec4(finalColor, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/godray_blur.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D maskTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    vec4 sunUVParams;
    vec4 weightCountParams;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 sunUV = push.sunUVParams.xy;
    float density = push.sunUVParams.z;
    float decay = push.sunUVParams.w;
    float weight = push.weightCountParams.x;
    int sampleCount = int(push.weightCountParams.y);

    vec2 deltaUV = (fragUV - sunUV) * density / float(sampleCount);

    vec2 uv = fragUV;
    float illuminationDecay = 1.0;
    vec3 result = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < sampleCount; i++)
    {
        uv -= deltaUV;
        vec3 sampleColor = texture(maskTexture, uv).rgb;
        sampleColor *= illuminationDecay * weight;
        result += sampleColor;
        illuminationDecay *= decay;
    }

    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);
}

// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/godray_composite.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D godrayTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    float intensity;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    outColor = vec4(texture(godrayTexture, fragUV).rgb * push.intensity, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/godray_mask.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D depthTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 invViewProj;
    vec4 sunDirIntensity;
    vec4 sunColorSize;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    if (push.sunDirIntensity.w < 0.5)
    {
        outColor = vec4(0.0);
        return;
    }

    float sceneDepth = texture(depthTexture, fragUV).r;

    if (sceneDepth < 0.9999)
    {
        outColor = vec4(0.0);
        return;
    }

    vec2 ndcXY = fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0;
    vec4 nearH = push.invViewProj * vec4(ndcXY, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec4 farH  = push.invViewProj * vec4(ndcXY, 1.0, 1.0);
    vec3 dir = normalize(farH.xyz / farH.w - nearH.xyz / nearH.w);

    float cosAngle = dot(dir, push.sunDirIntensity.xyz);
    float sunSize = push.sunColorSize.a;
    float sunDisk = smoothstep(sunSize, sunSize + 0.0005, cosAngle);

    outColor = vec4(push.sunColorSize.rgb * sunDisk, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/taa.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D currentColor;
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D velocityBuffer;
layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2D historyColor;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    float blendFactor;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

vec3 rgbToYCoCg(vec3 c)
{
    float y  = dot(c, vec3(0.25, 0.5, 0.25));
    float co = dot(c, vec3(0.5, 0.0, -0.5));
    float cg = dot(c, vec3(-0.25, 0.5, -0.25));
    return vec3(y, co, cg);
}

vec3 yCoCgToRgb(vec3 c)
{
    float y = c.x, co = c.y, cg = c.z;
    return vec3(y + co - cg, y + cg, y - co - cg);
}

void main()
{
    vec3 current = texture(currentColor, fragUV).rgb;

    vec2 velocity = texture(velocityBuffer, fragUV).rg;
    vec2 prevUV = fragUV - velocity;

    if (prevUV.x < 0.0 || prevUV.x > 1.0 || prevUV.y < 0.0 || prevUV.y > 1.0)
    {
        outColor = vec4(current, 1.0);
        return;
    }

    vec3 history = texture(historyColor, prevUV).rgb;

    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(currentColor, 0));

    vec3 neighborhoodMin = vec3(1e6);
    vec3 neighborhoodMax = vec3(-1e6);

    for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++)
    {
        for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++)
        {
            vec3 sampleColor = texture(currentColor, fragUV + vec2(x, y) * texelSize).rgb;
            vec3 sampleYCoCg = rgbToYCoCg(sampleColor);
            neighborhoodMin = min(neighborhoodMin, sampleYCoCg);
            neighborhoodMax = max(neighborhoodMax, sampleYCoCg);
        }
    }

    vec3 historyYCoCg = rgbToYCoCg(history);
    vec3 clampedYCoCg = clamp(historyYCoCg, neighborhoodMin, neighborhoodMax);
    vec3 clampedHistory = yCoCgToRgb(clampedYCoCg);

    vec3 result = mix(current, clampedHistory, push.blendFactor);
    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/tonemap.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

#include "tonemap.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D hdrBuffer;
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D bloomBuffer;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    float exposure;
    float contrast;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec3 hdrColor = texture(hdrBuffer, fragUV).rgb;
    vec3 bloomColor = texture(bloomBuffer, fragUV).rgb;

    vec3 combined = hdrColor + bloomColor * 0.65;
    combined *= push.exposure;

    vec3 mapped = tonemapACESSoft(combined, push.contrast);
    outColor = vec4(mapped, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/post/upsample.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D srcTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    float radius;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 texel = push.radius / vec2(textureSize(srcTexture, 0));

    vec3 result = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2(-1.0, -1.0)).rgb * 1.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 0.0, -1.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 1.0, -1.0)).rgb * 1.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2(-1.0,  0.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 0.0,  0.0)).rgb * 4.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 1.0,  0.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2(-1.0,  1.0)).rgb * 1.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 0.0,  1.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 1.0,  1.0)).rgb * 1.0;

    outColor = vec4(result / 16.0, 1.0);
}

    mat4 model;

} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragWorldPos;

void main()
{

    gl_FragDepth = dist / push.lightPos.w;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/shadow/skinned_point_shadow.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(binding = 9) uniform BoneMatrices {
    mat4 bones[100];
} boneData;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 lightViewProj;
    mat4 model;
    vec4 lightPos;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 inNormal;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 inTexCoord;
layout(location = 3) in vec3 inTangent;
layout(location = 4) in vec4 inBoneIndices;
layout(location = 5) in vec4 inBoneWeights;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;

void main()
{
    ivec4 boneIdx = ivec4(inBoneIndices);

    mat4 skinMatrix =
        inBoneWeights.x * boneData.bones[boneIdx.x] +
        inBoneWeights.y * boneData.bones[boneIdx.y] +
        inBoneWeights.z * boneData.bones[boneIdx.z] +
        inBoneWeights.w * boneData.bones[boneIdx.w];

    vec4 skinnedLocalPos = skinMatrix * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * skinnedLocalPos;
    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    gl_Position = push.lightViewProj * worldPos;
}

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;

layout(location = 3) in vec3 inTangent;
layout(location = 4) in vec4 inBoneIndices;
layout(location = 5) in vec4 inBoneWeights;

out gl_PerVertex { vec4 gl_Position; };

//void main() {

//    gl_Position.z = clamp(gl_Position.z, 0.0, gl_Position.w);

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/voxel/voxelize.frag
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 fragNormal;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 fragTexCoord;

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D albedoMap;
layout(binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image3D voxelTexture;

struct Light
{
    vec4 position;
    vec4 color;
};

layout(binding = 2) uniform LightUBO
{
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(binding = 3) uniform sampler2DShadow shadowMap;
layout(binding = 4) uniform sampler2D shadowAtlas;

layout(binding = 5) uniform ShadowData
{
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
} shadowData;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 model;
    vec4 voxelGridMin;
    vec4 voxelGridSize;
    ivec4 atlasOffset;
} push;

float calculateShadow(vec3 worldPos, vec3 N, vec3 L)
{
    vec4 lightSpacePos = shadowData.lightSpaceMatrix * vec4(worldPos, 1.0);
    vec3 proj = lightSpacePos.xyz / lightSpacePos.w;
    proj.xy = proj.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;

    if (proj.x < 0.0 || proj.x > 1.0 || proj.y < 0.0 || proj.y > 1.0 || proj.z < 0.0 || proj.z > 1.0)
        return 1.0;

    float bias = max(0.003 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.001);
    return texture(shadowMap, vec3(proj.xy, proj.z - bias));
}

void main()
{
    vec3 normalizedPos = (fragWorldPos - push.voxelGridMin.xyz) / push.voxelGridSize.xyz;
    int chunkResolution = push.atlasOffset.w;
    ivec3 localVoxelCoord = ivec3(normalizedPos * float(chunkResolution));

    if (any(lessThan(localVoxelCoord, ivec3(0))) || any(greaterThanEqual(localVoxelCoord, ivec3(chunkResolution))))
        return;

    ivec3 voxelCoord = push.atlasOffset.xyz + localVoxelCoord;

    vec3 albedo = texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    vec3 N = normalize(fragNormal);

    vec3 result = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < lightData.count.x; i++)
    {
        Light light = lightData.lights[i];
        vec3 L;
        vec3 radiance;

        if (light.position.w < 0.5)
        {
            L = normalize(-light.position.xyz);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a;
        }
        else
        {
            vec3 toLight = light.position.xyz - fragWorldPos;
            float dist2 = max(dot(toLight, toLight), 0.01);
            L = normalize(toLight);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a / dist2;
        }

        float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
        if (NdotL <= 0.0) continue;

        float visibility = 1.0;
        if (i == 0 && light.position.w < 0.5)
            visibility = calculateShadow(fragWorldPos, N, L);

        result += albedo * radiance * NdotL * visibility;
    }

    float maxBrightness = 4.0;
    float luminance = dot(result, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
    if (luminance > maxBrightness)
        result *= maxBrightness / luminance;

    imageStore(voxelTexture, voxelCoord, vec4(result, 1.0));
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/voxel/voxelize_skinned.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 inNormal;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 inTexCoord;
layout(location = 3) in vec3 inTangent;
layout(location = 4) in vec4 inBoneIndices;
layout(location = 5) in vec4 inBoneWeights;

layout(binding = 6) uniform BoneMatrices
{
    mat4 bones[100];
} boneData;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    mat4 model;
    vec4 voxelGridMin;
    vec4 voxelGridSize;
} push;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) out vec3 fragNormal;
layout(location = 2) out vec2 fragTexCoord;

void main()
{
    ivec4 boneIdx = ivec4(inBoneIndices);

    mat4 skinMatrix =
        inBoneWeights.x * boneData.bones[boneIdx.x] +
        inBoneWeights.y * boneData.bones[boneIdx.y] +
        inBoneWeights.z * boneData.bones[boneIdx.z] +
        inBoneWeights.w * boneData.bones[boneIdx.w];

    vec4 skinnedLocalPos = skinMatrix * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * skinnedLocalPos;

    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    fragNormal = mat3(push.model) * mat3(skinMatrix) * inNormal;
    fragTexCoord = inTexCoord;

    vec3 normalizedPos = (worldPos.xyz - push.voxelGridMin.xyz) / push.voxelGridSize.xyz;
    gl_Position = vec4(normalizedPos.xy * 2.0 - 1.0, normalizedPos.z, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/shaders/voxel/voxelize.vert
// ================================================================

#version 450

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 inNormal;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 inTexCoord;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 model;
    vec4 voxelGridMin;
    vec4 voxelGridSize;
} push;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) out vec3 fragNormal;
layout(location = 2) out vec2 fragTexCoord;

void main()
{
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    fragNormal = mat3(push.model) * inNormal;
    fragTexCoord = inTexCoord;

    vec3 normalizedPos = (worldPos.xyz - push.voxelGridMin.xyz) / push.voxelGridSize.xyz;
    gl_Position = vec4(normalizedPos.xy * 2.0 - 1.0, normalizedPos.z, 1.0);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/AI/AIController.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "AI/AIController.h"
#include "AI/ICommand.h"

void AIController::updateVision()
{
    m_canSeeTarget = false;

    if (!m_visionTarget)
    {
        LOG("updateVision: no vision target set!");
        return;
    }

    glm::vec3 eyePos = m_owner->transform.position + m_eyeOffset;
    glm::vec3 targetPos = m_visionTarget->transform.position;

    glm::vec3 toTarget = targetPos - eyePos;
    float distance = glm::length(toTarget);

    if (distance > m_visionRange)
    {
        LOG("updateVision: target too far, distance=" << distance << " range=" << m_visionRange);
        return;
    }

    glm::vec3 direction = toTarget / distance;

    float yawRad = glm::radians(m_owner->transform.rotation.y);
    glm::vec3 forward(sin(yawRad), 0.0f, -cos(yawRad));

    float facingDot = glm::dot(glm::normalize(glm::vec3(direction.x, 0.0f, direction.z)), forward);
    if (facingDot < m_visionFOVCos)
    {
        LOG("updateVision: target outside FOV, dot=" << facingDot << " required=" << m_visionFOVCos);
        return;
    }

    RaycastHit hit = m_physics.raycast(eyePos, direction, distance);
    bool blocked = hit.hit && hit.distance < distance - 0.2f;

    LOG("updateVision: distance=" << distance << " facingDot=" << facingDot << " blocked=" << blocked);

    if (!blocked)
    {
        m_canSeeTarget = true;
        m_lastKnowTargetPos = targetPos;
    }
}


void AIController::pushCommand(std::unique_ptr<ICommand> command)
{
    m_commandQueue.push_back(std::move(command));
}

void AIController::clearCommands()
{
    m_commandQueue.clear();
}

void AIController::updateCommands(float deltaTime)
{
    if (m_commandQueue.empty()) return;

    ICommand* current = m_commandQueue.front().get();
    bool finished = current->execute(*this, deltaTime);

    if (finished)
        m_commandQueue.erase(m_commandQueue.begin());
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Audio/AudioSystem.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Audio/AudioSystem.h"
#include "Core/Log.h"
#include "Core/Path.h"

void AudioSystem::initialize()
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    ma_backend backends[] = { ma_backend_wasapi, ma_backend_dsound, ma_backend_winmm };
#else
    ma_backend backends[] = { ma_backend_pulseaudio, ma_backend_alsa };
#endif

    ma_result ctxResult = ma_context_init(backends, 2, nullptr, &m_context);
    LOG("ma_context_init result=" << (int)ctxResult);
    if (ctxResult == MA_SUCCESS)
        LOG("selected backend=" << (int)m_context.backend);

    ma_engine_config engineConfig = ma_engine_config_init();
    engineConfig.pContext = &m_context;

    ma_result result = ma_engine_init(&engineConfig, &m_engine);
    if (result != MA_SUCCESS)
    {
        LOG("ma_engine_init FAILED, result=" << (int)result);
        throw std::runtime_error("failed to initialize miniaudio engine");
    }
    LOG("ma_engine_init succeeded");

    ma_device* device = ma_engine_get_device(&m_engine);
    if (device)
        LOG("audio device name: " << device->playback.name);
}

void AudioSystem::cleanup()
{
    m_sounds.clear();
    ma_engine_uninit(&m_engine);
    ma_context_uninit(&m_context);
}

bool AudioSystem::loadSound(const std::string& name, const std::string& path)
{
    if (m_sounds.find(name) != m_sounds.end())
        return true;
    
    std::string fullPath = (Paths::assetDir() / "assets" / path).string();

    auto sound = std::make_unique<ma_sound>();
    ma_result result = ma_sound_init_from_file(&m_engine, fullPath.c_str(), 0, nullptr, nullptr, sound.get());

    if (result != MA_SUCCESS)
    {
        LOG("failed to load sound: " << fullPath);
        return false;
    }

    m_sounds[name] = std::move(sound);
    return true;
}

void AudioSystem::playSound(const std::string& name, float volume)
{
    auto it = m_sounds.find(name);
    if (it == m_sounds.end()) return;

    ma_sound_set_volume(it->second.get(), volume);
    ma_sound_seek_to_pcm_frame(it->second.get(), 0);
    ma_sound_start(it->second.get());
}

void AudioSystem::playSound3D(const std::string& name, glm::vec3 position, float volume)
{
    auto it = m_sounds.find(name);
    if (it == m_sounds.end()) return;

    ma_sound_set_position(it->second.get(), position.x, position.y, position.z);
    ma_sound_set_volume(it->second.get(), volume);
    ma_sound_seek_to_pcm_frame(it->second.get(), 0);
    ma_sound_start(it->second.get());
}

void AudioSystem::updateSoundPosition(const std::string& name, glm::vec3 position)
{
    auto it = m_sounds.find(name);
    if (it == m_sounds.end()) return;
    ma_sound_set_position(it->second.get(), position.x, position.y, position.z);
}

void AudioSystem::stopSound(const std::string& name)
{
    auto it = m_sounds.find(name);
    if (it == m_sounds.end()) return;
    ma_sound_stop(it->second.get());
}

void AudioSystem::setOcclusion(const std::string& name, float occlusionFactor)
{
    auto it = m_sounds.find(name);
    if (it == m_sounds.end()) return;

    float volumeMultiplier = 1.0f - occlusionFactor * 0.7f;
    ma_sound_set_volume(it->second.get(), volumeMultiplier);

    if (occlusionFactor > 0.01f)
    {
        double cutoffFrequency = 8000.0 - occlusionFactor * 6500.0;
        ma_lpf_node_config lpfConfig = ma_lpf_node_config_init(1, m_engine.sampleRate, cutoffFrequency, 2);
    }
}

void AudioSystem::setListenerPosition(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 forward, glm::vec3 up)
{
    ma_engine_listener_set_position(&m_engine, 0, position.x, position.y, position.z);
    ma_engine_listener_set_direction(&m_engine, 0, forward.x, forward.y, forward.z);
    ma_engine_listener_set_world_up(&m_engine, 0, up.x, up.y, up.z);
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Core/AnimationLoader.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Core/AnimationLoader.h"
#include "Core/Path.h"
#include <fstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cstring>

namespace
{
    template<typename T>
    T readValue(const uint8_t* data, size_t& offset)
    {
        T value{};
        std::memcpy(&value, data + offset, sizeof(T));
        offset += sizeof(T);
        return value;
    }

    std::string reeadString(const uint8_t* data, size_t& offset)
    {
        uint8_t length = readValue<uint32_t>(data, offset);
        std::string result(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(data + offset), length);
        offset += length;
        return result;
    }
}

Animation AnimationLoader::load(const std::string& path)
{
    std::string fullPath = (Paths::assetDir() / path).string();

    std::ifstream file(fullPath, std::ios::binary | std::ios::ate);
    if (!file.is_open())
        throw std::runtime_error("failed to open animation file: " + fullPath);

    size_t fileSize = (size_t)file.tellg();
    file.seekg(0);

    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(fileSize);
    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(buffer.data()), fileSize);
    file.close();

    const uint8_t* data = buffer.data();
    size_t offset = 0;

    char magic[5] = {0};
    std::memcpy(magic, data, 4);
    offset += 4;
    if (std::strcmp(magic, "EANM") != 0)
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid animation file magic: " + fullPath);

    uint32_t version = readValue<uint32_t>(data, offset);
    (void)version;

    Animation anim;
    anim.name = reeadString(data, offset);
    anim.duration = readValue<float>(data, offset);

    uint32_t channelCount = readValue<uint32_t>(data, offset);
    anim.channels.resize(channelCount);

    for (auto& channel : anim.channels)
    {
        channel.jointName = reeadString(data, offset);

        uint32_t posCount = readValue<uint32_t>(data, offset);
        channel.poisitonKeys.resize(posCount);
        for (auto& key : channel.poisitonKeys)
        {
            key.time = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            key.value = readValue<glm::vec3>(data, offset);
        }

        uint32_t rotCount = readValue<uint32_t>(data, offset);
        channel.rotationKeys.resize(rotCount);
        for (auto& key : channel.rotationKeys)
        {
            key.time = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            float x = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            float y = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            float z = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            float w = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            key.value = glm::quat(w, x, y, z);
        }

        uint32_t scaleCount = readValue<uint32_t>(data, offset);
        channel.scaleKeys.resize(scaleCount);
        for (auto& key : channel.scaleKeys)
        {
            key.time = readValue<float>(data, offset);
            key.value = readValue<glm::vec3>(data, offset);
        }
    }

    return anim;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Core/Camera.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Core/Camera.h"
#define GLM_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL
#include <glm/gtx/rotate_vector.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>
#include <algorithm>

void Camera::initialize(glm::vec3 position)
{
    m_position = position;
    updateVectors();
}

void Camera::processKeyboard(glm::vec3 direction, float deltaTime)
{
    float velocity = m_moveSpeed * deltaTime;
    m_position += direction * velocity;
}

void Camera::lookAt(glm::vec3 target)
{
    m_front = glm::normalize(target - m_position);
    m_yaw = glm::degrees(atan2(m_front.z ,m_front.x));
    m_pitch = glm::degrees(asin(m_front.y));
    updateVectors();
}

void Camera::processMouse(float deltaX, float deltaY)
{
    m_yaw += deltaX * m_mouseSensitivity;
    m_pitch += deltaY * m_mouseSensitivity;

    m_pitch = std::clamp(m_pitch, -89.0f, 89.0f);

    updateVectors();
}

glm::mat4 Camera::getViewMatrix() const
{
    glm::vec3 rolledUp = glm::rotate(m_up, glm::radians(m_roll), m_front);
    return glm::lookAt(m_position, m_position + m_front, rolledUp);
}

void Camera::updateVectors()
{
    glm::vec3 front;
    front.x = cos(glm::radians(m_yaw)) * cos(glm::radians(m_pitch));
    front.y = sin(glm::radians(m_pitch));
    front.z = sin(glm::radians(m_yaw)) * cos(glm::radians(m_pitch));

    m_front = glm::normalize(front);
    m_right = glm::normalize(glm::cross(m_front, m_worldUp));
    m_up    = glm::normalize(glm::cross(m_right, m_front));    
}

glm::mat4 Camera::getProjectionMatrix() const
{
    glm::mat4 proj = glm::perspective(
        glm::radians(m_fov),
        m_aspect,
        m_nearPlane,
        m_farPlane
    );
    proj[1][1] *= -1; 
    return proj;
}
