        viewport.minDepth = 0.0f; viewport.maxDepth = 1.0f;
        cmd->setViewport(viewport);

        ASH::Rect2D scissor{ 0, 0, m_swapchain.getExtent().width, m_swapchain.getExtent().height };
        cmd->setScissor(scissor);

        m_fxaa.apply(cmd, m_currentFrame, m_ldrTarget.getColorTexture(), m_ldrTarget.getSampler());

        renderImGui(cmd);

        cmd->endRenderPass();
    }

    transition(m_swapchain.getImage(imageIndex), ASH::ResourceState::ColorAttachment, ASH::ResourceState::Present);
    cmd->end();
}

void Renderer::recreateSwapchain()
{
    LOG("recreateSwapchain() called!");
    ASH::Device* device = m_context.getDevice();
    device->waitIdle();

    m_framebuffers.cleanup();
    m_depthBuffer.cleanup();
    m_swapchain.recreate(m_context, m_window);
    ASH::Extent2D extent = m_swapchain.getExtent();

    m_godRayMaskFramebuffer.reset();
    m_godRayBlurFramebuffer.reset();
    m_godRayMaskTarget.cleanup();
    m_godRayBlurTarget.cleanup();
    m_godRayMaskTarget.initialize(device, extent);
    m_godRayBlurTarget.initialize(device, extent);

    ASH::FramebufferDesc maskFbDesc{};
    maskFbDesc.renderPass = m_postRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    maskFbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_godRayMaskTarget.getColorTexture() };
    maskFbDesc.extent = extent;
    maskFbDesc.layers = 1;
    m_godRayMaskFramebuffer = device->createFramebuffer(maskFbDesc);

    ASH::FramebufferDesc blurFbDesc{};
    blurFbDesc.renderPass = m_postRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    blurFbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_godRayBlurTarget.getColorTexture() };
    blurFbDesc.extent = extent;
    blurFbDesc.layers = 1;
    m_godRayBlurFramebuffer = device->createFramebuffer(blurFbDesc);

    m_depthBuffer.initialize(device, extent);
    m_framebuffers.initialize(device, m_swapchain, m_renderPass.getRenderPass(), m_depthBuffer.getTexture());

    createFogDescriptorSets();

    m_velocityTarget.cleanup();
    m_velocityTarget.initialize(device, extent);

    m_hdrFramebuffer.reset();
    m_hdrTarget.cleanup();
    m_hdrTarget.initialize(device, extent);

    ASH::FramebufferDesc hdrFbDesc{};
    hdrFbDesc.renderPass = m_hdrRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    hdrFbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_hdrTarget.getColorTexture(), m_velocityTarget.getColorTexture() };
    hdrFbDesc.depthStencilAttachment = m_depthBuffer.getTexture();
    hdrFbDesc.extent = extent;
    hdrFbDesc.layers = 1;
    m_hdrFramebuffer = device->createFramebuffer(hdrFbDesc);

    m_hdrDecalFramebuffer.reset();
    ASH::FramebufferDesc hdrDecalFbDesc{};
    hdrDecalFbDesc.renderPass = m_hdrDecalRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    hdrDecalFbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_hdrTarget.getColorTexture() };
    hdrDecalFbDesc.extent = extent;
    hdrDecalFbDesc.layers = 1;
    m_hdrDecalFramebuffer = device->createFramebuffer(hdrDecalFbDesc);

    m_brightFramebuffer.reset();
    m_brightTarget.cleanup();
    m_brightTarget.initialize(device, extent);

    ASH::FramebufferDesc brightFbDesc{};
    brightFbDesc.renderPass = m_postRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    brightFbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_brightTarget.getColorTexture() };
    brightFbDesc.extent = extent;
    brightFbDesc.layers = 1;
    m_brightFramebuffer = device->createFramebuffer(brightFbDesc);

    for (auto& fb : m_bloomFramebuffers) fb.reset();
    m_bloomChain.cleanup();
    m_bloomChain.initialize(device, extent);

    for (uint32_t level = 0; level < BloomChain::MIP_COUNT; level++)
    {
        ASH::FramebufferDesc fbDesc{};
        fbDesc.renderPass = m_postRenderPass.getRenderPass();
        fbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_bloomChain.getMipTexture(level) };
        fbDesc.extent = m_bloomChain.getMipExtent(level);
        fbDesc.layers = 1;
        m_bloomFramebuffers[level] = device->createFramebuffer(fbDesc);
    }

    m_ldrFramebuffer.reset();
    m_ldrTarget.cleanup();
    m_ldrTarget.initialize(device, extent);

    ASH::FramebufferDesc ldrFbDesc{};
    ldrFbDesc.renderPass = m_ldrRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    ldrFbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_ldrTarget.getColorTexture() };
    ldrFbDesc.extent = extent;
    ldrFbDesc.layers = 1;
    m_ldrFramebuffer = device->createFramebuffer(ldrFbDesc);

    for (auto& fb : m_taaFramebuffers) fb.reset();
    m_historyBuffer.cleanup();
    m_historyBuffer.initialize(device, extent);

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 2; i++)
    {
        ASH::FramebufferDesc fbDesc{};
        fbDesc.renderPass = m_postRenderPass.getRenderPass();
        fbDesc.colorAttachments = { m_historyBuffer.getTexture(i) };
        fbDesc.extent = extent;
        fbDesc.layers = 1;
        m_taaFramebuffers[i] = device->createFramebuffer(fbDesc);
    }

    auto writeDescSet = [&](ASH::DescriptorSet* set, ASH::Texture* texture, ASH::Sampler* sampler) {
        ASH::DescriptorImageInfo info{ texture, sampler };
        ASH::DescriptorWrite write{ 0, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &info };
        set->update(&write, 1);
    };

    for (uint32_t level = 0; level < BloomChain::MIP_COUNT; level++)
    {
        ASH::Texture* srcTex = (level == 0) ? m_brightTarget.getColorTexture() : m_bloomChain.getMipTexture(level - 1);
        ASH::Sampler* srcSampler = (level == 0) ? m_brightTarget.getSampler() : m_bloomChain.getSampler();
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
            writeDescSet(m_downsampleDescriptorSets[level][i].get(), srcTex, srcSampler);
    }

    for (uint32_t level = 0; level < BloomChain::MIP_COUNT - 1; level++)
    {
        ASH::Texture* srcTex = m_bloomChain.getMipTexture(level + 1);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
            writeDescSet(m_upsampleDescriptorSets[level][i].get(), srcTex, m_bloomChain.getSampler());
    }

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        writeDescSet(m_godRayMaskDescriptorSets[i].get(), m_depthBuffer.getTexture(), m_depthBuffer.getSampler());

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        writeDescSet(m_godRayBlurDescriptorSets[i].get(), m_godRayMaskTarget.getColorTexture(), m_godRayMaskTarget.getSampler());

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        writeDescSet(m_godRayCompositeDescriptorSets[i].get(), m_godRayBlurTarget.getColorTexture(), m_godRayBlurTarget.getSampler());

    for (uint32_t slot = 0; slot < 2; slot++)
    {
        uint32_t prevSlot = 1 - slot;
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        {
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo hdrInfo{ m_hdrTarget.getColorTexture(), m_hdrTarget.getSampler() };
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo velInfo{ m_velocityTarget.getColorTexture(), m_velocityTarget.getSampler() };
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo histInfo{ m_historyBuffer.getTexture(prevSlot), m_historyBuffer.getSampler() };

            std::array<ASH::DescriptorWrite, 3> writes{};
            writes[0] = { 0, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &hdrInfo };
            writes[1] = { 1, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &velInfo };
            writes[2] = { 2, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &histInfo };

            m_taaDescriptorSets[slot][i]->update(writes.data(), (uint32_t)writes.size());
        }
    }

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        writeDescSet(m_brightDescriptorSetsRaw[i].get(), m_hdrTarget.getColorTexture(), m_hdrTarget.getSampler());

    for (uint32_t slot = 0; slot < 2; slot++)
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
            writeDescSet(m_brightDescriptorSets[slot][i].get(), m_historyBuffer.getTexture(slot), m_historyBuffer.getSampler());

    for (uint32_t slot = 0; slot < 2; slot++)
    {
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        {
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo hdrInfo{ m_historyBuffer.getTexture(slot), m_historyBuffer.getSampler() };
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo bloomInfo{ m_bloomChain.getMipTexture(0), m_bloomChain.getSampler() };
            std::array<ASH::DescriptorWrite, 2> writes{};
            writes[0] = { 0, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &hdrInfo };
            writes[1] = { 1, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &bloomInfo };
            m_postDescriptorSets[slot][i]->update(writes.data(), (uint32_t)writes.size());
        }
    }

    {
        ASH::DescriptorImageInfo hdrInfo{ m_hdrTarget.getColorTexture(), m_hdrTarget.getSampler() };
        ASH::DescriptorImageInfo bloomInfo{ m_bloomChain.getMipTexture(0), m_bloomChain.getSampler() };
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        {
            std::array<ASH::DescriptorWrite, 2> writes{};
            writes[0] = { 0, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &hdrInfo };
            writes[1] = { 1, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &bloomInfo };
            m_postDescriptorSetsRaw[i]->update(writes.data(), (uint32_t)writes.size());
        }
    }

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT; i++)
        m_fxaa.updateDescriptorSet(i, m_ldrTarget.getColorTexture(), m_ldrTarget.getSampler());

    uint32_t imageCount = m_swapchain.getImageCount();
    if (m_renderFinishedSemaphore.size() != imageCount)
    {
        m_renderFinishedSemaphore.resize(imageCount);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < imageCount; i++)
            m_renderFinishedSemaphore[i] = device->createSemaphore();
        m_imagesInFlight.assign(imageCount, nullptr);
    }

    {
        auto cmd = device->createCommandBuffer();
        cmd->begin();

        ASH::ClearColor colorClear{};
        colorClear.f32[0] = 0.0f; colorClear.f32[1] = 0.0f; colorClear.f32[2] = 0.0f; colorClear.f32[3] = 1.0f;
        std::array<ASH::ClearColor, 2> hdrColorClears = { colorClear, colorClear };
        ASH::ClearDepthStencil depthClear{ 1.0f, 0 };

        ASH::RenderPassBeginInfo hdrPass{};
        hdrPass.renderPass = m_hdrRenderPass.getRenderPass();
        hdrPass.framebuffer = m_hdrFramebuffer.get();
        hdrPass.renderArea = { 0, 0, extent.width, extent.height };
        hdrPass.colorClearValues = hdrColorClears.data();
        hdrPass.colorClearCount = 2;
        hdrPass.depthStencilClear = &depthClear;
        cmd->beginRenderPass(hdrPass);
        cmd->endRenderPass();

        for (uint32_t slot = 0; slot < 2; slot++)
        {
            ASH::RenderPassBeginInfo pass{};
            pass.renderPass = m_postRenderPass.getRenderPass();
            pass.framebuffer = m_taaFramebuffers[slot].get();
            pass.renderArea = { 0, 0, extent.width, extent.height };
            pass.colorClearValues = &colorClear;
            pass.colorClearCount = 1;
            cmd->beginRenderPass(pass);
            cmd->endRenderPass();

            ASH::TextureBarrier tb{};
            tb.texture = m_historyBuffer.getTexture(slot);
            tb.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::ColorAttachment;
            tb.newState = ASH::ResourceState::ShaderReadOnly;
            cmd->barrier(&tb, 1, nullptr, 0);
        }

        cmd->end();

        ASH::SubmitInfo submitInfo{};
        submitInfo.commandBuffer = cmd.get();
        device->submit(submitInfo);
        device->waitIdle();
    }
}

void Renderer::setAAMethod(AAMethod method)
{
    m_aaMethod = method;

    switch (method)
    {
        case AAMethod::FXAA:
            break;
        case AAMethod::None:
        default:
            m_antiAliasing = std::make_unique<NoAA>();
            m_antiAliasing->initialize(m_context.getDevice(), m_swapchain.getExtent());
            break;
    }
}

void Renderer::cleanup()
{
    m_taaDescriptorSets[0].clear();
    m_taaDescriptorSets[1].clear();
    m_brightDescriptorSets[0].clear();
    m_brightDescriptorSets[1].clear();
    m_brightDescriptorSetsRaw.clear();
    for (auto& v : m_downsampleDescriptorSets) v.clear();
    for (auto& v : m_upsampleDescriptorSets) v.clear();
    m_godRayMaskDescriptorSets.clear();
    m_godRayBlurDescriptorSets.clear();
    m_godRayCompositeDescriptorSets.clear();
    m_postDescriptorSets[0].clear();
    m_postDescriptorSets[1].clear();
    m_postDescriptorSetsRaw.clear();
    m_fogDescriptorSets.clear();

    m_skyboxDescriptorSet.reset();
    m_skyboxPipeline.cleanup();
    m_environmentCubemap.cleanup();
    m_envBaker.cleanup();
    
    if (m_cleaned) return;
    m_cleaned = true;

    cleanupImGui();
    m_imguiDescriptorPool.reset();

    m_voxelGridInfoBuffer.reset();

    ASH::Device* device = m_context.getDevice();
    device->waitIdle();

    m_skinnedPipeline.cleanup();

    m_skyboxPipeline.cleanup();
    m_environmentCubemap.cleanup();
    m_irradianceCubemap.cleanup();
    m_envBaker.cleanup();

    m_voxelGI.cleanup();

    m_godRayMaskFramebuffer.reset();
    m_godRayBlurFramebuffer.reset();
    m_godRayMaskPipeline.cleanup();
    m_godRayBlurPipeline.cleanup();
    m_godRayCompositePipeline.cleanup();
    m_godRayMaskTarget.cleanup();
    m_godRayBlurTarget.cleanup();

    m_decalPipeline.cleanup();
    m_fxaa.cleanup();
    m_ldrFramebuffer.reset();
    m_ldrRenderPass.cleanup();
    m_ldrTarget.cleanup();

    m_fogPipeline.cleanup();
    m_fogCameraBuffer.cleanup();

    m_hdrFramebuffer.reset();
    m_postPipeline.cleanup();
    m_hdrRenderPass.cleanup();
    m_hdrTarget.cleanup();

    m_velocityTarget.cleanup();

    m_decalBox.cleanup();
    m_decalCameraBuffer.cleanup();
    m_hdrDecalFramebuffer.reset();
    m_hdrDecalRenderPass.cleanup();

    m_pointShadowPipeline.cleanup();
    m_pointShadowRenderPass.cleanup();
    m_shadowAtlasFramebuffer.reset();
    m_shadowAtlas.cleanup();

    for (auto& decal : m_screenSpaceDecals)
        decal->cleanup();
    m_screenSpaceDecals.clear();

    for (auto& fb : m_taaFramebuffers) fb.reset();
    m_taaPipeline.cleanup();
    m_historyBuffer.cleanup();

    for (auto& fb : m_bloomFramebuffers) fb.reset();
    m_upsamplePipeline.cleanup();
    m_downsamplePipeline.cleanup();
    m_bloomChain.cleanup();

    m_brightFramebuffer.reset();
    m_brightPipeline.cleanup();
    m_brightTarget.cleanup();

    m_postRenderPassLoad.cleanup();
    m_postRenderPass.cleanup();

    m_shadowFramebuffer.reset();
    m_shadowPipeline.cleanup();
    m_skinnedShadowPipeline.cleanup();
    m_shadowRenderPass.cleanup();
    m_shadowMap.cleanup();
    
    m_voxelizeShadowDataBuffer.reset();

    m_lightBuffer.cleanup();
    m_uniformBuffer.cleanup();
    m_imagesInFlight.clear();
    m_inFlightFences.clear();
    m_renderFinishedSemaphore.clear();
    m_imageAvailableSemaphore.clear();
    m_commandManager.cleanup();
    m_framebuffers.cleanup();
    m_pipeline.cleanup();
    m_renderPass.cleanup();
    m_depthBuffer.cleanup();
    m_swapchain.cleanup();
    m_context.shutdown();
}
std::array<glm::vec4, 6> Renderer::extractFrustumPlanes(const glm::mat4& viewProj) const
{
    std::array<glm::vec4, 6> planes;
    glm::mat4 m = glm::transpose(viewProj);

    planes[0] = m[3] + m[0];
    planes[1] = m[3] - m[0];
    planes[2] = m[3] + m[1];
    planes[3] = m[3] - m[1];
    planes[4] = m[3] + m[2];
    planes[5] = m[3] - m[2];

    for (auto& p : planes)
        p /= glm::length(glm::vec3(p));

    return planes;
}

bool Renderer::isSubmeshVisible(const glm::mat4& modelMatrix, const Submesh& sm, const std::array<glm::vec4, 6>& planes) const
{
    glm::vec3 corners[8] = {
        { sm.boundsMin.x, sm.boundsMin.y, sm.boundsMin.z },
        { sm.boundsMax.x, sm.boundsMin.y, sm.boundsMin.z },
        { sm.boundsMin.x, sm.boundsMax.y, sm.boundsMin.z },
        { sm.boundsMax.x, sm.boundsMax.y, sm.boundsMin.z },
        { sm.boundsMin.x, sm.boundsMin.y, sm.boundsMax.z },
        { sm.boundsMax.x, sm.boundsMin.y, sm.boundsMax.z },
        { sm.boundsMin.x, sm.boundsMax.y, sm.boundsMax.z },
        { sm.boundsMax.x, sm.boundsMax.y, sm.boundsMax.z }
    };

    glm::vec3 worldCorners[8];
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
        worldCorners[i] = glm::vec3(modelMatrix * glm::vec4(corners[i], 1.0f));

    for (const auto& plane : planes)
    {
        int outsideCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
        {
            float dist = glm::dot(glm::vec3(plane), worldCorners[i]) + plane.w;
            if (dist < 0.0f)
                outsideCount++;
        }
        if (outsideCount == 8)
            return false;
    }

    return true;
}

void Renderer::updatePointLightMatrices(int slot, glm::vec3 lightPos)
{
    float aspect = 1.0f;
    float nearPlane = 0.1f;
    glm::mat4 shadowProj = glm::perspective(glm::radians(90.0f), aspect, nearPlane, m_pointShadowFarPlane);
    m_pointLightViewProjArr[slot][0] = shadowProj * glm::lookAt(lightPos, lightPos + glm::vec3( 1, 0, 0), glm::vec3(0,-1, 0));
    m_pointLightViewProjArr[slot][1] = shadowProj * glm::lookAt(lightPos, lightPos + glm::vec3(-1, 0, 0), glm::vec3(0,-1, 0));
    m_pointLightViewProjArr[slot][2] = shadowProj * glm::lookAt(lightPos, lightPos + glm::vec3( 0, 1, 0), glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));
    m_pointLightViewProjArr[slot][3] = shadowProj * glm::lookAt(lightPos, lightPos + glm::vec3( 0,-1, 0), glm::vec3(0, 0,-1));
    m_pointLightViewProjArr[slot][4] = shadowProj * glm::lookAt(lightPos, lightPos + glm::vec3( 0, 0, 1), glm::vec3(0,-1, 0));
    m_pointLightViewProjArr[slot][5] = shadowProj * glm::lookAt(lightPos, lightPos + glm::vec3( 0, 0,-1), glm::vec3(0,-1, 0));
}

bool Renderer::isVisible(const GameObject& obj, const std::array<glm::vec4, 6>& planes) const
{
    if (!obj.mesh) return false;

    glm::vec3 worldCentre = glm::vec3(obj.transform.getMatrix() * glm::vec4(obj.mesh->getLocalBoundCentre(), 1.0f));

    float maxScale = glm::max(obj.transform.scale.x, glm::max(obj.transform.scale.y, obj.transform.scale.z));
    float worldRadius = obj.mesh->getLocalBoundsRadius() * maxScale;

    for (const auto& plane : planes)
    {
        float dist = glm::dot(glm::vec3(plane), worldCentre) + plane.w;

        if (dist < -worldRadius)
            return false;
    }

    return true;
}

std::vector<const GameObject*> Renderer::parallelCull(const Scene& scene, const std::array<glm::vec4, 6>& planes) const
{
    const auto& objects = scene.getObjects();
    size_t total = objects.size();

    unsigned int numThreads = std::max(1u, std::thread::hardware_concurrency());
    numThreads = std::min((size_t)numThreads, total > 0 ? total : 1);

    size_t chunkSize = (total + numThreads - 1) / numThreads;

    std::vector<std::future<std::vector<const GameObject*>>> futures;

    for (unsigned int t = 0; t < numThreads; t++)
    {
        size_t start = t * chunkSize;
        size_t end = std::min(start + chunkSize, total);
        if (start >= end) continue;

        futures.push_back(std::async(std::launch::async, [this, &objects, &planes, start, end]() {
            std::vector<const GameObject*> localVisible;
            for (size_t i = start; i < end; i++)
            {
                if (isVisible(*objects[i], planes))
                    localVisible.push_back(objects[i].get());
            }
            return localVisible;
        }));
    }

    std::vector<const GameObject*> visible;
    for (auto& f : futures)
    {
        auto chunk = f.get();
        visible.insert(visible.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
    }

    return visible;
}


// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/RenderPass.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/RenderPass.h"

void RenderPass::initialize(ASH::Device* device, ASH::Format swapchainFormat, ASH::Format depthFormat)
{
    ASH::AttachmentDesc colorAttachment{};
    colorAttachment.format = swapchainFormat;
    colorAttachment.sampleCount = 1;
    colorAttachment.loadOp = ASH::LoadOp::Clear;
    colorAttachment.storeOp = ASH::StoreOp::Store;
    colorAttachment.stencilLoadOp = ASH::LoadOp::DontCare;
    colorAttachment.stencilStoreOp = ASH::StoreOp::DontCare;

    ASH::RenderPassDesc desc{};
    desc.colorAttachments.push_back(colorAttachment);
    desc.hasDepthStencil = true;
    desc.depthStencilAttachment.format = depthFormat;
    desc.depthStencilAttachment.sampleCount = 1;
    desc.depthStencilAttachment.loadOp = ASH::LoadOp::Clear;
    desc.depthStencilAttachment.storeOp = ASH::StoreOp::DontCare;
    desc.depthStencilAttachment.stencilLoadOp = ASH::LoadOp::DontCare;
    desc.depthStencilAttachment.stencilStoreOp = ASH::StoreOp::DontCare;

    m_renderPass = device->createRenderPass(desc);
}

void RenderPass::cleanup()
{
    m_renderPass.reset();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/ShaderLoader.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/ShaderLoader.h"
#include "Core/Path.h"
#include <fstream>
#include <stdexcept>

std::vector<uint32_t> loadSpirvFile(const std::string& name)
{
    auto path = Paths::assetDir() / "shaders" / (name + ".spv");

    std::ifstream file(path.string(), std::ios::ate | std::ios::binary);
    if (!file.is_open())
        throw std::runtime_error("failed to open SPIR-V file: " + path.string());

    size_t fileSize = (size_t)file.tellg();
    std::vector<uint32_t> buffer(fileSize / sizeof(uint32_t));

    file.seekg(0);
    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(buffer.data()), fileSize);
    file.close();

    return buffer;
}

#include "Vulkan/ShadowAtlas.h"

void ShadowAtlas::initialize(ASH::Device* device)
{
    ASH::TextureDesc texDesc{};
    texDesc.type = ASH::TextureType::Texture2D;
    texDesc.format = m_format;
    texDesc.extent = { getWidth(), getHeight(), 1 };
    texDesc.usage = ASH::TextureUsage::DepthStencilAttachment | ASH::TextureUsage::Sampled;

    m_texture = device->createTexture(texDesc);

    ASH::SamplerDesc samplerDesc{};
    samplerDesc.magFilter = ASH::Filter::Linear;
    samplerDesc.minFilter = ASH::Filter::Linear;
    samplerDesc.addressModeU = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeV = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeW = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.compareEnable = false;

    m_sampler = device->createSampler(samplerDesc);
}

void ShadowAtlas::cleanup()
{
    m_sampler.reset();
    m_texture.reset();
}

void ShadowMap::initialize(ASH::Device* device, uint32_t size)
{
    m_size = size;

    ASH::TextureDesc texDesc{};
    texDesc.type = ASH::TextureType::Texture2D;
    texDesc.format = m_format;
    texDesc.extent = { size, size, 1 };
    texDesc.usage = ASH::TextureUsage::DepthStencilAttachment | ASH::TextureUsage::Sampled;

    m_texture = device->createTexture(texDesc);

    ASH::SamplerDesc samplerDesc{};
    samplerDesc.magFilter = ASH::Filter::Linear;
    samplerDesc.minFilter = ASH::Filter::Linear;
    samplerDesc.addressModeU = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeV = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeW = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.compareEnable = true;
    samplerDesc.compareOp = ASH::CompareOp::Less;
    samplerDesc.minLod = 0.0f;
    samplerDesc.maxLod = 1.0f;

    m_sampler = device->createSampler(samplerDesc);
}

    m_texture.reset();

#include <cstring>

void SkinnedVertexBuffer::initialize(ASH::Device* device, const std::vector<SkinnedVertex>& vertices)
{
    m_vertexCount = static_cast<uint32_t>(vertices.size());
    
    ASH::BufferDesc desc{};
    desc.size = sizeof(SkinnedVertex) * vertices.size();
    desc.usage = ASH::BufferUsage::VertexBuffer;
    desc.memory = ASH::MemoryUsage::CpuToGpu;

    m_buffer = device->createBuffer(desc);

    void* data = m_buffer->map();
    memcpy(data, vertices.data(), desc.size);
    m_buffer->unmap();
}

void SkinnedVertexBuffer::cleanup()
{
    m_buffer.reset();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/SwapChain.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/SwapChain.h"

void SwapChain::initialize(VulkanContext& context, GLFWwindow* window)
{
    int width, height;
    glfwGetFramebufferSize(window, &width, &height);

    ASH::SwapChainDesc desc{};
    desc.extent = { (uint32_t)width, (uint32_t)height };
    desc.format = m_format;
    desc.imageCount = 3;
    desc.presentMode = ASH::PresentMode::Fifo;

    m_swapChain = context.getDevice()->createSwapChain(desc);
}

void SwapChain::recreate(VulkanContext& context, GLFWwindow* window)
{
    int width = 0, height = 0;
    glfwGetFramebufferSize(window, &width, &height);
    while (width == 0 || height == 0)
    {
        glfwGetFramebufferSize(window, &width, &height);
        glfwWaitEvents();
    }

    m_swapChain->resize({ (uint32_t)width, (uint32_t)height });
}

void SwapChain::cleanup()
{
    m_swapChain.reset();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/Texture.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/Texture.h"
#include <stb_image.h>
#include <cstring>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

void Texture::upload(ASH::Device* device, const unsigned char* pixels, int width, int height, bool srgb)
{
    uint32_t mipLevels = (uint32_t)std::floor(std::log2(std::max(width, height))) + 1;

    ASH::TextureDesc texDesc{};
    texDesc.type = ASH::TextureType::Texture2D;
    texDesc.format = srgb ? ASH::Format::R8G8B8A8_SRGB : ASH::Format::R8G8B8A8_UNorm;
    texDesc.extent = { (uint32_t)width, (uint32_t)height, 1 };
    texDesc.usage = ASH::TextureUsage::Sampled | ASH::TextureUsage::TransferDst | ASH::TextureUsage::TransferSrc;
    texDesc.mipLevels = mipLevels;

    m_texture = device->createTexture(texDesc);

    size_t dataSize = (size_t)width * (size_t)height * 4;

    ASH::BufferDesc stagingDesc{};
    stagingDesc.size = dataSize;
    stagingDesc.usage = ASH::BufferUsage::TransferSrc;
    stagingDesc.memory = ASH::MemoryUsage::CpuToGpu;

    auto stagingBuffer = device->createBuffer(stagingDesc);
    void* mapped = stagingBuffer->map();
    memcpy(mapped, pixels, dataSize);
    stagingBuffer->unmap();

    auto cmd = device->createCommandBuffer();
    cmd->begin();

    ASH::TextureBarrier wholeToTransferDst{};
    wholeToTransferDst.texture = m_texture.get();
    wholeToTransferDst.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::Undefined;
    wholeToTransferDst.newState = ASH::ResourceState::TransferDst;
    cmd->barrier(&wholeToTransferDst, 1, nullptr, 0);

    cmd->copyBufferToTexture(stagingBuffer.get(), m_texture.get());

    int32_t mipWidth = width;
    int32_t mipHeight = height;

    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < mipLevels; i++)
    {
        ASH::TextureBarrier prevToSrc{};
        prevToSrc.texture = m_texture.get();
        prevToSrc.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::TransferDst;
        prevToSrc.newState = ASH::ResourceState::TransferSrc;
        prevToSrc.useSpecificMip = true;
        prevToSrc.mipLevel = i - 1;
        cmd->barrier(&prevToSrc, 1, nullptr, 0);

        int32_t nextWidth = mipWidth > 1 ? mipWidth / 2 : 1;
        int32_t nextHeight = mipHeight > 1 ? mipHeight / 2 : 1;

        ASH::TextureBlitRegion blitRegion{};
        blitRegion.srcTexture = m_texture.get();
        blitRegion.srcMipLevel = i - 1;
        blitRegion.srcExtentX = mipWidth;
        blitRegion.srcExtentY = mipHeight;
        blitRegion.dstTexture = m_texture.get();
        blitRegion.dstMipLevel = i;
        blitRegion.dstExtentX = nextWidth;
        blitRegion.dstExtentY = nextHeight;
        cmd->blitTexture(blitRegion);

        ASH::TextureBarrier prevToShaderRead{};
        prevToShaderRead.texture = m_texture.get();
        prevToShaderRead.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::TransferSrc;
        prevToShaderRead.newState = ASH::ResourceState::ShaderReadOnly;
        prevToShaderRead.useSpecificMip = true;
        prevToShaderRead.mipLevel = i - 1;
        cmd->barrier(&prevToShaderRead, 1, nullptr, 0);

        mipWidth = nextWidth;
        mipHeight = nextHeight;
    }

    ASH::TextureBarrier lastToShaderRead{};
    lastToShaderRead.texture = m_texture.get();
    lastToShaderRead.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::TransferDst;
    lastToShaderRead.newState = ASH::ResourceState::ShaderReadOnly;
    lastToShaderRead.useSpecificMip = true;
    lastToShaderRead.mipLevel = mipLevels - 1;
    cmd->barrier(&lastToShaderRead, 1, nullptr, 0);

    cmd->end();

    ASH::SubmitInfo submitInfo{};
    submitInfo.commandBuffer = cmd.get();
    device->submit(submitInfo);
    device->waitIdle();

    ASH::SamplerDesc samplerDesc{};
    samplerDesc.addressModeU = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeV = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeW = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.maxLod = (float)mipLevels;
    m_sampler = device->createSampler(samplerDesc);
}

void Texture::initializeFromMemory(ASH::Device* device, const unsigned char* pixels, int width, int height, bool srgb)
{
    upload(device, pixels, width, height, srgb);
}

void Texture::initialize(ASH::Device* device, const std::string& path, bool srgb)
{
    int width, height, channels;
    unsigned char* pixels = stbi_load(path.c_str(), &width, &height, &channels, STBI_rgb_alpha);
    if (!pixels)
        throw std::runtime_error("failed to load texture: " + path);

    upload(device, pixels, width, height, srgb);
    stbi_image_free(pixels);
}

void Texture::cleanup()
{
    m_sampler.reset();
    m_texture.reset();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/UniformBuffer.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/UniformBuffer.h"
#include <cstring>

void UniformBuffer::initialize(ASH::Device* device, uint32_t frameCount)
{
    m_buffers.resize(frameCount);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < frameCount; i++)
    {
        ASH::BufferDesc desc{};
        desc.size = sizeof(MVP);
        desc.usage = ASH::BufferUsage::UniformBuffer;
        desc.memory = ASH::MemoryUsage::CpuToGpu;
        m_buffers[i] = device->createBuffer(desc);
    }
}

void UniformBuffer::update(uint32_t frameIndex, const MVP& mvp)
{
    void* mapped = m_buffers[frameIndex]->map();
    memcpy(mapped, &mvp, sizeof(MVP));
    m_buffers[frameIndex]->unmap();
}

void UniformBuffer::cleanup()
{
    m_buffers.clear();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/VoxelChunkManager.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/VoxelChunkManager.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

void VoxelChunkManager::initialize(float chunkSize, int32_t visibilityRadiusChunks, int32_t atlasSlotsPerAxis)
{
    m_chunkSize = chunkSize;
    m_visibilityRadiusChunks = visibilityRadiusChunks;
    m_atlasSlotsPerAxis = atlasSlotsPerAxis;
    m_atlasSlotOccupied.assign(getMaxAtlasSlots(), false);
}

ChunkCoord VoxelChunkManager::worldToChunkCoord(glm::vec3 worldPos) const
{
    return ChunkCoord{
        (int32_t)std::floor(worldPos.x / m_chunkSize),
        (int32_t)std::floor(worldPos.y / m_chunkSize),
        (int32_t)std::floor(worldPos.z / m_chunkSize)
    };
}

glm::vec3 VoxelChunkManager::chunkCoordToWorldMin(ChunkCoord coord) const
{
    return glm::vec3((float)coord.x, (float)coord.y, (float)coord.z) * m_chunkSize;
}

int32_t VoxelChunkManager::findFreeAtlasSlot()
{
    for (size_t i = 0; i < m_atlasSlotOccupied.size(); i++)
    {
        if (!m_atlasSlotOccupied[i])
        {
            m_atlasSlotOccupied[i] = true;
            return (int32_t)i;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

VoxelChunkManager::UpdateResult VoxelChunkManager::update(glm::vec3 cameraPosition)
{
    UpdateResult result;

    ChunkCoord centerCoord = worldToChunkCoord(cameraPosition);

    std::unordered_map<ChunkCoord, bool, ChunkCoordHash> desiredChunks;
    for (int32_t dx = -m_visibilityRadiusChunks; dx <= m_visibilityRadiusChunks; dx++)
    for (int32_t dy = -m_visibilityRadiusChunks; dy <= m_visibilityRadiusChunks; dy++)
    for (int32_t dz = -m_visibilityRadiusChunks; dz <= m_visibilityRadiusChunks; dz++)
    {
        ChunkCoord c{ centerCoord.x + dx, centerCoord.y + dy, centerCoord.z + dz };
        desiredChunks[c] = true;
    }

    for (auto it = m_activeChunks.begin(); it != m_activeChunks.end(); )
    {
        if (desiredChunks.find(it->first) == desiredChunks.end())
        {
            if (it->second.atlasSlotIndex >= 0)
                m_atlasSlotOccupied[it->second.atlasSlotIndex] = false;

            result.chunksToEvict.push_back(it->first);
            it = m_activeChunks.erase(it);
        }
        else
        {
            ++it;
        }
    }

    std::vector<ChunkCoord> sortedCoords;
    for (const auto& [coord, _] : desiredChunks)
        sortedCoords.push_back(coord);

    std::sort(sortedCoords.begin(), sortedCoords.end(), [&](const ChunkCoord& a, const ChunkCoord& b)
    {
        int32_t da = (a.x - centerCoord.x) * (a.x - centerCoord.x) + (a.y - centerCoord.y) * (a.y - centerCoord.y) + (a.z - centerCoord.z) * (a.z - centerCoord.z);
        int32_t db = (b.x - centerCoord.x) * (b.x - centerCoord.x) + (b.y - centerCoord.y) * (b.y - centerCoord.y) + (b.z - centerCoord.z) * (b.z - centerCoord.z);
        return da < db;
    });

    for (const auto& coord : sortedCoords)
    {
        if (!result.chunksToBake.empty())
            break;

        if (m_activeChunks.find(coord) == m_activeChunks.end())
        {
            int32_t slot = findFreeAtlasSlot();
            if (slot < 0)
                continue;

            VoxelChunk chunk{};
            chunk.coord = coord;
            chunk.atlasSlotIndex = slot;
            chunk.needsRebake = true;

            m_activeChunks[coord] = chunk;
            result.chunksToBake.push_back(coord);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

VoxelChunk* VoxelChunkManager::getChunk(ChunkCoord coord)
{
    auto it = m_activeChunks.find(coord);
    return (it != m_activeChunks.end()) ? &it->second : nullptr;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/VoxelGI.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/VoxelGI.h"
#include "Scene/Scene.h"
#include "Core/Log.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Scene/Mesh.h"
#include "Scene/Material.h"
#include <cstring>

void VoxelGI::initialize(ASH::Device* device, float chunkSize, uint32_t chunkResolution, int32_t visibilityRadiusChunks)
{
    m_device = device;
    m_chunkResolution = chunkResolution;

    int32_t atlasSlotsPerAxis = visibilityRadiusChunks * 2 + 1;
    m_chunkManager.initialize(chunkSize, visibilityRadiusChunks, atlasSlotsPerAxis);

    uint32_t paddedResolution = chunkResolution + 2 * PADDING;
    uint32_t atlasResolution = (uint32_t)atlasSlotsPerAxis * paddedResolution;
    m_atlas.initialize(device, atlasResolution, ASH::Format::R8G8B8A8_UNorm, 1);

    {
        auto cmd = device->createCommandBuffer();
        cmd->begin();
        ASH::TextureBarrier tb{};
        tb.texture = m_atlas.getTexture();
        tb.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::Undefined;
        tb.newState = ASH::ResourceState::ShaderReadOnly;
        cmd->barrier(&tb, 1, nullptr, 0);
        cmd->end();

        ASH::SubmitInfo submitInfo{};
        submitInfo.commandBuffer = cmd.get();
        device->submit(submitInfo);
        device->waitIdle();
    }

    m_voxelizeRenderPass.initialize(device);

    PipelineConfig voxelizeConfig{};
    voxelizeConfig.vertexShader = "voxelize";
    voxelizeConfig.fragmentShader = "voxelize";
    voxelizeConfig.renderPass = m_voxelizeRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    voxelizeConfig.frameCount = 1;
    voxelizeConfig.layoutType = LayoutType::Voxelize;
    voxelizeConfig.colorAttachmentCount = 0;
    voxelizeConfig.depthTest = false;
    voxelizeConfig.depthWrite = false;
    voxelizeConfig.cullMode = ASH::CullMode::None;
    voxelizeConfig.useVertexInput = true;
    voxelizeConfig.pushConstantSize = sizeof(glm::mat4) + sizeof(glm::vec4) * 2 + sizeof(glm::ivec4);
    voxelizeConfig.pushStage = ASH::ShaderStage::Vertex | ASH::ShaderStage::Fragment;
    m_voxelizePipeline.initialize(device, voxelizeConfig);

    ComputePipelineConfig clearConfig{};
    clearConfig.computeShader = "clear_voxels";
    clearConfig.layoutType = LayoutType::VoxelClear;
    m_clearPipeline.initialize(device, clearConfig);

    int32_t pageTableSize = atlasSlotsPerAxis * atlasSlotsPerAxis * atlasSlotsPerAxis;
    ASH::BufferDesc pageTableDesc{};
    pageTableDesc.size = sizeof(int32_t) * pageTableSize;
    pageTableDesc.usage = ASH::BufferUsage::StorageBuffer;
    pageTableDesc.memory = ASH::MemoryUsage::CpuToGpu;
    m_pageTableBuffer = device->createBuffer(pageTableDesc);

    std::vector<int32_t> initialTable(pageTableSize, -1);
    void* mapped = m_pageTableBuffer->map();
    memcpy(mapped, initialTable.data(), initialTable.size() * sizeof(int32_t));
    m_pageTableBuffer->unmap();
}

void VoxelGI::updatePageTable(ASH::Device* device)
{
    int32_t radius = (m_chunkManager.getAtlasSlotsPerAxis() - 1) / 2;
    int32_t slotsPerAxis = m_chunkManager.getAtlasSlotsPerAxis();
    int32_t tableSize = slotsPerAxis * slotsPerAxis * slotsPerAxis;

    std::vector<int32_t> table(tableSize, -1);

    for (const auto& [coord, chunk] : m_chunkManager.getActiveChunks())
    {
        int32_t rx = coord.x - m_lastCameraChunk.x + radius;
        int32_t ry = coord.y - m_lastCameraChunk.y + radius;
        int32_t rz = coord.z - m_lastCameraChunk.z + radius;

        if (rx < 0 || rx >= slotsPerAxis || ry < 0 || ry >= slotsPerAxis || rz < 0 || rz >= slotsPerAxis)
            continue;

        int32_t index = rx + ry * slotsPerAxis + rz * slotsPerAxis * slotsPerAxis;
        table[index] = chunk.atlasSlotIndex;
    }

    void* mapped = m_pageTableBuffer->map();
    memcpy(mapped, table.data(), table.size() * sizeof(int32_t));
    m_pageTableBuffer->unmap();
}

void VoxelGI::update(ASH::CommandBuffer* cmd, const Scene& scene, glm::vec3 cameraPosition,
    ASH::Buffer* lightBuffer,
    ASH::Texture* shadowTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowSampler,
    ASH::Texture* shadowAtlasTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowAtlasSampler,
    ASH::Buffer* shadowDataBuffer)
{
    m_lastCameraChunk = m_chunkManager.worldToChunkCoord(cameraPosition);

    auto result = m_chunkManager.update(cameraPosition);

    if (!result.chunksToBake.empty())
    {
        ChunkCoord coord = result.chunksToBake[0];
        LOG("Voxel GI: baking chunk (" << coord.x << "," << coord.y << "," << coord.z << ")");
        VoxelChunk* chunk = m_chunkManager.getChunk(coord);
        if (chunk)
        {
            bakeChunk(cmd, coord, chunk->atlasSlotIndex, scene, lightBuffer,
                shadowTexture, shadowSampler, shadowAtlasTexture, shadowAtlasSampler, shadowDataBuffer);
        }
    }

    if (!result.chunksToBake.empty() || !result.chunksToEvict.empty())
        updatePageTable(m_device);
}

void VoxelGI::bakeChunk(ASH::CommandBuffer* cmd, ChunkCoord coord, int32_t atlasSlot, const Scene& scene,
    ASH::Buffer* lightBuffer,
    ASH::Texture* shadowTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowSampler,
    ASH::Texture* shadowAtlasTexture, ASH::Sampler* shadowAtlasSampler,
    ASH::Buffer* shadowDataBuffer)
{
    m_bakeDescriptorSets.clear();

    float chunkSize = m_chunkManager.getChunkSize();
    glm::vec3 chunkWorldMin = m_chunkManager.chunkCoordToWorldMin(coord);
    glm::vec3 chunkWorldSize(chunkSize, chunkSize, chunkSize);

    int32_t slotsPerAxis = m_chunkManager.getAtlasSlotsPerAxis();
    int32_t slotZ = atlasSlot / (slotsPerAxis * slotsPerAxis);
    int32_t slotY = (atlasSlot / slotsPerAxis) % slotsPerAxis;
    int32_t slotX = atlasSlot % slotsPerAxis;


    uint32_t paddedResolution = m_chunkResolution + 2 * PADDING;

    uint32_t atlasOffsetX = (uint32_t)slotX * paddedResolution;
    uint32_t atlasOffsetY = (uint32_t)slotY * paddedResolution;
    uint32_t atlasOffsetZ = (uint32_t)slotZ * paddedResolution;

    if (!m_chunkFramebuffer)
    {
        ASH::FramebufferDesc fbDesc{};
        fbDesc.renderPass = m_voxelizeRenderPass.getRenderPass();
        fbDesc.extent = { m_chunkResolution, paddedResolution };
        fbDesc.layers = 1;
        m_chunkFramebuffer = m_device->createFramebuffer(fbDesc);
    }

    ASH::TextureBarrier toGeneral{};
    toGeneral.texture = m_atlas.getTexture();
    toGeneral.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::ShaderReadOnly;
    toGeneral.newState = ASH::ResourceState::General;
    cmd->barrier(&toGeneral, 1, nullptr, 0);

    ASH::RenderPassBeginInfo passInfo{};
    passInfo.renderPass = m_voxelizeRenderPass.getRenderPass();
    passInfo.framebuffer = m_chunkFramebuffer.get();
    passInfo.renderArea = { 0, 0, m_chunkResolution, paddedResolution };

    cmd->beginRenderPass(passInfo);
    cmd->bindPipeline(m_voxelizePipeline.getPipeline());

    ASH::Viewport vp{};
    vp.width = (float)paddedResolution;
    vp.height = (float)paddedResolution;
    vp.maxDepth = 1.0f;
    cmd->setViewport(vp);
    ASH::Rect2D sc{ 0, 0, paddedResolution, paddedResolution };
    cmd->setScissor(sc);

    for (const auto& obj : scene.getObjects())
    {
        if (!obj->mesh) continue;

        obj->mesh->bind(cmd);

        for (const auto& sm : obj->mesh->getSubmeshes())
        {
            auto mat = obj->getMaterial(sm.materialIndex);
            if (!mat) continue;

            m_bakeDescriptorSets.push_back(m_voxelizePipeline.createDescriptorSet());
            ASH::DescriptorSet* descSet = m_bakeDescriptorSets.back().get();

            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo albedoInfo{ mat->getAlbedoTexture(), mat->getAlbedoSampler() };
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo voxelInfo{};
            voxelInfo.texture = m_atlas.getTexture();
            voxelInfo.useSpecificMipLayer = true;
            voxelInfo.mipLevel = 0;

            ASH::DescriptorBufferInfo lightInfo{ lightBuffer, 0, lightBuffer->getSize() };
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo shadowInfo{ shadowTexture, shadowSampler };
            ASH::DescriptorImageInfo shadowAtlasInfo{ shadowAtlasTexture, shadowAtlasSampler };
            ASH::DescriptorBufferInfo shadowDataInfo{ shadowDataBuffer, 0, shadowDataBuffer->getSize() };

            std::array<ASH::DescriptorWrite, 6> writes{};
            writes[0] = { 0, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &albedoInfo };
            writes[1] = { 1, ASH::DescriptorType::StorageImage, 0, nullptr, &voxelInfo };
            writes[2] = { 2, ASH::DescriptorType::UniformBuffer, 0, &lightInfo, nullptr };
            writes[3] = { 3, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &shadowInfo };
            writes[4] = { 4, ASH::DescriptorType::CombinedImageSampler, 0, nullptr, &shadowAtlasInfo };
            writes[5] = { 5, ASH::DescriptorType::UniformBuffer, 0, &shadowDataInfo, nullptr };
            descSet->update(writes.data(), (uint32_t)writes.size());

            cmd->bindDescriptorSet(m_voxelizePipeline.getPipeline(), 0, descSet);

            struct ChunkVoxelizePush
            {
                glm::mat4 model;
                glm::vec4 gridMin;
                glm::vec4 gridSize;
                glm::ivec4 atlasOffset;
            } push{};
            
            float voxelWorldSize = chunkSize / (float)m_chunkResolution;
            float paddingWorld = voxelWorldSize * (float)PADDING;
            glm::vec3 expandedMin = chunkWorldMin - glm::vec3(paddingWorld);
            glm::vec3 expandedSize = chunkWorldSize + glm::vec3(paddingWorld * 2.0f);

            push.model = obj->transform.getMatrix();
            push.gridMin = glm::vec4(expandedMin, 0.0f);
            push.gridSize = glm::vec4(expandedSize, 0.0f);
            push.atlasOffset = glm::ivec4((int)atlasOffsetX, (int)atlasOffsetY, (int)atlasOffsetZ, (int)paddedResolution);
            cmd->pushConstants(m_voxelizePipeline.getPipeline(), ASH::ShaderStage::Vertex | ASH::ShaderStage::Fragment, 0, sizeof(ChunkVoxelizePush), &push);
                    
            obj->mesh->drawSubmesh(cmd, sm);
        }
    }

    cmd->endRenderPass();

    ASH::TextureBarrier toShaderRead{};
    toShaderRead.texture = m_atlas.getTexture();
    toShaderRead.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::General;
    toShaderRead.newState = ASH::ResourceState::ShaderReadOnly;
    cmd->barrier(&toShaderRead, 1, nullptr, 0);
}

void VoxelGI::cleanup()
{
    m_chunkFramebuffer.reset();
    m_bakeDescriptorSets.clear();
    m_pageTableBuffer.reset();
    m_clearPipeline.cleanup();
    m_voxelizePipeline.cleanup();
    m_voxelizeRenderPass.cleanup();
    m_atlas.cleanup();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/VoxelizeRenderPass.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/VoxelizeRenderPass.h"

void VoxelizeRenderPass::initialize(ASH::Device* device)
{
    ASH::RenderPassDesc desc{};
    m_renderPass = device->createRenderPass(desc);
}

void VoxelizeRenderPass::cleanup()
{
    m_renderPass.reset();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/VoxelTexture3D.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/VoxelTexture3D.h"

void VoxelTexture3D::initialize(ASH::Device* device, uint32_t resolution, ASH::Format format, uint32_t mipLevels)
{
    m_resolution = resolution;
    m_mipLevels = mipLevels;

    ASH::TextureDesc texDesc{};
    texDesc.type = ASH::TextureType::Texture3D;
    texDesc.format = format;
    texDesc.extent = { resolution, resolution, resolution };
    texDesc.mipLevels = mipLevels;
    texDesc.usage = ASH::TextureUsage::Storage | ASH::TextureUsage::Sampled | ASH::TextureUsage::TransferDst;

    m_texture = device->createTexture(texDesc);

    auto cmd = device->createCommandBuffer();
    cmd->begin();
    ASH::TextureBarrier tb{};
    tb.texture = m_texture.get();
    tb.oldState = ASH::ResourceState::Undefined;
    tb.newState = ASH::ResourceState::General;
    cmd->barrier(&tb, 1, nullptr, 0);
    cmd->end();

    ASH::SubmitInfo submitInfo{};
    submitInfo.commandBuffer = cmd.get();
    device->submit(submitInfo);
    device->waitIdle();

    ASH::SamplerDesc samplerDesc{};
    samplerDesc.magFilter = ASH::Filter::Linear;
    samplerDesc.minFilter = ASH::Filter::Linear;
    samplerDesc.minLod = 0.0f;
    samplerDesc.maxLod = (float)(mipLevels -1);
    samplerDesc.addressModeU = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeV = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;
    samplerDesc.addressModeW = ASH::AddressMode::ClampToEdge;

    m_sampler = device->createSampler(samplerDesc);
}

void VoxelTexture3D::cleanup()
{
    m_sampler.reset();
    m_texture.reset();
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: Engine/src/Vulkan/VulkanContext.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "Vulkan/VulkanContext.h"
#include <vulkan/VulkanDevice.h>
#include <stdexcept>

void VulkanContext::initialize(GLFWwindow* window)
{
#ifdef NDEBUG
    bool enableValidation = false;
#else
    bool enableValidation = true;
#endif

    uint32_t glfwExtensionCount = 0;
    const char** glfwExtensions = glfwGetRequiredInstanceExtensions(&glfwExtensionCount);
    std::vector<const char*> requiredExtensions(glfwExtensions, glfwExtensions + glfwExtensionCount);

    m_device = ASH::createDevice(ASH::Backend::Vulkan, enableValidation, requiredExtensions);

    auto* vulkanDevice = static_cast<ASH::vulkan::VulkanDevice*>(m_device.get());
    VkResult result = glfwCreateWindowSurface(vulkanDevice->getInstance(), window, nullptr, &m_surface);
    if (result != VK_SUCCESS)
        throw std::runtime_error("failed to create window surface via GLFW");

    m_device->attachSurface(reinterpret_cast<void*>(m_surface));
}

void VulkanContext::shutdown()
{
    m_surface = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    m_device.reset();
}

if(NIX_INSTANTIATE_EXECUTABLE)
    execute_process(
        COMMAND ${NIX_INSTANTIATE_EXECUTABLE} --eval -E "(import <nixpkgs> {}).alsa-lib.outPath"
        OUTPUT_VARIABLE ALSA_LIB_PATH_RAW
        OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE
        RESULT_VARIABLE NIX_EVAL_RESULT
        ERROR_VARIABLE NIX_EVAL_ERROR
    )
    string(REPLACE "\"" "" ALSA_LIB_PATH "${ALSA_LIB_PATH_RAW}")

    if(NIX_EVAL_RESULT EQUAL 0 AND EXISTS "${ALSA_LIB_PATH}/lib")
        message(STATUS "Found alsa-lib via Nix: ${ALSA_LIB_PATH}/lib")
        set_target_properties(game PROPERTIES
            BUILD_RPATH "${ALSA_LIB_PATH}/lib"
            INSTALL_RPATH "${ALSA_LIB_PATH}/lib"
        )
    else()
        message(STATUS "Nix eval for alsa-lib failed or path not found — skipping RPATH injection")
        message(STATUS "Nix eval result: ${NIX_EVAL_RESULT}")
        message(STATUS "Nix eval error output: ${NIX_EVAL_ERROR}")
    endif()
else()
    message(STATUS "nix-instantiate not found — assuming system libraries are in standard paths")
endif()

target_include_directories(ELE PUBLIC
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include
)

add_custom_command(TARGET game POST_BUILD
    COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E copy_directory
            ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/Engine/assets
            $<TARGET_FILE_DIR:game>/assets
)

// ================================================================
// FILE: game/include/Components/DoorComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Components/RigidBodyComponent.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>

class DoorComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_rigidBody = owner.getComponent<RigidBodyComponent>();

        m_closedRotationY = owner.transform.rotation.y;
        m_openRotationY = m_closedRotationY + m_openAngle;
    }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        float target = m_isOpen ? m_openRotationY : m_closedRotationY;
        float current = m_owner->transform.rotation.y;

        float t = 1.0f - exp(-m_openSpeed * deltaTime);
        m_owner->transform.rotation.y = glm::mix(current, target, t);

        if (m_rigidBody)
        {
            glm::quat rot = glm::quat(glm::radians(m_owner->transform.rotation));
            m_rigidBody->setTransform(m_owner->transform.position, rot);
        }
    }

    void toggle() { m_isOpen = !m_isOpen; }

    void setOpenAngle(float degrees) { m_openAngle = degrees; m_openRotationY = m_closedRotationY + m_openAngle; }
    void setOpenSpeed(float speed) { m_openSpeed = speed; }
    bool isOpen() const { return m_isOpen; }

private:
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    RigidBodyComponent* m_rigidBody = nullptr;

    bool m_isOpen = false;
    float m_closedRotationY = 0.0f;
    float m_openRotationY = 0.0f;
    float m_openAngle = 90.0f;
    float m_openSpeed = 4.0f;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: game/include/Components/HeadBobComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Components/CameraComponent.h"
#include "Components/CharacterComponent.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <cmath>

class HeadBobComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    HeadBobComponent(glm::vec3 baseEyeOffset)
        : m_baseEyeOffset(baseEyeOffset)
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_camera = owner.getComponent<CameraComponent>();
        m_character = owner.getComponent<CharacterComponent>();
    }

    void applyBob(float deltaTime)
    {
        if (!m_camera || !m_character) return;

        float speed = glm::length(glm::vec2(m_character->getVelocity().x, m_character->getVelocity().z));

        if (speed > m_moveThreshold && m_character->isOnGround())
            updateBob(deltaTime);
        else
            resetBob(deltaTime);
    }

    void setBobFrequency(float f) { m_bobFrequency = f; }
    void setAmplitudeVertical(float a) { m_bobAmplitudeV = a; }
    void setAmplitudeHorizontal(float a) { m_bobAmplitudeH = a; }
    void setStepRollAngle(float degrees) { m_stepRollAngle = degrees; }
    void setRollInterpSpeed(float s) { m_rollInterpSpeed = s; }
    void setRollResetSpeed(float s) { m_rollResetSpeed = s; }
    void setBobResetSpeed(float s) { m_bobResetSpeed = s; }

private:
    static float expInterpTo(float current, float target, float deltaTime, float speed)
    {
        if (speed <= 0.0f) return target;
        float t = 1.0f - exp(-speed * deltaTime);
        return glm::mix(current, target, t);
    }
    
    static glm::vec3 expInterpTo(glm::vec3 current, glm::vec3 target, float deltaTime, float speed)   // ДОБАВИТЬ
    {
        return glm::vec3(
            expInterpTo(current.x, target.x, deltaTime, speed),
            expInterpTo(current.y, target.y, deltaTime, speed),
            expInterpTo(current.z, target.z, deltaTime, speed)
        );
    }

    void updateBob(float deltaTime)
    {
        m_bobTime += deltaTime * m_bobFrequency;
        float sinValue = sin(m_bobTime);

        if (m_lastSinValue <= 0.0f && sinValue > 0.0f)
        {
            m_stepSide = !m_stepSide;
            m_targetRoll = m_stepSide ? m_stepRollAngle : -m_stepRollAngle;
        }
        m_lastSinValue = sinValue;

        glm::vec3 offset = m_baseEyeOffset;
        offset.y += sinValue * m_bobAmplitudeV;
        offset.x += sin(m_bobTime * 0.5f) * m_bobAmplitudeH;

        m_camera->setEyeOffset(offset);

        m_targetRoll = expInterpTo(m_targetRoll, 0.0f, deltaTime, m_rollResetSpeed);
        m_currentRoll = expInterpTo(m_currentRoll, m_targetRoll, deltaTime, m_rollInterpSpeed);

        m_camera->getCamera().setRoll(m_currentRoll);
    }

    void resetBob(float deltaTime)
    {
        glm::vec3 currentOffset = m_camera->getEyeOffset();
        glm::vec3 target = expInterpTo(currentOffset, m_baseEyeOffset, deltaTime, m_bobResetSpeed);
        m_camera->setEyeOffset(target);

        m_currentRoll = expInterpTo(m_currentRoll, 0.0f, deltaTime, m_rollResetSpeed);
        m_targetRoll = 0.0f;
        m_camera->getCamera().setRoll(m_currentRoll);

        m_bobTime = expInterpTo(m_bobTime, 0.0f, deltaTime, m_bobResetSpeed);
        m_lastSinValue = 0.0f;
    }

    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    CameraComponent* m_camera = nullptr;
    CharacterComponent* m_character = nullptr;

    glm::vec3 m_baseEyeOffset;

    float m_bobTime = 0.0f;
    float m_lastSinValue = 0.0f;
    bool m_stepSide = false;

    float m_currentRoll = 0.0f;
    float m_targetRoll = 0.0f;

    float m_moveThreshold = 0.1f;
    float m_bobFrequency = 10.0f;
    float m_bobAmplitudeV = 0.04f;
    float m_bobAmplitudeH = 0.02f;
    float m_stepRollAngle = 2.5f;
    float m_rollInterpSpeed = 6.0f;
    float m_rollResetSpeed = 15.0f;
    float m_bobResetSpeed = 10.0f;
};

// ================================================================
// FILE: game/include/Components/PlayerComponent.h
// ================================================================

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Components/InputComponent.h"
#include "Components/CharacterComponent.h"
#include "HeadBobComponent.h"
#include "Components/CameraComponent.h"
#include "InteractableComponent.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

class PlayerComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    explicit PlayerComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics) : m_physics(physics) {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;

        m_input = owner.getComponent<InputComponent>();
        m_camera = owner.getComponent<CameraComponent>();
        m_character = owner.getComponent<CharacterComponent>();
        m_headBob = owner.getComponent<HeadBobComponent>();
    }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (!m_input) return;
    
        if (m_input->isFireJustPressed())
        {
            glm::vec3 origin = m_camera->getCamera().getPosition();
            glm::vec3 dir = m_camera->getCamera().getFront();
        
            RaycastHit hit = m_physics.raycast(origin, dir, 3.0f);
            if (hit.hit)
            {
                GameObject* target = m_physics.getOwner(hit.bodyId);
                if (target)
                {
                    LOG("Looked at object: " << target->name << ", distance=" << hit.distance);
                }
                else
                {
                    LOG("Hit unnamed body at distance=" << hit.distance);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                LOG("Raycast: nothing in range");
            }
        }

        if (m_character)
            m_character->syncTransform(deltaTime);

        glm::vec2 look = m_input->getLookDelta();
        m_owner->transform.rotation.y += look.x * m_lookSensitivity;

        m_pitch = glm::clamp(m_pitch + look.y * m_lookSensitivity, -89.0f, 89.0f);
        if (m_camera)
            m_camera->setPitch(m_pitch);

        if (m_character)
        {
            glm::vec2 moveAxis = m_input->getMoveAxis();
            float yawRad = glm::radians(m_owner->transform.rotation.y);

            glm::vec3 forward(sin(yawRad), 0.0f, -cos(yawRad));
            glm::vec3 right(cos(yawRad), 0.0f, sin(yawRad));

            glm::vec3 moveDir = forward * moveAxis.y + right * moveAxis.x;
            if (glm::length(moveDir) > 0.0001f)
                moveDir = glm::normalize(moveDir);

            m_character->setMoveInput(moveDir * m_moveSpeed);

            if (m_input->isJumpJustPressed())
                m_character->jump(m_jumpSpeed);
        }

        if (m_input->isInteractJustPressed())
        {
            LOG("Interact pressed!");
        
            glm::vec3 origin = m_camera->getCamera().getPosition();
            glm::vec3 dir = m_camera->getCamera().getFront();
            RaycastHit hit = m_physics.raycast(origin, dir, m_interactRange);
        
            LOG("Raycast hit: " << hit.hit << ", distance: " << hit.distance);
        
            if (hit.hit)
            {
                GameObject* target = m_physics.getOwner(hit.bodyId);
                LOG("target found: " << (target != nullptr) << ", name: '" << (target ? target->name : "null") << "'");
            
                if (target)
                {
                    auto* interactable = target->getComponent<InteractableComponent>();
                    LOG("interactable found: " << (interactable != nullptr));
                
                    if (interactable && interactable->onInteract)
                        interactable->onInteract();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                LOG("Interact: nothing in range");
            }
        }

        if (m_headBob)
            m_headBob->applyBob(deltaTime);

        if (m_camera)
            m_camera->applyToCamera();
    }

    void setLookSensitivity(float s) { m_lookSensitivity = s; }
    void setMoveSpeed(float s) { m_moveSpeed = s; }
    void setJumpSpeed(float s) { m_jumpSpeed = s; }

private:
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    InputComponent* m_input = nullptr;
    CameraComponent* m_camera = nullptr;
    CharacterComponent* m_character = nullptr;
    HeadBobComponent* m_headBob = nullptr;
    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;

    float m_interactRange = 3.0f;

    float m_pitch = 0.0f;
    float m_lookSensitivity = 0.1f;
    float m_moveSpeed = 2.5f;
    float m_jumpSpeed = 5.0f;
};

#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"

class DebugConsoleController : public IComponent
{
public:
    DebugConsoleController(Input& input, DebugConsoleWidget& console)
        : m_input(input), m_console(console)
    {}

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (m_input.isActionJustPressed("ToggleConsole"))
            m_console.setVisible(!m_console.isVisible());
    }

private:
    Input& m_input;
    DebugConsoleWidget& m_console;
};


// ================================================================
// FILE: game/src/AI/EnemyBehaviorCommand.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "AI/EnemyBehaviorCommand.h"

bool EnemyBehaviorCommand::execute(AIController& ai, float deltaTime)
{
    if (ai.canSeeTarget())
    {
        m_loseVisionTimer = m_loseVisionGracePeriod;
    }
    else if (m_loseVisionTimer > 0.0f)
    {
        m_loseVisionTimer -= deltaTime;
    }

    bool effectivelyChasing = m_loseVisionTimer > 0.0f;

    if (effectivelyChasing)
    {
        m_patrolState.reset();

        if (!m_chaseMove || m_repathTimer <= 0.0f)
        {
            m_chaseMove = std::make_unique<MoveToCommand>(m_navMesh, ai.getLastKnowTargetPosition(), m_chaseSpeed);
            m_repathTimer = m_repathInterval;
        }
        m_repathTimer -= deltaTime;
        m_chaseMove->execute(ai, deltaTime);
    }
    else
    {
        m_chaseMove.reset();
        if (!m_patrolState)
            m_patrolState = std::make_unique<PatrolCommand>(m_navMesh, m_patrolPoints, m_ptarolSpeed);
        m_patrolState->execute(ai, deltaTime);
    }
    return false;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: game/src/AI/MoveToCommand.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "AI/MoveToCommand.h"

bool MoveToCommand::execute(AIController& ai, float deltaTime)
{
    if (!m_pathComputed)
    {
        m_path = m_navMesh.findPath(ai.getOwnerPosition(), m_targetPos);
        m_pathComputed = true;
        m_currentWaypoint = 0;

        if (m_path.empty())
            return true;
    }

    if (m_currentWaypoint >= m_path.size())
        return true;
    
    glm::vec3 currentPos = ai.getOwnerPosition();
    glm::vec3 waypoint = m_path[m_currentWaypoint];
    glm::vec3 toWaypoint = waypoint - currentPos;
    toWaypoint.y = 0.0f;

    float distance = glm::length(toWaypoint);

    if (distance < m_arriveThreshold)
    {
        m_currentWaypoint++;
        return m_currentWaypoint >= m_path.size();
    }

    glm::vec3 direction = toWaypoint / distance;
    ai.moveTowards(direction, m_speed);
    ai.faceDirection(direction);

    return false;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: game/src/AI/PatrolCommand.cpp
// ================================================================

#include "AI/PatrolCommand.h"

bool PatrolCommand::execute(AIController& ai, float deltaTime)
{
    if (m_waypoints.empty()) return true;

    if (ai.canSeeTarget())
        return true;

    if (!m_currentMove)
    {
        m_currentMove = std::make_unique<MoveToCommand>(m_navMesh, m_waypoints[m_currentIndex], m_speed);
    }

    bool finished = m_currentMove->execute(ai, deltaTime);

    if (finished)
    {
        m_currentMove.reset();
        m_currentIndex = (m_currentIndex + 1) % m_waypoints.size();
    }

    return false;
}

void InputInScene(Engine& engine)
{
    engine.getInput().bindAction("ToggleConsole", InputBinding::fromKey(Key::GraveAccent));

    engine.getInput().bindAction("Up", InputBinding::fromKey(Key::Space));
    engine.getInput().bindAction("Up", InputBinding::fromGamepad(GamepadButton::RightBumper));

    engine.getInput().bindAction("Down", InputBinding::fromKey(Key::Q));
    engine.getInput().bindAction("Down", InputBinding::fromGamepad(GamepadButton::LeftBumper));

    engine.getInput().bindAction("Fire", InputBinding::fromMouse(MouseButton::Left));
    engine.getInput().bindAction("Fire", InputBinding::fromGamepad(GamepadButton::B));
    
    engine.getInput().bindAction("Interact", InputBinding::fromKey(Key::E));
}

GameObject* createDoor(Engine& engine, glm::vec3 pos, glm::vec3 rot, std::function<void()> onInteractExtra = nullptr)
{
    static bool soundLoaded = false;
    if (!soundLoaded)
    {
        engine.getAudio().loadSound("door_creak", "audio/door_creak.wav");
        soundLoaded = true;
    }

    auto& door = engine.getScene().addObject();
    door.name = "Door";
    door.transform.position = pos;
    door.transform.rotation = rot;
    door.transform.scale = glm::vec3(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);


    auto material = engine.loadMaterial(
        "assets/textures/door/Door_1_BaseColor.png",
        "assets/textures/door/Door_1_Normal.png",
        "assets/textures/door/Door_1_Metallic.png",
        "assets/textures/door/Door_1_Roughness.png"
    );

    auto doorMesh = engine.loadMesh("assets/models/Door.glb");

    door.addComponent<TransformComponent>();
    door.setMaterial(material);
    door.addComponent<MeshComponent>().setMesh(doorMesh);

    door.addComponent<RigidBodyComponent>(engine.getPhysics(),
        RigidBodyComponent::Shape::ConvexHull, RigidBodyComponent::Motion::Kinematic, engine.loadMesh("assets/models/Door.glb", true),
        glm::vec3(1.0f), 0.0f);

    auto& doorComp = door.addComponent<DoorComponent>();
    doorComp.setOpenAngle(100.0f);
    doorComp.setOpenSpeed(3.0f);

    auto& audioComp = door.addComponent<AudioComponent>(engine.getAudio(), engine.getPhysics(), "door_creak");

    auto& interact = door.addComponent<InteractableComponent>();
    interact.onInteract = [&doorComp, &audioComp, onInteractExtra]()
    {
        doorComp.toggle();
        audioComp.play(0.6f);
        if (onInteractExtra) onInteractExtra();
    };

    return &door;
}

int main()
{
    Engine engine;
    engine.initialize(800, 600, "ELE Game");
    engine.setAntialiasing(AAMethod::TAA);
    engine.setExposure(1.0f);
    InputInScene(engine);

    engine.getRenderer().setVoxelGIEnabled(false);

    IBLSettings iblSettings{};
    iblSettings.diffuseIntensity = 0.25f;
    engine.setIBLSettings(iblSettings);

    auto& level = engine.loadLevel("Levels/Level.elvl");
    engine.getScene().flush();

    engine.getAudio().loadSound("music", "audio/yebimenya.wav");
    engine.getAudio().playSound("music", 0.5f);

    auto cube = engine.loadModel("assets/models/TestModel2.emodel");
    LOG("loadModel: mesh=" << (cube.mesh ? "valid" : "NULL") << " materials count=" << cube.materials.size());
    
    auto& cubeobj = engine.getScene().addObject();
    cubeobj.mesh = cube.mesh;
    for (const auto& sm : cubeobj.mesh->getSubmeshes())
    {
        LOG("submesh materialIndex=" << sm.materialIndex);
        if (sm.materialIndex >= 0 && sm.materialIndex < (int)cube.materials.size())
            cubeobj.setMaterial(cube.materials[sm.materialIndex]);
    }
    cubeobj.addComponent<TransformComponent>().setPosition(glm::vec3(2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f));

    auto& console = engine.getUI().addWidget<DebugConsoleWidget>();
    console.setPosition(glm::vec2(10, 10));
    console.setSize(glm::vec2(600, 400));
    console.setVisible(false);

    auto& player = engine.getScene().addObject();

    player.addComponent<DebugConsoleController>(engine.getInput(), console);

    for (GameObject* spawnPoint : engine.getScene().findAllByType("PlayerSpawn"))
    {
        player.transform.position = spawnPoint->transform.position;
        LOG("player spawned at: " << player.transform.position.x << "," 
            << player.transform.position.y << "," << player.transform.position.z);
    }

    auto skinnedModel = engine.loadModel("assets/models/TestMesh.emodel");
    LOG("loadModel SKINNED: hasSkeleton=" << skinnedModel.hasSkeleton
        << " skeletalMesh=" << (skinnedModel.skeletalMesh ? "valid" : "NULL")
        << " materials count=" << skinnedModel.materials.size());

    auto& skinnedObj = engine.getScene().addObject();
    skinnedObj.addComponent<TransformComponent>().setPosition(glm::vec3(0.0f, -0.5f, 0.0f));

    auto& skelComp = skinnedObj.addComponent<SkeletalMeshComponent>(engine.getRenderer().getDevice(), engine.getRenderer().getFramesInFlight());
    skelComp.setSkeletalMesh(skinnedModel.skeletalMesh);

    if (skinnedModel.skeletalMesh)
    {
        for (const auto& sm : skinnedModel.skeletalMesh->getSubmeshes())
        {
            LOG("skinned submesh materialIndex=" << sm.materialIndex);
            if (sm.materialIndex >= 0 && sm.materialIndex < (int)skinnedModel.materials.size())
            {
                auto mat = skinnedModel.materials[sm.materialIndex];
                skinnedObj.setMaterial(mat);
                engine.createSkinnedMaterialDescriptorSet(mat, skelComp);
            }
        }

        skelComp.createShadowDescriptorSets(engine.getRenderer().getDevice(),
            engine.getRenderer().getSkinnedShadowDescriptorSetLayout(),
            engine.getRenderer().getFramesInFlight());
    }

    auto animData = std::make_shared<Animation>(engine.loadAnimation("assets/Animations/Animation.eanim"));
    LOG("Animation loaded: name=" << animData->name << " duration=" << animData->duration
        << " channels=" << animData->channels.size());

    for (const auto& ch : animData->channels)
       LOG("  animation channel joint: '" << ch.jointName << "'");

    if (skinnedModel.skeletalMesh && skinnedModel.skeletalMesh->getSkeleton())
    {
        auto skeleton = skinnedModel.skeletalMesh->getSkeleton();
        for (const auto& joint : skeleton->getJoints())
            LOG("  skeleton joint: '" << joint.name << "'");
    }
    skelComp.play(animData, true);

    player.addComponent<TransformComponent>();
    player.addComponent<InputComponent>(engine.getInput());
    player.addComponent<CharacterComponent>(engine.getPhysics(), engine.getTime(), 0.3f, 1.4f, 60.0f);
    player.addComponent<CameraComponent>(engine.getCamera(), CameraComponent::Mode::FirstPerson);
    player.addComponent<HeadBobComponent>(glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.6f, 0.0f));
    player.addComponent<PlayerComponent>(engine.getPhysics());

    auto& testText = engine.getUI().addWidget<TextWidget>("Penis");
    testText.setFontScale(10.0f);
    //createInteractableCube(engine, glm::vec3(2.0f, 1.0f, 2.0f), [&engine]()
    //{
    //    //createCube(engine, glm::vec3(0.0f, 10.0f, 0.0f));
    //});
    engine.setCameraControlMode(false);

    //////////////////////////////
    
    engine.run();

    cube.mesh.reset();
    cube.materials.clear();
    skinnedModel.skeletalMesh.reset();
    skinnedModel.materials.clear();

    engine.shutdown();
    return 0;
}

// ================================================================
// FILE: tools/emodel_optimizer/CMakeLists.txt
// ================================================================

add_executable(emodel_optimizer main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(emodel_optimizer PRIVATE meshoptimizer)

// ================================================================
// FILE: tools/emodel_optimizer/main.cpp
// ================================================================

#include <meshoptimizer.h>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>
#include <cstring>

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <io.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#endif

int main()
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    _setmode(_fileno(stdin), _O_BINARY);
    _setmode(_fileno(stdout), _O_BINARY);
#endif

    uint32_t vertexCount, vertexStride, indexCount;
    fread(&vertexCount, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdin);
    fread(&vertexStride, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdin);
    fread(&indexCount, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdin);

    std::vector<uint8_t> vertices(vertexCount * vertexStride);
    fread(vertices.data(), 1, vertices.size(), stdin);

    std::vector<uint32_t> indices(indexCount);
    fread(indices.data(), sizeof(uint32_t), indexCount, stdin);

    std::vector<uint32_t> remap(vertexCount);
    size_t uniqueVertexCount = meshopt_generateVertexRemap(
        remap.data(), indices.data(), indexCount,
        vertices.data(), vertexCount, vertexStride);

    std::vector<uint8_t> remappedVertices(uniqueVertexCount * vertexStride);
    std::vector<uint32_t> remappedIndices(indexCount);

    meshopt_remapVertexBuffer(remappedVertices.data(), vertices.data(),
        vertexCount, vertexStride, remap.data());
    meshopt_remapIndexBuffer(remappedIndices.data(), indices.data(),
        indexCount, remap.data());

    meshopt_optimizeVertexCache(remappedIndices.data(), remappedIndices.data(),
        indexCount, uniqueVertexCount);

    meshopt_optimizeOverdraw(remappedIndices.data(), remappedIndices.data(),
        indexCount, (const float*)remappedVertices.data(),
        uniqueVertexCount, vertexStride, 1.05f);

    std::vector<uint8_t> finalVertices(uniqueVertexCount * vertexStride);
    meshopt_optimizeVertexFetch(finalVertices.data(), remappedIndices.data(),
        indexCount, remappedVertices.data(), uniqueVertexCount, vertexStride);

    std::vector<uint8_t> compressedVertices(meshopt_encodeVertexBufferBound(uniqueVertexCount, vertexStride));
    size_t compressedVertexSize = meshopt_encodeVertexBuffer(
        compressedVertices.data(), compressedVertices.size(),
        finalVertices.data(), uniqueVertexCount, vertexStride);

    std::vector<uint8_t> compressedIndices(meshopt_encodeIndexBufferBound(indexCount, uniqueVertexCount));
    size_t compressedIndexSize = meshopt_encodeIndexBuffer(
        compressedIndices.data(), compressedIndices.size(),
        remappedIndices.data(), indexCount);

    uint32_t outVertexCount = (uint32_t)uniqueVertexCount;
    uint32_t outCompVertexSize = (uint32_t)compressedVertexSize;
    uint32_t outIndexCount = (uint32_t)indexCount;
    uint32_t outCompIndexSize = (uint32_t)compressedIndexSize;

    fwrite(&outVertexCount, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdout);
    fwrite(&outCompVertexSize, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdout);
    fwrite(&outIndexCount, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdout);
    fwrite(&outCompIndexSize, sizeof(uint32_t), 1, stdout);
    fwrite(compressedVertices.data(), 1, compressedVertexSize, stdout);
    fwrite(compressedIndices.data(), 1, compressedIndexSize, stdout);

    return 0;
}
